      displaySettingsSaved: null,
      displaySettingsSaveChain: Promise.resolve()
    };

    var app = document.getElementById("app");
    var notice = document.getElementById("notice");
    var list = document.getElementById("leaderboard-list");
    var statsList = document.getElementById("stats-list");
    var preview = document.getElementById("preview-content");
    var previewHome = document.getElementById("preview-panel").parentNode;
    var inlineDetailHost = null;
    var authPanel = document.getElementById("auth-panel");
    var submitModal = document.getElementById("submit-modal");
    var editSubmissionModal = document.getElementById("edit-submission-modal");
    var guessModal = document.getElementById("guess-modal");
    var editModal = document.getElementById("edit-modal");
    var confirmModal = document.getElementById("confirm-modal");
    var rejectRunModal = document.getElementById("reject-run-modal");
    var authMessage = document.getElementById("auth-message");
    var editMessage = document.getElementById("edit-message");

    function byId(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
    function text(value) { return value == null ? "" : String(value); }
    function lower(value) { return text(value).trim().toLowerCase(); }
    function openRememberedSessionDatabase() {
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        try {
          if (!window.indexedDB) throw new Error("Persistent browser storage is unavailable.");
          var request = window.indexedDB.open(REMEMBERED_SESSION_DB, 1);
          request.onupgradeneeded = function () {
            var database = request.result;
            if (!database.objectStoreNames.contains(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE)) database.createObjectStore(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE);
          };
          request.onsuccess = function () { resolve(request.result); };
          request.onerror = function () { reject(request.error || new Error("Could not open persistent browser storage.")); };
          request.onblocked = function () { reject(new Error("Persistent browser storage is blocked.")); };
        } catch (error) { reject(error); }
      });
    }
    async function readIndexedDbRememberedToken() {
      var database = await openRememberedSessionDatabase();
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        try {
          var transaction = database.transaction(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE, "readonly");
          var request = transaction.objectStore(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE).get(REMEMBERED_SESSION_RECORD);
          request.onsuccess = function () {
            var value = request.result;
            database.close();
            resolve(typeof value === "string" ? value : "");
          };
          request.onerror = function () {
            database.close();
            reject(request.error || new Error("Could not read the saved sign-in."));
          };
        } catch (error) {
          database.close();
          reject(error);
        }
      });
    }
    async function writeIndexedDbRememberedToken(token) {
      var database = await openRememberedSessionDatabase();
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        try {
          var transaction = database.transaction(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE, "readwrite");
          transaction.objectStore(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE).put(token, REMEMBERED_SESSION_RECORD);
          transaction.oncomplete = function () { database.close(); resolve(); };
          transaction.onerror = function () { database.close(); reject(transaction.error || new Error("Could not save the sign-in.")); };
          transaction.onabort = function () { database.close(); reject(transaction.error || new Error("Could not save the sign-in.")); };
        } catch (error) {
          database.close();
          reject(error);
        }
      });
    }
    async function removeIndexedDbRememberedToken() {
      var database = await openRememberedSessionDatabase();
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        try {
          var transaction = database.transaction(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE, "readwrite");
          transaction.objectStore(REMEMBERED_SESSION_STORE).delete(REMEMBERED_SESSION_RECORD);
          transaction.oncomplete = function () { database.close(); resolve(); };
          transaction.onerror = function () { database.close(); reject(transaction.error || new Error("Could not clear the saved sign-in.")); };
          transaction.onabort = function () { database.close(); reject(transaction.error || new Error("Could not clear the saved sign-in.")); };
        } catch (error) {
          database.close();
          reject(error);
        }
      });
    }
    async function getRememberedTokens() {
      var tokens = [];
      var damaged = false;
      var databaseToken = "";
      try { databaseToken = await readIndexedDbRememberedToken(); } catch (_) {}
      if (/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(databaseToken)) tokens.push(databaseToken);
      else if (databaseToken) damaged = true;
      var localToken = "";
      try { localToken = window.localStorage.getItem(REMEMBERED_SESSION_KEY) || ""; } catch (_) {}
      if (/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(localToken) && tokens.indexOf(localToken) < 0) tokens.push(localToken);
      else if (localToken && !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(localToken)) damaged = true;
      if (!tokens.length && damaged) state.rememberedLoginIssue = "The saved sign-in on this device was damaged. Please sign in again and select Keep me signed in.";
      return tokens;
    }
    async function getRememberedToken() {
      var tokens = await getRememberedTokens();
      return tokens[0] || "";
    }
    async function saveRememberedToken(token) {
      var saved = false;
      var storageErrors = [];
      try {
        await writeIndexedDbRememberedToken(token);
        if (await readIndexedDbRememberedToken() !== token) throw new Error("IndexedDB did not retain the session token.");
        saved = true;
      } catch (error) { storageErrors.push(error); }
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(REMEMBERED_SESSION_KEY, token);
        if (window.localStorage.getItem(REMEMBERED_SESSION_KEY) !== token) throw new Error("Local storage did not retain the session token.");
        saved = true;
      } catch (error) { storageErrors.push(error); }
      if (!saved) throw new Error("This browser blocked both persistent storage options. " + text(storageErrors[0] && storageErrors[0].message || ""));
    }
    async function clearRememberedToken() {
      try { window.localStorage.removeItem(REMEMBERED_SESSION_KEY); }
      catch (_) {}
      try { await removeIndexedDbRememberedToken(); }
      catch (_) {}
    }
    function unwrapRpcData(response) {
      return Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data[0] : response.data;
    }
    function modeIdEquals(a, b) { return text(a) === text(b); }
    function modeChallengeType(mode) {
      var type = lower(mode && mode.challenge_type);
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(MODE_CHALLENGE_TYPES, type) ? type : "standard";
    }
    function modeChallengeLabel(modeOrType) {
      var type = typeof modeOrType === "string" ? modeOrType : modeChallengeType(modeOrType);
      return MODE_CHALLENGE_TYPES[type] || MODE_CHALLENGE_TYPES.standard;
    }
    function modePlayableLabel(mode) {
      var playableOn = lower(mode && (mode.playable_on || mode.playableOn));
      if (playableOn === "mobile") return "Mobile only";
      if (playableOn === "pc") return "PC only";
      return "Mobile and PC";
    }
    function rankedModes() {
      return state.modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return Number(a.placement) - Number(b.placement) || text(a.id).localeCompare(text(b.id));
      });
    }
    function leaderboardConfig(listKey) {
      return LEADERBOARDS.find(function (item) { return item.key === (listKey || state.activeLeaderboard); }) || LEADERBOARDS[0];
    }
    function modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, listKey) {
      return Boolean(mode && leaderboardConfig(listKey).types.indexOf(modeChallengeType(mode)) >= 0);
    }
    function rankedModesForLeaderboard(listKey) {
      return state.modes.filter(function (mode) { return modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, listKey); }).slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return Number(a.placement) - Number(b.placement) || text(a.id).localeCompare(text(b.id));
      });
    }
    function leaderboardRank(mode, listKey) {
      if (!mode || !modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, listKey)) return null;
      var index = rankedModesForLeaderboard(listKey).findIndex(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, mode.id); });
      return index < 0 ? null : index + 1;
    }
    function modeRank(mode) {
      if (!mode) return null;
      var index = rankedModes().findIndex(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, mode.id); });
      return index < 0 ? null : index + 1;
    }
    function primaryLeaderboardForMode(mode) {
      return LEADERBOARDS.find(function (board) { return modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, board.key); }) || LEADERBOARDS[0];
    }
    function modePositionLabel(mode) {
      var board = primaryLeaderboardForMode(mode);
      var rank = leaderboardRank(mode, board.key);
      return board.label + (rank ? " #" + rank : "");
    }
    function modePoints(mode, listKey) {
      var board = listKey || state.activeLeaderboard;
      if (!modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, board)) return 0;
      var count = rankedModesForLeaderboard(board).length;
      var rank = leaderboardRank(mode, board);
      return rankPoints(rank, count);
    }
    function nameBadge(key) {
      return NAME_BADGES.find(function (badge) { return badge.key === text(key); }) || null;
    }
    function customNameGradient(key) {
      var match = /^custom:([0-9a-f-]{36})$/i.exec(text(key));
      if (!match) return null;
      return state.customBadgesById[match[1]] || state.customBadgesById[match[1].toLowerCase()] || null;
    }
    function normalizeHexColor(value) {
      var color = text(value).trim();
      return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(color) ? color.toLowerCase() : "";
    }
    function normalizeSavedColor(value, legacyColors, fallback) {
      var hex = normalizeHexColor(value);
      if (hex) return hex;
      var key = lower(value);
      return legacyColors[key] || fallback;
    }
    function readableTextColor(hex) {
      var color = normalizeHexColor(hex) || "#1b1b1b";
      var red = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
      var green = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
      var blue = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
      function linear(channel) { return channel <= 0.04045 ? channel / 12.92 : Math.pow((channel + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4); }
      var luminance = 0.2126 * linear(red) + 0.7152 * linear(green) + 0.0722 * linear(blue);
      return luminance > 0.48 ? "#151515" : "#f5f5f5";
    }
    function normalizedSiteTheme(value) {
      var theme = text(value).trim().toLowerCase();
      return SITE_THEMES.indexOf(theme) >= 0 || normalizeHexColor(theme) ? theme : "";
    }
    function applySiteTheme(theme, previewOnly) {
      var selected = normalizedSiteTheme(theme) || "graphite";
      var isPreset = SITE_THEMES.indexOf(selected) >= 0;
      var color = isPreset ? SITE_THEME_COLORS[selected] : selected;
      document.body.dataset.siteTheme = isPreset ? selected : "custom";
      if (isPreset) {
        document.body.style.removeProperty("--custom-theme-color");
        document.body.style.removeProperty("--custom-accent-text");
      } else {
        document.body.style.setProperty("--custom-theme-color", color);
        document.body.style.setProperty("--custom-accent-text", readableTextColor(color));
      }
      var selector = byId("site-background-theme");
      if (selector) selector.value = color;
      var picker = byId("site-background-picker");
      if (picker) picker.value = color;
      if (!previewOnly) state.siteTheme = selected;
    }
    function textSizeScale(size) {
      return size === "small" ? 0.9 : size === "large" ? 1.15 : 1;
    }
    function accountTextElements(roots) {
      var found = new Set();
      roots.forEach(function (root) {
        if (!root || root.nodeType !== 1) return;
        found.add(root);
        if (root.querySelectorAll) root.querySelectorAll("*").forEach(function (node) { found.add(node); });
      });
      return Array.from(found).filter(function (node) {
        return node !== document.documentElement && !node.matches("script, style");
      });
    }
    function setAccountTextBases(roots, refreshExisting) {
      var root = document.documentElement;
      var currentScale = textSizeScale(state.textSize || "standard");
      root.style.setProperty("--account-text-scale", "1");
      var nodes = accountTextElements(roots);
      if (refreshExisting) nodes.forEach(function (node) { node.classList.remove("account-text-scaled"); });
      nodes.forEach(function (node) {
        if (!refreshExisting && node.classList.contains("account-text-scaled")) return;
        var baseSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(node).fontSize);
        if (!Number.isFinite(baseSize) || baseSize <= 0) baseSize = 16;
        node.style.setProperty("--account-base-font-size", baseSize + "px");
        node.classList.add("account-text-scaled");
      });
      root.style.setProperty("--account-text-scale", String(currentScale));
    }
    function applyAccountTextSize(size) {
      var selected = size === "small" || size === "large" ? size : "standard";
      state.textSize = selected;
      document.body.dataset.textSize = selected;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--account-text-scale", String(textSizeScale(selected)));
      var selector = byId("account-text-size");
      if (selector) selector.value = selected;
    }
    function initializeAccountTextSize() {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--account-text-scale", "1");
      setAccountTextBases([document.body], true);
      if (typeof MutationObserver === "function") {
        var observer = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
          var added = [];
          records.forEach(function (record) {
            record.addedNodes.forEach(function (node) { if (node.nodeType === 1) added.push(node); });
          });
          if (added.length) setAccountTextBases(added, false);
        });
        observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      }
      var resizeTimer = 0;
      window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
        resizeTimer = window.setTimeout(function () { setAccountTextBases([document.body], true); }, 140);
      });
    }
    function normalizeDisplaySettings(settings) {
      var raw = settings && typeof settings === "object" ? settings : {};
      var strength = Number(raw.thumbnail_background_strength);
      if (!Number.isFinite(strength)) strength = DISPLAY_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS.thumbnail_background_strength;
      return {
        mode_layout: raw.mode_layout === "two-up" ? "two-up" : "base",
        card_density: raw.card_density === "compact" ? "compact" : "comfortable",
        thumbnail_backgrounds: raw.thumbnail_backgrounds !== false,
        thumbnail_background_strength: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(strength))),
        highlight_beaten_modes: raw.highlight_beaten_modes !== false,
        reduced_motion: raw.reduced_motion === true,
        text_size: raw.text_size === "small" || raw.text_size === "large" ? raw.text_size : "standard"
      };
    }
    function readDisplaySettingsControls() {
      return normalizeDisplaySettings({
        mode_layout: byId("mode-list-layout").value,
        card_density: byId("mode-card-density").value,
        thumbnail_backgrounds: byId("thumbnail-backgrounds-setting").checked,
        thumbnail_background_strength: byId("thumbnail-background-strength").value,
        highlight_beaten_modes: byId("highlight-beaten-modes-setting").checked,
        reduced_motion: byId("reduced-motion-setting").checked,
        text_size: byId("account-text-size").value
      });
    }
    function setDisplaySettingsStatus(message) {
      ["mode-layout-status", "density-status", "thumbnail-background-status", "highlight-status", "motion-status", "text-size-status"]
        .forEach(function (id) { var status = byId(id); if (status) status.textContent = message; });
    }
    function applyDisplaySettings(settings, statusMessage) {
      var prefs = normalizeDisplaySettings(settings);
      state.modeLayout = prefs.mode_layout;
      state.cardDensity = prefs.card_density;
      state.thumbnailBackgrounds = prefs.thumbnail_backgrounds;
      state.thumbnailBackgroundStrength = prefs.thumbnail_background_strength;
      state.highlightBeatenModes = prefs.highlight_beaten_modes;
      state.reducedMotion = prefs.reduced_motion;
      state.textSize = prefs.text_size;
      document.body.dataset.modeLayout = prefs.mode_layout;
      document.body.dataset.cardDensity = prefs.card_density;
      document.body.dataset.thumbnailBackgrounds = prefs.thumbnail_backgrounds ? "on" : "off";
      document.body.dataset.highlightBeatenModes = prefs.highlight_beaten_modes ? "on" : "off";
      document.body.dataset.reducedMotion = prefs.reduced_motion ? "on" : "off";
      document.body.style.setProperty("--thumbnail-background-opacity", String(prefs.thumbnail_background_strength / 100));
      applyAccountTextSize(prefs.text_size);
      byId("mode-list-layout").value = prefs.mode_layout;
      byId("mode-card-density").value = prefs.card_density;
      byId("thumbnail-backgrounds-setting").checked = prefs.thumbnail_backgrounds;
      byId("thumbnail-background-strength").value = String(prefs.thumbnail_background_strength);
      byId("thumbnail-background-strength-value").textContent = prefs.thumbnail_background_strength + "%";
      byId("thumbnail-background-strength").disabled = !prefs.thumbnail_backgrounds;
      byId("highlight-beaten-modes-setting").checked = prefs.highlight_beaten_modes;
      byId("reduced-motion-setting").checked = prefs.reduced_motion;
      state.displaySettingsSaved = prefs;
      document.querySelectorAll("#leaderboard-list > .mode-card[data-mode-id]").forEach(function (card) {
        var mode = modeById(card.dataset.modeId);
        var beaten = Boolean(mode && state.user && prefs.highlight_beaten_modes && modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, state.user.username));
        card.classList.toggle("player-completed-highlight", beaten);
        var background = card.querySelector(".mode-card-background");
        if (background) background.classList.toggle("is-hidden", !prefs.thumbnail_backgrounds);
        var chips = card.querySelector(".chips");
        var beatenChip = chips && chips.querySelector(".beaten-chip");
        if (beaten && chips && !beatenChip) chips.appendChild(element("span", "chip beaten-chip", "Beaten"));
        else if (!beaten && beatenChip) beatenChip.remove();
      });
      if (statusMessage) setDisplaySettingsStatus(statusMessage);
    }
    function loadDisplaySettings() {
      try {
        ["granny-modes-mode-layout-v1", "granny-modes-card-density-v1", "granny-modes-thumbnail-backgrounds-v1", "granny-modes-reduced-motion-v1"]
          .forEach(function (key) { window.localStorage.removeItem(key); });
      } catch (_) {}
      applyDisplaySettings(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, "Sign in to load and save your account settings.");
    }
    async function loadAccountDisplaySettings() {
      var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_player_website_preferences", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_preferences: null
      });
      var result = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not load account settings");
      if (!result.preferences || typeof result.preferences !== "object") throw new Error("The account settings response was invalid.");
      applyDisplaySettings(result.preferences, "Saved to your account.");
    }
    function saveAccountDisplaySettings() {
      if (!state.user || !state.db || !state.accountPassword) {
        if (!state.user) requireLogin();
        return Promise.resolve(false);
      }
      applyDisplaySettings(readDisplaySettingsControls(), "Saving to your account…");
      state.displaySettingsSaveChain = state.displaySettingsSaveChain.catch(function () {}).then(async function () {
        var preferences = readDisplaySettingsControls();
        try {
          var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_player_website_preferences", {
            p_username: state.user.username,
            p_password: state.accountPassword,
            p_preferences: preferences
          });
          var result = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not save account settings");
          if (!result.preferences || typeof result.preferences !== "object") throw new Error("Supabase returned invalid account settings.");
          var current = readDisplaySettingsControls();
          state.displaySettingsSaved = normalizeDisplaySettings(result.preferences);
          if (JSON.stringify(current) === JSON.stringify(preferences)) applyDisplaySettings(result.preferences, "Saved to your account.");
          return true;
        } catch (error) {
          setDisplaySettingsStatus("Could not save: " + text(error.message || error));
          setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error");
          return false;
        }
      });
      return state.displaySettingsSaveChain;
    }
    async function resetAllSettings() {
      var status = byId("settings-reset-status");
      if (status) status.textContent = "Restoring account defaults…";
      applyDisplaySettings(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, "Saving defaults to your account…");
      var preferencesSaved = await saveAccountDisplaySettings();
      var themeSaved = await saveAccountSiteTheme("graphite");
      if (status) status.textContent = preferencesSaved && themeSaved
        ? "All settings reset and saved to your account."
        : "Some settings could not be saved. Check the status messages above and retry.";
    }
    async function loadAccountSiteTheme() {
      var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_player_website_theme", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_theme: null
      });
      var result = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not load account appearance");
      if (!normalizedSiteTheme(result.theme)) throw new Error("The saved website theme is invalid. Run the current supabase-full-repair.sql setup again.");
      applySiteTheme(result.theme);
      var status = byId("site-theme-status");
      if (status) status.textContent = "This appearance is saved to your account.";
      await loadAccountDisplaySettings();
    }
    async function saveAccountSiteTheme(theme) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return false;
      var selected = normalizedSiteTheme(theme);
      if (!selected) {
        var invalidStatus = byId("site-theme-status");
        if (invalidStatus) invalidStatus.textContent = "Enter a valid hex color in #RRGGBB format.";
        return false;
      }
      var previous = state.siteTheme;
      var selector = byId("site-background-theme");
      var picker = byId("site-background-picker");
      applySiteTheme(selected);
      var status = byId("site-theme-status");
      if (status) status.textContent = "Saving appearance to your account…";
      if (selector) selector.disabled = true;
      if (picker) picker.disabled = true;
      try {
        var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_player_website_theme", {
          p_username: state.user.username,
          p_password: state.accountPassword,
          p_theme: selected
        });
        var result = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not save account appearance");
        if (!normalizedSiteTheme(result.theme)) throw new Error("Supabase returned an invalid appearance value.");
        applySiteTheme(result.theme);
        if (status) status.textContent = "Appearance saved to your account.";
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        applySiteTheme(previous);
        if (status) status.textContent = text(error.message || error);
        setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error");
        return false;
      } finally {
        if (selector) selector.disabled = false;
        if (picker) picker.disabled = false;
      }
    }
    function setSiteSettingsOpen(open) {
      var panel = byId("site-settings-panel");
      var trigger = document.querySelector('[data-action="toggle-site-settings"]');
      if (!panel) return;
      panel.classList.toggle("hidden", !open);
      if (trigger) trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", open ? "true" : "false");
    }
    function setNavigationOpen(open) {
      var drawer = byId("site-navigation");
      var backdrop = document.querySelector(".nav-drawer-backdrop");
      var trigger = document.querySelector('[data-action="toggle-navigation"]');
      if (!drawer || !backdrop || !trigger) return;
      var restoreFocus = drawer.contains(document.activeElement) || document.activeElement === backdrop;
      drawer.classList.toggle("hidden", !open);
      backdrop.classList.toggle("hidden", !open);
      drawer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", open ? "false" : "true");
      if (open) drawer.removeAttribute("inert");
      else drawer.setAttribute("inert", "");
      trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", open ? "true" : "false");
      trigger.setAttribute("aria-label", open ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu");
      trigger.title = open ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu";
      document.body.classList.toggle("site-nav-open", open);
      if (open) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
          var firstItem = drawer.querySelector(".drawer-nav button:not(:disabled), .drawer-nav a[href]") || drawer.querySelector("button:not(:disabled), a[href]");
          if (!drawer.classList.contains("hidden") && firstItem) firstItem.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        });
      } else if (restoreFocus) {
        trigger.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    }
    function snapshotPlayerNameGradient(node) {
      if (!node || !node.classList.contains("player-name-gradient")) return null;
      var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
      return {
        backgroundImage: style.backgroundImage,
        backgroundSize: style.backgroundSize,
        backgroundClip: style.backgroundClip,
        webkitBackgroundClip: style.webkitBackgroundClip,
        color: style.color,
        webkitTextFillColor: style.webkitTextFillColor
      };
    }
    function applyPlayerNameGradientSnapshot(node, snapshot) {
      if (!node || !snapshot || !snapshot.backgroundImage || snapshot.backgroundImage === "none") return;
      node.style.display = "inline-block";
      node.style.setProperty("background-image", snapshot.backgroundImage, "important");
      node.style.setProperty("background-size", snapshot.backgroundSize);
      node.style.setProperty("background-clip", "text", "important");
      node.style.setProperty("-webkit-background-clip", snapshot.webkitBackgroundClip || "text", "important");
      node.style.setProperty("color", "transparent", "important");
      node.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color", "transparent", "important");
    }
    function defaultProfileCustomization() {
      var sections = Object.create(null);
      PROFILE_SECTIONS.forEach(function (section) { sections[section.key] = true; });
      return { bio: "", accent_theme: "#c9c9c9", background_theme: "#1b1b1b", visible_sections: sections, featured_mode_id: null, country_code: null, show_country_flag: true };
    }
    function playerProfileCustomization(username) {
      var defaults = defaultProfileCustomization();
      var saved = state.profileCustomizations[lower(username)] || {};
      return {
        bio: text(saved.bio),
        accent_theme: normalizeSavedColor(saved.accent_theme, PROFILE_THEME_COLORS, defaults.accent_theme),
        background_theme: normalizeSavedColor(saved.background_theme, PROFILE_BACKGROUND_COLORS, defaults.background_theme),
        visible_sections: defaults.visible_sections,
        featured_mode_id: saved.featured_mode_id == null ? null : saved.featured_mode_id,
        country_code: normalizeCountryCode(saved.country_code) || null,
        show_country_flag: saved.show_country_flag !== false
      };
    }
    function createCustomColorField(labelText, textId, pickerId, color) {
      var label = element("label", "field", labelText);
      label.htmlFor = textId;
      var controls = element("div", "custom-color-control");
      var picker = document.createElement("input");
      picker.type = "color";
      picker.id = pickerId;
      picker.value = normalizeHexColor(color) || "#000000";
      picker.setAttribute("aria-label", "Pick " + labelText.toLowerCase());
      var hex = document.createElement("input");
      hex.type = "text";
      hex.id = textId;
      hex.value = normalizeHexColor(color) || "#000000";
      hex.maxLength = 7;
      hex.pattern = "#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}";
      hex.placeholder = "#RRGGBB";
      hex.autocomplete = "off";
      hex.spellcheck = false;
      hex.setAttribute("aria-label", labelText + " as a hex color");
      controls.appendChild(picker);
      controls.appendChild(hex);
      label.appendChild(controls);
      return label;
    }
    function playerBadge(username) {
      return state.accountBadges[lower(username)] || { equipped_name_badge: "none", platinum_unlocked: false };
    }
    function customGradientText(badge, label) {
      var sample = element("span", "player-name-gradient badge-custom custom-gradient-text", label || badge.name || "Name gradient");
      var start = normalizeHexColor(badge.gradient_start) || "#4f46e5";
      var middle = normalizeHexColor(badge.gradient_mid) || "#a855f7";
      var end = normalizeHexColor(badge.gradient_end) || "#ec4899";
      var glow = normalizeHexColor(badge.glow_color) || end;
      var rawAngle = Number(badge.gradient_angle);
      var angle = Number.isFinite(rawAngle) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(360, rawAngle)) : 110;
      sample.style.setProperty("background-image", "linear-gradient(" + angle + "deg, " + start + ", " + middle + ", " + end + ")", "important");
      sample.style.setProperty("background-size", "200% 100%");
      sample.style.setProperty("background-clip", "text", "important");
      sample.style.setProperty("-webkit-background-clip", "text", "important");
      sample.style.setProperty("color", "transparent", "important");
      sample.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color", "transparent", "important");
      sample.style.setProperty("filter", "drop-shadow(0 0 3px " + glow + ")");
      sample.title = text(badge.description || badge.name || "Name gradient");
      return sample;
    }
    function packBonusPointsForPlayer(username, listKey) {
      var field = (listKey || state.activeLeaderboard) + "_points";
      return state.packCompletions.reduce(function (sum, completion) {
        if (lower(completion.player_username) !== lower(username)) return sum;
        var points = Number(completion[field]);
        return sum + (Number.isFinite(points) ? points : 0);
      }, 0);
    }
    function badgeKeyFromPlayerButton(anchor) {
      var nameText = anchor && typeof anchor.querySelector === "function" ? anchor.querySelector(".player-name-text") : null;
      if (!nameText) return null;
      if (nameText.dataset.nameBadge && nameText.dataset.nameBadge !== "none") return nameText.dataset.nameBadge;
      for (var i = 0; i < NAME_BADGES.length; i++) {
        if (nameText.classList.contains(NAME_BADGES[i].className)) return NAME_BADGES[i].key;
      }
      return null;
    }
    function decoratePlayerName(node, username, badgeKey) {
      var key = badgeKey === undefined ? playerBadge(username).equipped_name_badge : text(badgeKey);
      var badge = nameBadge(key);
      var custom = customNameGradient(key);
      node.classList.remove("player-name-gradient");
      node.classList.remove("badge-custom");
      NAME_BADGES.forEach(function (item) { node.classList.remove(item.className); });
      ["background-image", "background-size", "background-clip", "-webkit-background-clip", "color", "-webkit-text-fill-color", "filter"].forEach(function (property) { node.style.removeProperty(property); });
      if (badge) {
        node.classList.add("player-name-gradient", badge.className);
        node.dataset.nameBadge = badge.key;
      } else if (custom) {
        var start = normalizeHexColor(custom.gradient_start) || "#4f46e5";
        var middle = normalizeHexColor(custom.gradient_mid) || "#a855f7";
        var end = normalizeHexColor(custom.gradient_end) || "#ec4899";
        var rawAngle = Number(custom.gradient_angle);
        var angle = Number.isFinite(rawAngle) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(360, rawAngle)) : 110;
        node.classList.add("player-name-gradient", "badge-custom");
        node.style.setProperty("background-image", "linear-gradient(" + angle + "deg, " + start + ", " + middle + ", " + end + ")", "important");
        node.style.setProperty("background-size", "200% 100%");
        node.style.setProperty("background-clip", "text", "important");
        node.style.setProperty("-webkit-background-clip", "text", "important");
        node.style.setProperty("color", "transparent", "important");
        node.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color", "transparent", "important");
        var glow = normalizeHexColor(custom.glow_color);
        if (glow) node.style.setProperty("filter", "drop-shadow(0 0 3px " + glow + ")");
        node.dataset.nameBadge = "custom:" + text(custom.badge_id);
      } else {
        node.dataset.nameBadge = "none";
      }
    }
    function updateCompletionFilterOptions() {
      var hasUser = Boolean(state.user);
      var hasClan = Boolean(currentClan());
      ["filter-player-beaten", "filter-player-not-beaten"].forEach(function (id) {
        var checkbox = byId(id);
        if (!checkbox) return;
        checkbox.disabled = !hasUser;
        if (!hasUser) checkbox.checked = false;
      });
      ["filter-clan-beaten", "filter-clan-not-beaten"].forEach(function (id) {
        var checkbox = byId(id);
        if (!checkbox) return;
        checkbox.disabled = !hasClan;
        if (!hasClan) checkbox.checked = false;
      });
      var hint = byId("mode-filter-hint");
      if (hint) {
        var accessNote = !hasUser
          ? "Sign in for player filters"
          : hasClan
            ? "Clan filters count completions by any current member"
            : "Join a clan to enable clan filters";
        hint.textContent = "Completion choices match within each group; player and clan groups combine. " + accessNote + ".";
      }
    }
    function parseNameBadgeResponse(response, label) {
      if (response.error) {
        var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
          ? "The Supabase badge function is missing or has old session-token arguments. Run supabase-full-repair.sql, then reload the site."
          : friendlyDatabaseError(response.error);
        throw new Error(label + ": " + detail);
      }
      var result = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data[0] : response.data;
      if (!result || !result.ok) {
        var messages = {
          invalid_session: "Your saved sign-in expired. Sign in again, then retry.",
          wrong_password: "The password could not be confirmed. Sign out and back in, then try again.",
          banned: "This account is blocked from changing name gradients.",
          badge_locked: "You have not unlocked that name gradient yet.",
          platinum_locked: "Complete every mode to unlock the platinum badge.",
          invalid_badge: "That name gradient is not available. Reopen your profile and try again."
        };
        throw new Error(label + ": " + (result && messages[result.code] ? messages[result.code] : "Badge data could not be saved."));
      }
      return result;
    }
    function parseSubmissionResponse(response, label) {
      if (response.error) {
        var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
          ? "The Supabase run-review function is missing or out of date. Run supabase-full-repair.sql in Supabase, then refresh the site."
          : friendlyDatabaseError(response.error);
        throw new Error(label + ": " + detail);
      }
      var result = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data[0] : response.data;
      var messages = {
        invalid_credentials: "Sign in again and retry.",
        invalid_session: "Your saved sign-in expired. Sign in again and retry.",
        wrong_password: "Sign in again and retry.",
        banned: "This account is blocked from submitting or reviewing runs.",
        invalid_request: "Check the mode and video URL, then retry.",
        mode_not_found: "That mode no longer exists.",
        duplicate_submission: "That submission is already recorded.",
        submission_not_found: "That submission could not be found.",
        submission_not_pending: "That submission has already been reviewed.",
        runner_banned: "This account is banned. Reject the queued run instead.",
        not_staff: "Only staff can review submissions.",
        tracker_missing: "Run supabase-submission-tracker.sql in Supabase first."
      };
      if (!result || !result.ok) throw new Error(label + ": " + (result && messages[result.code] ? messages[result.code] : "The submission request could not be saved."));
      return result;
    }
    function friendlyDatabaseError(error) {
      var message = text(error && error.message || error);
      var code = text(error && error.code);
      if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed|fetch failed|timeout|timed out/i.test(message)) {
        return "Supabase could not be reached. Check Supabase service status and your connection, then try again.";
      }
      if (code === "42501" || /row-level security|permission denied/i.test(message)) {
        return "Supabase denied this save. Run the updated supabase-full-repair.sql migration and retry.";
      }
      if (/schema cache|could not find the .* column|does not exist/i.test(message)) {
        return "The Supabase schema is missing an update. Run supabase-full-repair.sql, wait for it to finish, and reload the site.";
      }
      if (/502|503|504|gateway|temporarily unavailable/i.test(message)) {
        return "Supabase is temporarily unavailable. Wait for the service to recover, then retry.";
      }
      return message || "Supabase could not save this change.";
    }

        clan_not_found: "That clan could not be found.",
        clan_closed: "That clan is closed and is not accepting members.",
        tag_in_use: "That clan tag is already in use.",

        not_founder: "Only the clan founder can do that.",

        invalid_theme: "Enter a valid hex color in #RRGGBB format.",

        duplicate_username: "More than one account matches that username. Ask the site owner to resolve the duplicate accounts."
      };
      if (!result || !result.ok) throw new Error(label + ": " + (result && messages[result.code] ? messages[result.code] : "Supabase could not save this change."));

    }
    function parseProfileCustomizationResponse(response, label) {
      if (response.error) {
        var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
          ? "Run supabase-full-repair.sql in Supabase, then reload the site."
          : response.error.message;
        throw new Error(label + ": " + detail);
      }

      var messages = {
        invalid_credentials: "Sign in again and retry.",
        wrong_password: "Sign out and back in, then retry.",
        banned: "This account cannot change profile settings.",
        invalid_profile: "Check the bio length, theme, and country selection, then retry.",

      };

      return result;
    }
    function renderCompletionBadges(box, username, completions, badgeInfo, isOwnProfile) {
      if (!isOwnProfile) return;
      var count = completions.length;
      var totalModes = rankedModes().length;
      var platinumUnlocked = Boolean(badgeInfo && badgeInfo.platinum_unlocked);
      var earned = NAME_BADGES.filter(function (badge) {
        if (badge.key === "owner_red") return lower(username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME;
        return badge.key === "platinum" ? platinumUnlocked : count >= badge.threshold;
      });
      var customSeen = Object.create(null);
      var earnedCustom = [];
      state.packCompletions.forEach(function (award) {
        if (lower(award.player_username) !== lower(username) || !award.badge_id) return;
        var key = text(award.badge_id).toLowerCase();
        if (customSeen[key]) return;
        var custom = state.customBadgesById[text(award.badge_id)] || state.customBadgesById[key];
        if (!custom) return;
        customSeen[key] = true;
        earnedCustom.push(custom);
      });
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Name gradients"));
      box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", count + " of " + totalModes + " ranked modes completed. Earn completion gradients at 1, 5, 10, 25, and 50 completions, or unlock custom gradients by completing packs."));
      if (!earned.length && !earnedCustom.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Complete a mode or a pack to unlock your first name gradient."));
      else {
        var earnedList = element("div", "completion-badge-list name-gradient-previews");
        earned.forEach(function (badge) {
          var sample = element("span", "player-name-gradient name-gradient-preview", badge.label);
          decoratePlayerName(sample, username, badge.key);
          earnedList.appendChild(sample);
        });
        earnedCustom.forEach(function (custom) {
          var key = "custom:" + text(custom.badge_id);
          var sample = element("span", "player-name-gradient badge-custom name-gradient-preview", custom.name);
          decoratePlayerName(sample, username, key);
          earnedList.appendChild(sample);
        });
        box.appendChild(earnedList);
      }
      var label = element("label", "field", "Choose your player name gradient");
      var select = document.createElement("select");
      select.id = "player-badge-select";
      var none = document.createElement("option");
      none.value = "none";
      none.textContent = "No gradient";
      select.appendChild(none);
      earned.forEach(function (badge) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = badge.key;
        option.textContent = badge.label;
        select.appendChild(option);
      });
      earnedCustom.forEach(function (custom) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = "custom:" + text(custom.badge_id);
        option.textContent = custom.name;
        select.appendChild(option);
      });
      select.value = badgeInfo && badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge || "none";
      if (!Array.from(select.options).some(function (option) { return option.value === select.value; })) select.value = "none";
      select.disabled = !state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable;
      label.appendChild(select);
      box.appendChild(label);
      var saveButton = button("Equip gradient", "save-name-badge", { playerName: username }, "success");
      saveButton.disabled = !state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable;
      box.appendChild(saveButton);
      if (!state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Run the combined Supabase setup to save equipped name gradients."));
      else if (count >= totalModes && totalModes > 0 && !platinumUnlocked) {
        box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Your platinum badge unlock is being recorded. Reopen this profile in a moment if it does not appear."));
      }
    }
    function modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, username) {
      var name = lower(username);
      if (!name) return false;
      var names = [mode.verifier].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
      return names.some(function (candidate) { return lower(candidate) === name; });
    }
    function completionVideosFor(mode) {
      var videos = mode && mode.completion_videos;
      if (videos && typeof videos === "object" && !Array.isArray(videos)) return Object.assign({}, videos);
      if (typeof videos === "string") {
        try {
          videos = JSON.parse(videos);
          if (videos && typeof videos === "object" && !Array.isArray(videos)) return Object.assign({}, videos);
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      return {};
    }
    function completionVideoFor(mode, username) {
      var videos = completionVideosFor(mode);
      var key = lower(username);
      var savedKey = Object.keys(videos).find(function (candidate) { return lower(candidate) === key; });
      return savedKey ? safeUrl(videos[savedKey]) : "";
    }
    function removeCompletionVideo(videos, username) {
      var key = lower(username);
      Object.keys(videos).forEach(function (candidate) {
        if (lower(candidate) === key) delete videos[candidate];
      });
      return videos;
    }
    function modeBeatenByClan(mode, clan) {
      if (!clan) return false;
      var members = Object.create(null);
      (Array.isArray(clan.members) ? clan.members : []).forEach(function (member) {
        var name = lower(member);
        if (name) members[name] = true;
      });
      var names = [mode.verifier].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
      return names.some(function (candidate) {
        var name = lower(candidate);
        return Boolean(name && members[name]);
      });
    }
    function renderClanGoals(parent, clan) {
      var ranked = rankedModesForLeaderboard(state.activeLeaderboard);
      var goals = ranked.filter(function (mode) { return !modeBeatenByClan(mode, clan); });
      var section = element("section", "clan-goals");
      section.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Clan goals"));
      if (!ranked.length) {
        section.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "There are no ranked modes yet."));
      } else {
        section.appendChild(element("p", "muted", (ranked.length - goals.length) + " of " + ranked.length + " ranked modes beaten · " + goals.length + " remaining."));
        if (!goals.length) {
          section.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Your clan has beaten every ranked mode!"));
        } else {
          var details = document.createElement("details");
          var summary = document.createElement("summary");
          summary.textContent = "Show unbeaten modes (" + goals.length + ")";
          details.appendChild(summary);
          var listNode = element("ul", "detail-list");
          goals.forEach(function (mode) {
            var li = document.createElement("li");
            li.appendChild(button("#" + leaderboardRank(mode, state.activeLeaderboard) + " · " + (text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode"), "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "link-button"));
            listNode.appendChild(li);
          });
          details.appendChild(listNode);
          section.appendChild(details);
        }
      }
      parent.appendChild(section);
    }
    function safeUrl(value) {
      try {
        var url = new URL(text(value).trim());
        return url.protocol === "https:" || url.protocol === "http:" ? url.href : "";
      } catch (_) { return ""; }
    }
    function youtubeVideoId(value) {
      var normalized = safeUrl(value);
      if (!normalized) return "";
      try {
        var url = new URL(normalized);
        var host = url.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./, "");
        var id = "";
        if (host === "youtu.be") {
          id = url.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean)[0] || "";
        } else if (["youtube.com", "m.youtube.com", "music.youtube.com", "youtube-nocookie.com"].indexOf(host) >= 0) {
          if (url.pathname === "/watch") id = url.searchParams.get("v") || "";
          else {
            var match = url.pathname.match(/^\/(?:embed|shorts|live|v)\/([^/?]+)/);
            if (match) id = match[1];
          }
        }
        return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(id) ? id : "";
      } catch (_) { return ""; }
    }
    function modeVideoEmbed(value) {
      var normalized = safeUrl(value);
      if (!normalized) return null;
      var youtubeId = youtubeVideoId(normalized);
      if (youtubeId) return { kind: "iframe", src: "https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/" + youtubeId + "?autoplay=1&rel=0" };
      try {
        var url = new URL(normalized);
        var host = url.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./, "");
        if (host === "vimeo.com" || host === "player.vimeo.com") {
          var vimeoMatch = url.pathname.match(/(?:^|\/)(?:video\/)?(\d+)(?:$|\/)/);
          if (vimeoMatch) return { kind: "iframe", src: "https://player.vimeo.com/video/" + vimeoMatch[1] + "?autoplay=1" };
        }
        if (/\.(?:mp4|webm|ogg|ogv)$/i.test(url.pathname)) return { kind: "video", src: normalized };
      } catch (_) { }
      return { kind: "external", src: normalized };
    }
    function thumbnailFromVideoUrl(value) {
      var id = youtubeVideoId(value);
      return id ? "https://img.youtube.com/vi/" + id + "/hqdefault.jpg" : "";
    }
    function resolveModeThumbnail(imageValue, videoValue, imageField) {
      var fromVideo = thumbnailFromVideoUrl(videoValue);
      var manualImage = safeUrl(imageValue);
      return imageField && imageField.dataset.autoThumbnail === "true"
        ? fromVideo || manualImage
        : manualImage || fromVideo;
    }
    function syncModeThumbnailFields(videoField, imageField, detectExisting) {
      if (!videoField || !imageField) return;
      var generated = thumbnailFromVideoUrl(videoField.value);
      if (detectExisting && generated && safeUrl(imageField.value) === generated) imageField.dataset.autoThumbnail = "true";
      var isAuto = imageField.dataset.autoThumbnail === "true";
      if (!imageField.value.trim() || isAuto) {
        if (generated) {
          imageField.value = generated;
          imageField.dataset.autoThumbnail = "true";
        } else if (isAuto) {
          imageField.value = "";
          imageField.dataset.autoThumbnail = "false";
        }
      }
    }
    function setNotice(message, tone) {
      notice.textContent = message || "";
      notice.dataset.tone = message ? (tone || "info") : "";
    }
    function setAuthMessage(message, good) {
      authMessage.textContent = message || "";
      authMessage.classList.toggle("visible", Boolean(message));
      authMessage.classList.toggle("good", Boolean(good));
    }
    function setEditMessage(message, good) {
      editMessage.textContent = message || "";
      editMessage.classList.toggle("visible", Boolean(message));
      editMessage.classList.toggle("good", Boolean(good));
    }
    function setSubmissionEditMessage(message, good) {
      var target = byId("edit-submission-message");
      target.textContent = message || "";
      target.classList.toggle("visible", Boolean(message));
      target.classList.toggle("good", Boolean(good));
    }
    function setRejectRunMessage(message, good) {
      var target = byId("reject-run-message");
      target.textContent = message || "";
      target.classList.toggle("visible", Boolean(message));
      target.classList.toggle("good", Boolean(good));
    }
    function validateEditForm(form) {
      if (form.checkValidity()) return true;
      var invalidField = form.querySelector(":invalid");
      var invalidLabel = invalidField && invalidField.labels && invalidField.labels.length
        ? invalidField.labels[0].textContent.trim()
        : "A required field";
      setEditMessage(invalidLabel + " is missing or invalid. Please check it and try again.", false);
      if (invalidField) invalidField.focus();
      return false;
    }
    function showModal(modal) {
      modal.classList.remove("hidden");
      var field = modal.querySelector("input:not([type=hidden]), select, textarea");
      if (field) field.focus();
    }
    function hideModal(modal) { modal.classList.add("hidden"); }
    function askConfirm(message, title, confirmLabel) {
      byId("confirm-title").textContent = title || "Confirm action";
      byId("confirm-message").textContent = message;
      byId("confirm-accept").textContent = confirmLabel || "Confirm";
      confirmModal.classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("confirm-accept").focus();
      return new Promise(function (resolve) { state.confirmResolver = resolve; });
    }
    function finishConfirm(accepted) {
      if (!state.confirmResolver) return;
      var resolve = state.confirmResolver;
      state.confirmResolver = null;
      hideModal(confirmModal);
      resolve(Boolean(accepted));
    }
    function button(label, action, data, className) {
      var node = document.createElement("button");
      node.type = "button";
      node.textContent = label;
      node.dataset.action = action;
      if (className) node.className = className;
      Object.keys(data || {}).forEach(function (key) { node.dataset[key] = text(data[key]); });
      if (action === "open-player") {
        var playerNameText = element("span", "player-name-text", label);
        decoratePlayerName(playerNameText, data && data.playerName);
        node.replaceChildren(playerNameText);
      }
      return node;
    }
    function element(tag, className, value) {
      var node = document.createElement(tag);
      if (className) node.className = className;
      if (value != null) node.textContent = text(value);
      return node;
    }
    function actionRow() { return element("div", "mini-actions"); }
    function addParagraph(parent, label, value) {
      var p = document.createElement("p");
      p.appendChild(element("strong", "", label + (value ? ": " : "")));
      if (value) p.appendChild(document.createTextNode(text(value)));
      parent.appendChild(p);
      return p;
    }
    function openExternal(url, label, className) {
      var href = safeUrl(url);
      if (!href) return null;
      var link = element("a", className || "", label);
      link.href = href;
      link.target = "_blank";
      link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
      return link;
    }
    function must(result, label) {
      if (result && result.error) throw new Error(label + ": " + friendlyDatabaseError(result.error));
      return result ? result.data : null;
    }
    function isStaff() {
      if (state.user && lower(state.user.username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME) return true;
      var role = state.user ? lower(state.user.role) : "";
      return role === "staff" || role === "admin";
    }
    function isListOwner() {
      return Boolean(state.user && lower(state.user.username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME);
    }
    function currentClan() {
      if (!state.user) return null;
      var username = lower(state.user.username);
      var tags = Object.keys(state.clans);
      for (var i = 0; i < tags.length; i++) {
        var clan = state.clans[tags[i]];
        if ((clan.members || []).some(function (member) { return lower(member) === username; })) return clan;
      }
      return null;
    }
    function normalizeCountryCode(code) {
      code = text(code).trim().toUpperCase();
      return COUNTRY_CODES.indexOf(code) >= 0 ? code : "";
    }
    function countryName(code) {
      code = normalizeCountryCode(code);
      if (!code) return "";
      if (code === "XK") return "Kosovo";
      try {
        var name = COUNTRY_DISPLAY_NAMES ? COUNTRY_DISPLAY_NAMES.of(code) : code;
        return name && name !== code ? name : code;
      } catch (_) { return code; }
    }
    function countryFlag(code) {
      code = normalizeCountryCode(code);
      if (!code) return "";
      return code;
    }
    function countryFlagImage(code) {
      code = normalizeCountryCode(code);
      if (!code) return null;
      var image = document.createElement("img");
      image.className = "country-flag-image";
      image.src = "https://flagcdn.com/w40/" + code.toLowerCase() + ".png";
      image.alt = "";
      image.width = 24;
      image.height = 16;
      image.loading = "lazy";
      image.decoding = "async";
      image.onerror = function () {
        var fallback = element("span", "country-flag-fallback", countryFlag(code));
        fallback.setAttribute("aria-label", countryName(code));
        if (image.parentNode) image.parentNode.replaceChild(fallback, image);
      };
      return image;
    }
    function countryCodeForPlayer(username) {
      var profile = state.profileCustomizations[lower(username)] || {};
      return normalizeCountryCode(profile.country_code);
    }
    function playerShowsCountryFlag(username) {
      var profile = state.profileCustomizations[lower(username)] || {};
      return Boolean(countryCodeForPlayer(username)) && profile.show_country_flag !== false;
    }
    function countryFlagButton(code, className) {
      code = normalizeCountryCode(code);
      if (!code) return null;
      var name = countryName(code);
      var control = button("", "open-country", { countryCode: code }, className || "country-flag-button");
      control.appendChild(countryFlagImage(code));
      control.setAttribute("aria-label", "View " + name + " country page");
      control.title = "View " + name + " country page";
      return control;
    }
    function countryNameButton(code, includeFlag, className) {
      code = normalizeCountryCode(code);
      if (!code) return null;
      var control = button("", "open-country", { countryCode: code }, className || "country-name-button");
      if (includeFlag) {
        control.appendChild(countryFlagImage(code));
        control.appendChild(document.createTextNode(" "));
      }
      control.appendChild(document.createTextNode(countryName(code)));
      control.setAttribute("aria-label", "View " + countryName(code) + " country page");
      control.title = "View " + countryName(code) + " country page";
      return control;
    }
    function appendPlayerCountryFlag(parent, username) {
      var code = countryCodeForPlayer(username);
      if (!code || !playerShowsCountryFlag(username)) return null;
      var flagButton = countryFlagButton(code);
      if (flagButton) parent.appendChild(flagButton);
      return flagButton;
    }
    function availableCountries() {
      var found = Object.create(null);
      Object.keys(state.profileCustomizations).forEach(function (key) {
        var code = normalizeCountryCode(state.profileCustomizations[key].country_code);
        if (code) found[code] = true;
      });
      return Object.keys(found).sort(function (a, b) {
        return countryName(a).localeCompare(countryName(b), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      });
    }
    function clanForPlayer(username) {
      var needle = lower(username);
      var tags = Object.keys(state.clans);
      for (var i = 0; i < tags.length; i++) {
        var clan = state.clans[tags[i]];
        if ((clan.members || []).some(function (member) { return lower(member) === needle; })) return clan;
      }
      return null;
    }
    function modeById(id) {
      return state.modes.find(function (mode) { return modeIdEquals(mode.id, id); }) || null;
    }
    function normalizeGuess(value) {
      return lower(value).replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
    }
    function setGuessFeedback(message, good) {
      var feedback = byId("guess-feedback");
      feedback.textContent = message || "";
      feedback.classList.add("visible");
      feedback.classList.toggle("good", Boolean(good));
    }
    function renderGuessStreakSummary(message) {
      var summary = byId("guess-streak-summary");
      if (message) {
        summary.textContent = message;
      } else if (!state.user) {
        summary.textContent = "Sign in to save a personal streak.";
      } else if (!state.guessTrackingAvailable) {
        summary.textContent = "Streak saving is unavailable until the Supabase streak setup is run.";
      } else {
        summary.textContent = "Your streak: " + state.guessStreak + " · Best: " + state.guessBestStreak;
      }
    }
    function renderGuessStreakLeaderboard(rows, message) {
      var list = byId("guess-streak-list");
      list.replaceChildren();
      if (message) {
        list.appendChild(element("li", "muted", message));
        return;
      }
      if (!rows || !rows.length) {
        list.appendChild(element("li", "muted", "No recorded streaks yet. Sign in and play to appear here."));
        return;
      }
      rows.forEach(function (row, index) {
        var item = element("li", "streak-row");
        item.appendChild(element("span", "", (index + 1) + ". " + row.username));
        item.appendChild(element("span", "", "Current " + Number(row.guess_streak || 0) + " · Best " + Number(row.guess_best_streak || 0)));
        list.appendChild(item);
      });
    }
    async function loadGuessStreakLeaderboard() {
      if (!state.db) {
        renderGuessStreakLeaderboard([], "Streak leaderboard is unavailable while the database is offline.");
        return;
      }
      var result = await state.db.from("accounts")
        .select("username,guess_streak,guess_best_streak")
        .gt("guess_best_streak", 0)
        .order("guess_best_streak", { ascending: false })
        .order("guess_streak", { ascending: false })
        .order("username", { ascending: true })
        .limit(10);
      if (result.error) {
        renderGuessStreakLeaderboard([], "Run supabase-guess-streak.sql to enable streak rankings.");
        return;
      }
      renderGuessStreakLeaderboard(result.data || []);
    }
    function parseGuessRoundResponse(response, actionLabel) {
      if (response.error) {
        var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
          ? "The Supabase streak functions are missing or have old session-token arguments. Run supabase-full-repair.sql, then reload the site."
          : friendlyDatabaseError(response.error);
        throw new Error(actionLabel + ": " + detail);
      }
      var result = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data[0] : response.data;
      if (!result || !result.ok) throw new Error(actionLabel + ": " + (result && result.message ? result.message : "Streak could not be updated."));
      return result;
    }
    async function beginGuessRound() {
      if (!state.user || !state.accountPassword || !state.db) {
        state.guessTrackingAvailable = false;
        state.guessRoundStarted = false;
        renderGuessStreakSummary();
        return;
      }
      var result = parseGuessRoundResponse(await state.db.rpc("begin_guess_mode_round", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword
      }), "Could not start streak tracking");
      state.guessRoundToken = result.round_token;
      state.guessStreak = Number(result.guess_streak || 0);
      state.guessBestStreak = Number(result.guess_best_streak || 0);
      state.guessTrackingAvailable = true;
      state.guessRoundStarted = true;
      renderGuessStreakSummary();
    }
    async function finishGuessRound(won) {
      if (!state.guessRoundStarted || !state.user || !state.accountPassword || !state.db) return null;
      if (state.guessResultPending) throw new Error("Your streak is still being saved. Please wait a moment.");
      state.guessResultPending = true;
      try {
        var result = parseGuessRoundResponse(await state.db.rpc("finish_guess_mode_round", {
          p_username: state.user.username,
          p_password: state.accountPassword,
          p_round_token: state.guessRoundToken,
          p_won: Boolean(won)
        }), "Could not save streak");
        state.guessRoundStarted = false;
        state.guessRoundToken = null;
        state.guessStreak = Number(result.guess_streak || 0);
        state.guessBestStreak = Number(result.guess_best_streak || 0);
        renderGuessStreakSummary();
        loadGuessStreakLeaderboard();
        return result;
      } finally {
        state.guessResultPending = false;
      }
    }
    async function closeGuessGame() {
      if (state.guessRoundStarted && !state.guessFinished) {
        await finishGuessRound(state.guessOutcome === true);
        if (state.guessOutcome !== true) setNotice("Round abandoned. Your Guess the Mode streak has been reset.", "error");
      } else if (state.guessRoundStarted && state.guessFinished) {
        await finishGuessRound(state.guessOutcome === true);
      }
      state.guessFinished = true;
      hideModal(guessModal);
    }
    function requestCloseGuessGame() {
      closeGuessGame().catch(function (error) { setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error"); });
    }
    function applyGuessReveal(level) {
      var revealLevel = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Number(level) || 0));
      var cropSize = [10, 22, 40, 100][revealLevel];
      var frame = byId("guess-image-frame");
      var image = byId("guess-image");
      frame.dataset.reveal = String(revealLevel);
      if (cropSize >= 100) {
        image.style.clipPath = "inset(0 round 8px)";
        return;
      }
      var left = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - cropSize, state.guessCropX * 100 - cropSize / 2));
      var top = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - cropSize, state.guessCropY * 100 - cropSize / 2));
      var right = 100 - left - cropSize;
      var bottom = 100 - top - cropSize;
      image.style.clipPath = "inset(" + top + "% " + right + "% " + bottom + "% " + left + "% round 8px)";
    }
    async function startGuessGame() {
      var eligible = state.modes.filter(function (mode) {
        return Boolean(safeUrl(mode.direct_image_url || mode.directImageUrl));
      });
      if (!eligible.length) {
        setNotice("Guess the Mode needs at least one mode with a valid thumbnail image.", "error");
        return;
      }
      if (state.guessRoundStarted && !state.guessFinished) {
        await finishGuessRound(state.guessOutcome === true);
        if (state.guessOutcome !== true) setNotice("Previous round abandoned. Your Guess the Mode streak has been reset.", "error");
      } else if (state.guessRoundStarted && state.guessFinished) {
        await finishGuessRound(state.guessOutcome === true);
      }
      state.guessMode = eligible[Math.floor(Math.random() * eligible.length)];
      var selectedMode = state.guessMode;
      var selectedImageUrl = safeUrl(selectedMode.direct_image_url || selectedMode.directImageUrl);
      state.guessAttempts = 0;
      state.guessFinished = false;
      state.guessRoundStarted = false;
      state.guessRoundToken = null;
      state.guessOutcome = null;
      state.guessTrackingAvailable = false;
      state.guessCropX = Math.random();
      state.guessCropY = Math.random();
      applyGuessReveal(0);
      var image = byId("guess-image");
      image.onerror = function () {
        if (state.guessMode !== selectedMode) return;
        state.guessFinished = true;
        byId("guess-input").disabled = true;
        byId("guess-submit").disabled = true;
        byId("guess-again").classList.remove("hidden");
        setGuessFeedback("This mode's picture could not be loaded. Try another round.", false);
      };
      image.alt = "A small cropped part of a mode picture";
      image.src = selectedImageUrl;
      byId("guess-input").value = "";
      byId("guess-input").disabled = false;
      byId("guess-submit").disabled = false;
      byId("guess-again").classList.add("hidden");
      setGuessFeedback("3 guesses left. What mode is this?", false);
      try {
        await beginGuessRound();
      } catch (error) {
        state.guessTrackingAvailable = false;
        state.guessRoundStarted = false;
        renderGuessStreakSummary();
      }
      showModal(guessModal);
      await loadGuessStreakLeaderboard();
    }
    async function submitGuess() {
      if (!state.guessMode || state.guessFinished) return;
      var input = byId("guess-input");
      var answer = state.guessMode.title || "Untitled mode";
      state.guessAttempts += 1;
      if (normalizeGuess(input.value) === normalizeGuess(answer)) {
        state.guessFinished = true;
        state.guessOutcome = true;
        applyGuessReveal(3);
        input.disabled = true;
        byId("guess-submit").disabled = true;
        byId("guess-again").classList.remove("hidden");
        try {
          var winResult = await finishGuessRound(true);
          setGuessFeedback("Correct! The mode was “" + answer + "”." + (winResult ? " Streak: " + state.guessStreak + " · Best: " + state.guessBestStreak + "." : ""), true);
        } catch (error) {
          setGuessFeedback("Correct, but the streak could not be saved. Keep this game open and try again after checking the Supabase setup.", false);
        }
        return;
      }
      applyGuessReveal(state.guessAttempts);
      var remaining = 3 - state.guessAttempts;
      if (remaining <= 0) {
        state.guessFinished = true;
        state.guessOutcome = false;
        input.disabled = true;
        byId("guess-submit").disabled = true;
        byId("guess-again").classList.remove("hidden");
        try {
          await finishGuessRound(false);
          setGuessFeedback("No guesses left — the mode was “" + answer + "”. Your current streak is reset.", false);
        } catch (error) {
          setGuessFeedback("No guesses left — the mode was “" + answer + "”. The streak reset could not be saved; please retry.", false);
        }
      } else {
        input.value = "";
        setGuessFeedback("Not quite. " + remaining + " guesses left; more of the picture is revealed.", false);
        input.focus();
      }
    }
    function skillPercentage(mode, key) {
      var raw = mode ? mode[key] : null;
      if (raw === null || raw === undefined || text(raw).trim() === "") return null;
      var value = Number(raw);
      return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 100 ? value : null;
    }
    function highestModeSkills(mode) {
      var configured = MODE_SKILLS.map(function (skill) {
        return { key: skill.key, label: skill.label, value: skillPercentage(mode, skill.key) };
      }).filter(function (skill) { return skill.value !== null; });
      if (!configured.length) return [];
      var highest = Math.max.apply(null, configured.map(function (skill) { return skill.value; }));
      if (highest <= 0) return [];
      return configured.filter(function (skill) { return skill.value === highest; });
    }
    function skillLabel(key) {
      var skill = MODE_SKILLS.find(function (item) { return item.key === key; });
      return skill ? skill.label : "selected skill";
    }
    function rankPoints(rank, modeCount) {
      var count = modeCount == null ? rankedModes().length : Number(modeCount);
      if (!count || !Number.isFinite(Number(rank))) return 1;
      rank = Math.max(1, Math.min(count, Number(rank)));
      var max = count * 5;
      var inverse = Math.max(0, 1 - ((Number(rank) - 1) / count));
      var curveA = max * Math.pow(inverse, 2.5);
      var curveB = (max * (Math.exp(7 * Math.pow(inverse, .7)) - 1)) / (Math.exp(7) - 1);
      return Math.min(max, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Math.max(curveA, curveB))));
    }
    function playerScores() {
      var scores = Object.create(null);
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        if (!modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, state.activeLeaderboard)) return;
        var names = [text(mode.verifier).trim()].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
        var seen = Object.create(null);
        names.forEach(function (name) {
          name = text(name).trim();
          if (!name || lower(name) === "unknown" || seen[lower(name)]) return;
          seen[lower(name)] = true;
          var key = lower(name);
          if (!scores[key]) scores[key] = { name: name, points: 0, completions: 0 };
          scores[key].points += modePoints(mode);
          scores[key].completions += 1;
        });
      });
      if (state.includePackPoints) Object.keys(scores).forEach(function (key) {
        scores[key].points += packBonusPointsForPlayer(key, state.activeLeaderboard);
      });
      return Object.keys(scores).map(function (key) { return scores[key]; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return b.points - a.points || a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
    }
    function modeAverageEnjoyment(mode) {
      var summary = state.modeEnjoymentAverages[text(mode && mode.id)];
      return summary && summary.count ? summary.total / summary.count : null;
    }
    function modeEnjoymentRatingFor(mode, username) {
      var ratings = state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[text(mode && mode.id)] || [];
      return ratings.find(function (rating) { return lower(rating.player_username) === lower(username); }) || null;
    }
    function enjoymentRatingDescription(rating) {
      var labels = ["Hated it", "Terrible", "Very unenjoyable", "Unenjoyable", "Slightly unenjoyable", "Neutral", "Slightly enjoyable", "Enjoyable", "Very enjoyable", "Extremely enjoyable", "Loved it"];
      var value = Number(rating);
      return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value < labels.length ? labels[value] : "";
    }
    function beatenModeEnjoymentSummary(completions) {
      var total = 0;
      var count = 0;
      completions.forEach(function (item) {
        var modeAverage = modeAverageEnjoyment(item.mode);
        if (modeAverage === null) return;
        total += modeAverage;
        count += 1;
      });
      return { average: count ? total / count : null, count: count };
    }
    function playerSkillProfile(completions) {
      var totals = Object.create(null);
      var counts = Object.create(null);
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) { totals[skill.key] = 0; counts[skill.key] = 0; });
      completions.forEach(function (item) {
        MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
          var value = skillPercentage(item.mode, skill.key);
          if (value === null) return;
          totals[skill.key] += value;
          counts[skill.key] += 1;
        });
      });
      var averages = Object.create(null);
      var totalAverage = 0;
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
        averages[skill.key] = counts[skill.key] ? totals[skill.key] / counts[skill.key] : 0;
        totalAverage += averages[skill.key];
      });
      if (!totalAverage) return null;
      var values = Object.create(null);
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
        values[skill.key] = averages[skill.key] / totalAverage * 100;
      });
      return { values: values, ratedCompletions: completions.filter(function (item) {
        return MODE_SKILLS.some(function (skill) { return skillPercentage(item.mode, skill.key) !== null; });
      }).length };
    }
    function modeSkillMix(mode) {
      var percentages = MODE_SKILLS.map(function (skill) { return skillPercentage(mode, skill.key); });
      var total = percentages.reduce(function (sum, value) { return sum + (value === null ? 0 : value); }, 0);
      if (total <= 0) return null;
      var mix = Object.create(null);
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill, index) { mix[skill.key] = (percentages[index] || 0) / total * 100; });
      return mix;
    }
    function recommendMode(completions, profile, skippedModeIds) {
      if (!completions.length || !profile) return null;
      var activeBoard = leaderboardConfig();
      var completedIds = Object.create(null);
      completions.forEach(function (item) { completedIds[text(item.mode.id)] = true; });
      var completedRanks = completions.map(function (item) { return leaderboardRank(item.mode, activeBoard.key); })
        .filter(function (rank) { return rank !== null; });
      var beatenEnjoyment = beatenModeEnjoymentSummary(completions);
      var playerAverageEnjoyment = beatenEnjoyment.average;
      var skippedIds = Object.create(null);
      (skippedModeIds || []).forEach(function (id) { skippedIds[text(id)] = true; });
      var rankedCount = rankedModesForLeaderboard(activeBoard.key).length;
      var candidates = [];
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        if (!modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, activeBoard.key) || completedIds[text(mode.id)] || skippedIds[text(mode.id)]) return;
        var candidateMix = modeSkillMix(mode);
        if (!candidateMix) return;
        var skillDistance = 0;
        MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
          skillDistance += Math.abs(profile.values[skill.key] - candidateMix[skill.key]);
        });
        var skillMatch = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 - skillDistance / 2));
        var candidateEnjoyment = modeAverageEnjoyment(mode);
        var candidateRank = leaderboardRank(mode, activeBoard.key);
        var enjoymentMatch = playerAverageEnjoyment === null || candidateEnjoyment === null
          ? null
          : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 - Math.abs(playerAverageEnjoyment - candidateEnjoyment) * 10));
        var nearestPlacementDistance = null;
        if (candidateRank !== null && completedRanks.length) {
          nearestPlacementDistance = Math.min.apply(null, completedRanks.map(function (rank) {
            return Math.abs(candidateRank - rank);
          }));
        }
        var placementMatch = nearestPlacementDistance === null
          ? null
          : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 - nearestPlacementDistance / Math.max(1, rankedCount - 1) * 100));
        var weightedMatch = skillMatch * 0.5;
        var totalWeight = 0.5;
        if (enjoymentMatch !== null) {
          weightedMatch += enjoymentMatch * 0.1;
          totalWeight += 0.1;
        }
        if (placementMatch !== null) {
          weightedMatch += placementMatch * 0.4;
          totalWeight += 0.4;
        }
        candidates.push({
          mode: mode,
          rank: candidateRank,
          assignedPlacement: Number(mode.placement),
          skillMatch: skillMatch,
          enjoymentMatch: enjoymentMatch,
          placementMatch: placementMatch,
          matchPercent: weightedMatch / totalWeight
        });
      });
      candidates.sort(function (a, b) {
        return b.matchPercent - a.matchPercent
          || b.skillMatch - a.skillMatch
          || (b.placementMatch === null ? -1 : b.placementMatch) - (a.placementMatch === null ? -1 : a.placementMatch)
          || (b.enjoymentMatch === null ? -1 : b.enjoymentMatch) - (a.enjoymentMatch === null ? -1 : a.enjoymentMatch)
          || (a.rank || a.assignedPlacement) - (b.rank || b.assignedPlacement);
      });
      return candidates.length ? candidates[0] : null;
    }
    function renderPlayerRecommendations(box, username, completions) {
      var profile = playerSkillProfile(completions);
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Completion skill profile"));
      if (profile) {
        var breakdown = element("div", "skill-breakdown");
        MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
          breakdown.appendChild(element("div", "detail-box", skill.label + " " + profile.values[skill.key].toFixed(1) + "%"));
        });
        box.appendChild(breakdown);
        box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Based on " + profile.ratedCompletions + " completed " + (profile.ratedCompletions === 1 ? "mode with skill ratings." : "modes with skill ratings.")));
      } else {
        box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No skill ratings are available from this player's completed modes yet."));
      }

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Suggested mode"));
      var playerKey = lower(username);
      var skippedModeIds = state.skippedSuggestions[playerKey] || [];
      var recommendation = recommendMode(completions, profile, skippedModeIds);
      if (!recommendation) {
        box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", profile
          ? (skippedModeIds.length ? "No more uncompleted mode suggestions are available." : "No uncompleted modes with skill ratings are available to suggest.")
          : "Add skill percentages to completed modes to get a personalized suggestion."));
