        return;
      }
      var mode = recommendation.mode;
      var card = element("div", "queue-card suggested-mode-card");
      card.appendChild(button(mode.title || "Untitled mode", "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "mode-title"));
      card.appendChild(element("p", "source", (mode.source || "Source not listed") + " · " + "Position #" + recommendation.rank));
      card.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Based on your skill profile, nearest completed ranked placement, and average enjoyment of modes you have beaten."));
      var matchChips = element("div", "chips");
      matchChips.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Overall match " + Math.round(recommendation.matchPercent) + "%"));
      matchChips.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Skill profile match " + Math.round(recommendation.skillMatch) + "%"));
      if (recommendation.placementMatch !== null) matchChips.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Placement match " + Math.round(recommendation.placementMatch) + "%"));
      if (recommendation.enjoymentMatch !== null) matchChips.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Enjoyment match " + Math.round(recommendation.enjoymentMatch) + "%"));
      card.appendChild(matchChips);
      card.appendChild(button("Next closest suggestion", "skip-mode-suggestion", { playerName: username, modeId: mode.id }));
      box.appendChild(card);
    }
    function restorePreviewHome() {
      var panel = byId("preview-panel");
      if (panel.parentNode !== previewHome) previewHome.appendChild(panel);
      if (inlineDetailHost) {
        inlineDetailHost.remove();
        inlineDetailHost = null;
      }
      byId("workspace").classList.remove("inline-details");
    }
    var DETAIL_QUERY_KEYS = ["mode", "player", "clan", "country"];
    function detailPermalink(type, value) {
      var url = new URL(window.location.href);
      DETAIL_QUERY_KEYS.forEach(function (key) { url.searchParams.delete(key); });
      if (type && value != null && text(value).trim()) {
        url.searchParams.delete("page");
        url.searchParams.set(type, text(value).trim());
      }
      return url.href;
    }
    function updateDetailLocation(type, value, replace) {
      var url = new URL(detailPermalink(type, value));
      var next = url.pathname + url.search + url.hash;
      var current = window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash;
      if (next === current) return;
      try {
        window.history[replace ? "replaceState" : "pushState"]({ leaderboardDetail: type || null }, "", next);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn("Could not update the detail link in this preview:", error);
      }
    }
    var SITE_PAGE_NAMES = ["leaderboard", "stats", "clans", "packs", "guess", "submit", "rules", "inbox", "settings", "signin", "staff"];
    function updateSitePageLocation(page, replace) {
      var url = new URL(window.location.href);
      DETAIL_QUERY_KEYS.forEach(function (key) { url.searchParams.delete(key); });
      if (page === "leaderboard") url.searchParams.delete("page");
      else url.searchParams.set("page", page);
      var next = url.pathname + url.search + url.hash;
      var current = window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash;
      if (next === current) return;
      try {
        window.history[replace ? "replaceState" : "pushState"]({ sitePage: page }, "", next);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn("Could not update the page link in this preview:", error);
      }
    }
    function syncSitePageNav(page) {
      var activePage = page === "detail" ? "leaderboard" : page;
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-site-page-link]").forEach(function (link) {
        if (link.dataset.sitePageLink === activePage) link.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
        else link.removeAttribute("aria-current");
      });
    }
    async function navigateToSitePage(page, options) {
      options = options || {};
      page = lower(page);
      var requestedPage = page;
      if (SITE_PAGE_NAMES.indexOf(page) < 0) page = "leaderboard";
      if (page === "signin" && state.user) {
        setNotice("You are already signed in as " + state.user.username + ".", "info");
        page = "leaderboard";
      }
      if ((page === "clans" || page === "submit" || page === "inbox" || page === "settings") && !state.user) {
        setNotice("Sign in to open that page.", "info");
        page = "signin";
      }
      if (page === "staff" && !isStaff()) {
        if (!state.user) {
          setNotice("Sign in to open staff tools.", "info");
          page = "signin";
        } else {
          setNotice("Staff access is required to open that page.", "error");
          page = "leaderboard";
        }
      }

      var previousPage = document.body.dataset.sitePage || "leaderboard";
      if (!options.fromHistory || requestedPage !== page) updateSitePageLocation(page, Boolean(options.replace || options.fromHistory));
      document.body.dataset.sitePage = page;
      syncSitePageNav(page);
      if (previousPage === "inbox" && page !== "inbox" && !byId("inbox-panel").classList.contains("hidden")) await closeInbox();
      if (previousPage === "guess" && page !== "guess" && !guessModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
        await closeGuessGame();
        hideModal(guessModal);
      }
      if (page !== "submit") hideModal(submitModal);
      if (page !== "signin") hideModal(authPanel);
      if (page !== "settings") setSiteSettingsOpen(false);
      if (page !== "clans" && state.activeView === "clans") closeClanHub(false);
      if (page !== "detail") showEmptyPreview();
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: state.reducedMotion ? "auto" : "smooth" });

      switch (page) {
        case "leaderboard":
          state.activeView = "leaderboard";
          renderListMode();
          break;
        case "stats":
          state.activeView = "leaderboard";
          renderStatsMode();
          break;
        case "clans":
          openClanHub();
          break;
        case "packs":
          renderPacksPage();
          break;
        case "guess":
          showModal(guessModal);
          if (!state.guessRoundStarted || state.guessFinished) await startGuessGame();
          break;
        case "submit":
          byId("submit-as").textContent = state.user.username;
          populateModeSelect();
          showModal(submitModal);
          break;
        case "rules":
          renderListRules(null, true);
          break;
        case "inbox":
          await openInbox();
          break;
        case "settings":
          setSiteSettingsOpen(true);
          break;
        case "signin":
          setAuthMessage("", false);
          authPanel.classList.remove("hidden");
          var signinField = authPanel.querySelector("input:not([type=hidden])");
          if (signinField) signinField.focus();
          break;
        case "staff":
          byId("staff-panel").classList.remove("hidden");
          await renderStaffTab("modes");
          break;
      }
    }
    function addDetailShareLink(box, type, value) {
      updateDetailLocation(type, value, false);
      document.body.dataset.sitePage = "detail";
      syncSitePageNav("detail");
      if (state.preview) {
        state.preview.detailType = type;
        state.preview.detailValue = text(value);
      }
      var shareRow = actionRow();
      shareRow.classList.add("detail-share-actions");
      shareRow.appendChild(button("Copy link", "copy-detail-link", { detailType: type, detailValue: value }));
      var subtitle = box.querySelector(".detail-heading + .muted");
      if (subtitle) subtitle.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", shareRow);
      else {
        var heading = box.querySelector(".detail-heading");
        if (heading) heading.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", shareRow);
        else box.prepend(shareRow);
      }
    }
    function playModeVideo(modeId, trigger) {
      var mode = modeById(modeId);
      var video = mode && modeVideoEmbed(mode.watch_url || mode.watchUrl);
      if (!mode || !video || !trigger) return;
      if (video.kind === "external") {
        var external = openExternal(video.src, "Open video in a new tab");
        if (external) {
          external.className = "detail-box mode-video-fallback";
          trigger.replaceWith(external);
        }
        return;
      }
      var player = element("div", "detail-video-player");
      var closeButton = button("Close video", "stop-mode-video", { modeId: mode.id }, "detail-video-close");
      closeButton.setAttribute("aria-label", "Close video player");
      player.appendChild(closeButton);
      if (video.kind === "video") {
        var nativePlayer = document.createElement("video");
        nativePlayer.controls = true;
        nativePlayer.autoplay = true;
        nativePlayer.playsInline = true;
        nativePlayer.src = video.src;
        nativePlayer.setAttribute("aria-label", "Verification video for " + (mode.title || "mode"));
        player.appendChild(nativePlayer);
      } else {
        var frame = document.createElement("iframe");
        frame.src = video.src;
        frame.title = "Verification video for " + (mode.title || "mode");
        frame.allow = "autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture; fullscreen";
        frame.referrerPolicy = "strict-origin-when-cross-origin";
        frame.allowFullscreen = true;
        frame.loading = "lazy";
        player.appendChild(frame);
      }
      trigger.replaceWith(player);
    }
    async function copyDetailLink(type, value) {
      if (!/^https?:$/.test(window.location.protocol)) {
        setNotice("This is a local preview, so its link cannot be shared. Open the published Netlify site and copy the link there.", "error");
        return;
      }
      var link = detailPermalink(type, value);
      try {
        if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link);
        } else {
          var input = document.createElement("textarea");
          input.value = link;
          input.setAttribute("readonly", "");
          input.style.position = "fixed";
          input.style.opacity = "0";
          document.body.appendChild(input);
          input.select();
          var copied = document.execCommand("copy");
          input.remove();
          if (!copied) throw new Error("Clipboard access is unavailable.");
        }
        setNotice("Link copied. Anyone with it can open this page.", "success");
      } catch (_) {
        setNotice("Could not copy automatically. Copy the page address from your browser.", "error");
      }
    }
    async function restoreDetailFromUrl() {
      var params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      var modeId = params.get("mode");
      if (modeId) {
        if (modeById(modeId)) renderModeDetails(modeId);
        else {
          document.body.dataset.sitePage = "leaderboard";
          syncSitePageNav("leaderboard");
          updateSitePageLocation("leaderboard", true);
          showEmptyPreview("That mode link could not be found.");
        }
        return;
      }
      var playerName = text(params.get("player")).trim();
      if (playerName) {
        await renderPlayerProfile(playerName);
        return;
      }
      var clanTag = text(params.get("clan")).trim();
      if (clanTag) {
        renderClanProfile(clanTag);
        return;
      }
      var countryCode = params.get("country");
      if (countryCode) {
        renderCountryProfile(countryCode);
        return;
      }
      var sitePage = params.get("page");
      if (sitePage) {
        await navigateToSitePage(sitePage, { fromHistory: true });
        return;
      }
      await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard", { fromHistory: true });
    }
    function placePreviewAfter(control) {
      var panel = byId("preview-panel");
      if (!control || panel.contains(control)) return Boolean(inlineDetailHost);
      var row = control.closest("li.mode-card") || control.closest("li");
      if (row && panel.contains(row)) {
        restorePreviewHome();
        return false;
      }
      if (!row) {
        restorePreviewHome();
        var fallbackHost = document.createElement("div");
        fallbackHost.className = "inline-detail-host inline-detail-fallback";
        fallbackHost.setAttribute("role", "region");
        fallbackHost.setAttribute("aria-label", "Selected details");
        byId("workspace").insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", fallbackHost);
        fallbackHost.appendChild(panel);
        inlineDetailHost = fallbackHost;
        byId("workspace").classList.add("inline-details");
        return true;
      }
      var host = document.createElement("li");
      host.className = "inline-detail-host";
      host.setAttribute("role", "presentation");
      row.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", host);
      host.appendChild(panel);
      if (inlineDetailHost && inlineDetailHost !== host) inlineDetailHost.remove();
      inlineDetailHost = host;
      byId("workspace").classList.add("inline-details");
      return true;
    }
    function showPreview(title, subtitle, anchor, dedicatedPage) {
      var workspace = byId("workspace");
      if (dedicatedPage) {
        restorePreviewHome();
        workspace.classList.add("dedicated-detail-view");
      } else {
        workspace.classList.remove("dedicated-detail-view");
        if (anchor) placePreviewAfter(anchor);
      }
      preview.replaceChildren();
      preview.className = "";
      var heading = element("div", "detail-heading");
      heading.appendChild(element("h2", "preview-title", title));
      var closeButton = button(dedicatedPage ? "Back to list" : "Close", "close-preview", {}, "detail-close");
      closeButton.setAttribute("aria-label", dedicatedPage ? "Back to leaderboard" : "Close details");
      heading.appendChild(closeButton);
      preview.appendChild(heading);
      if (subtitle) preview.appendChild(element("p", "muted", subtitle));
      state.preview = { title: title };
      var detailPanel = byId("preview-panel");
      detailPanel.classList.remove("detail-entering");
      detailPanel.classList.remove("hidden");
      void detailPanel.offsetWidth;
      detailPanel.classList.add("detail-open", "detail-entering");
      if (dedicatedPage && anchor) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
          window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: state.reducedMotion ? "auto" : "smooth" });
        });
      }
      return preview;
    }
    function showEmptyPreview(message) {
      updateDetailLocation(null, null, true);
      if (document.body.dataset.sitePage === "detail") {
        document.body.dataset.sitePage = "leaderboard";
        syncSitePageNav("leaderboard");
      }
      byId("workspace").classList.remove("dedicated-detail-view");
      restorePreviewHome();
      byId("preview-panel").classList.remove("detail-open");
      byId("preview-panel").classList.add("hidden");
      preview.replaceChildren(element("div", "preview-empty", message || "Choose a mode, player, or clan to see its details here."));
      state.preview = null;
    }
    function renderListRules(anchor, dedicatedPage) {
      var box = showPreview("List Rules", "How modes qualify for ranked placements", anchor, Boolean(dedicatedPage));
      document.body.dataset.sitePage = "rules";
      box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "These rules explain what the staff reviews before adding a mode to the ranked list. Submitting a run does not automatically add a mode or assign its placement."));

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "How the three lists work"));
      var listRules = element("ul", "detail-list");
      [
        "Staff choose a mode type when adding or editing a mode. That type determines which leaderboard lists include it.",
        "Base modes appear on the Main List, Extended List, and Unlimited List.",
        "Additional Modes, Mod of Mod, and Hardest Settings Challenge modes appear on the Extended List and Unlimited List.",
        "Regular Settings Challenge, Mixed Challenge, Self-imposed Challenge, and Pre-patch modes appear only on the Unlimited List.",
        "Each list calculates its own ranks and points from the modes included on that list. The selected list is shown with the list switch above the rankings."
      ].forEach(function (rule) { listRules.appendChild(element("li", "", rule)); });
      box.appendChild(listRules);

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Ranked mode eligibility"));
      var eligibility = element("ul", "detail-list");
      [
        "The mode must be a real, playable Granny mode with a clear title and source so players can identify the exact challenge.",
        "A ranked entry must represent a distinct challenge. Cosmetic-only duplicates do not receive a separate placement; challenge variants follow the rules below.",
        "Older versions of a mode are called pre-patches. They appear on the Unlimited List only, and only the hardest pre-patch version of that mode is eligible for a placement; easier pre-patches cannot place.",
        "The challenge must be completable through legitimate gameplay, and its completion must be reviewable by list staff.",
        "Staff review each candidate and decide whether it belongs on the list and where it places. A mode may be declined if it is unverifiable, duplicated, or does not fit the list."
      ].forEach(function (rule) { eligibility.appendChild(element("li", "", rule)); });
      box.appendChild(eligibility);

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Self-imposed and settings challenges"));
      var challengeRules = element("ul", "detail-list");
      [
        "Self-imposed challenges are player-chosen restrictions, such as completing a mode without dying or without using certain items. Any self-imposed challenge can receive an Unlimited List placement; it does not have to be the hardest combination.",
        "Changed-settings challenges use options built into the mode and are separate from self-imposed restrictions. The hardest settings challenge appears on the Extended and Unlimited Lists.",
        "Other settings-only challenges can receive placements on the Unlimited List only. Mixed settings and self-imposed challenges remain Unlimited List modes."
      ].forEach(function (rule) { challengeRules.appendChild(element("li", "", rule)); });
      box.appendChild(challengeRules);

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Completion proof"));
      var proof = element("ul", "detail-list");
      [
        "A completion submission needs a video link that lets staff identify the mode and verify that the challenge was completed.",
        "Submission videos must be at least 30 frames per second and 480p resolution.",
        "The video must show the settings menu before the run. For settings challenges, all required settings must be visibly turned on. Then show the entire run continuously from the starting frame through the finish.",
        "Any cuts in the submission video will result in rejection.",
        "If your run uses any glitch staff deem unfair, your submission will be rejected.",
        "Using external tools or modifying gameplay will result in rejection.",
        "If the run does not use the correct mode and settings for the challenge you submitted, it will be rejected.",
        "Cheats are not allowed and will result in rejection.",
        "Staff may ask for clearer proof before approving a record."
      ].forEach(function (rule) { proof.appendChild(element("li", "", rule)); });
      box.appendChild(proof);

    }
    function closeClanHub(restoreScroll) {
      showEmptyPreview();
      state.activeView = "leaderboard";
      byId("clan-panel").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("leaderboard-panel").classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("staff-panel").classList.toggle("hidden", !(state.user && isStaff()));
      byId("clan-button").textContent = "Clan hub";
      if (restoreScroll !== false && state.lastLeaderboardScroll) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
          window.scrollTo({ top: state.lastLeaderboardScroll, behavior: "smooth" });
        });
      }
    }
    function openClanHub() {
      state.lastLeaderboardScroll = window.scrollY;
      state.activeView = "clans";
      showEmptyPreview();
      byId("leaderboard-panel").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("staff-panel").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("clan-panel").classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("clan-button").textContent = "Clan hub";
      renderClanHub();
      window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        byId("clan-panel").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      });
    }
    function parseInboxResponse(response, label) {
      if (response.error) {
        var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
          ? "Inbox support is missing in Supabase. Run the updated supabase-full-repair.sql, then reload the site."
          : friendlyDatabaseError(response.error);
        throw new Error(label + ": " + detail);
      }
      var result = unwrapRpcData(response);
      if (!result || !result.ok) {
        var reason = result && result.code === "banned"
          ? "This account cannot access the inbox."
          : "Sign in again and retry.";
        throw new Error(label + ": " + reason);
      }
      return result;
    }
    function renderInboxCount() {
      var inboxButton = byId("inbox-button");
      var drawerInboxButton = byId("drawer-inbox-button");
      var badge = byId("inbox-count");
      if (!inboxButton || !drawerInboxButton || !badge) return;
      inboxButton.classList.toggle("hidden", !state.user);
      drawerInboxButton.classList.toggle("hidden", !state.user);
      badge.textContent = state.inboxError ? "!" : (state.inboxCount > 99 ? "99+" : text(state.inboxCount));
      badge.classList.toggle("hidden", !state.inboxError && state.inboxCount < 1);
      inboxButton.title = state.inboxError ? "Inbox setup needs attention" : "Inbox";
      inboxButton.setAttribute("aria-label", state.inboxError ? "Open inbox; setup issue" : "Open inbox" + (state.inboxCount ? ", " + state.inboxCount + " unread" : ""));
      inboxButton.setAttribute("aria-controls", "inbox-panel");
      inboxButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", byId("inbox-panel") && !byId("inbox-panel").classList.contains("hidden") ? "true" : "false");
      drawerInboxButton.setAttribute("aria-controls", "inbox-panel");
      drawerInboxButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", inboxButton.getAttribute("aria-expanded"));
      drawerInboxButton.textContent = "Inbox" + (state.inboxCount ? " · " + state.inboxCount + " new" : "");
    }
    function renderInboxPopup() {
      var content = byId("inbox-content");
      if (!content) return;
      content.replaceChildren();
      if (state.inboxError) {
        content.appendChild(element("p", "muted", state.inboxError));
        return;
      }
      if (!state.inboxNotifications.length) {
        content.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "You're all caught up. New submission decisions will appear here."));
        return;
      }
      var notificationsList = element("ul", "list");
      state.inboxNotifications.forEach(function (notification) {
        var item = element("li", "inbox-notification");
        var approved = lower(notification.outcome) === "approved";
        var outcomeText = approved ? "Approved" : "Rejected";
        var mode = modeById(notification.mode_id);
        var modeTitle = mode ? text(mode.title) : "Mode #" + text(notification.mode_id);
        item.appendChild(element("span", "chip inbox-outcome-" + (approved ? "approved" : "rejected"), outcomeText));
        item.appendChild(element("p", "", "Your submission for " + modeTitle + " was " + outcomeText.toLowerCase() + "."));
        if (!approved && notification.rejection_reason) {
          var reasonLabel = REJECTION_REASON_LABELS[notification.rejection_reason] || "Other";
          item.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Reason: " + reasonLabel));
          if (notification.rejection_note) item.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Staff note: " + notification.rejection_note));
        }
        var createdAt = notification.created_at ? new Date(notification.created_at) : null;
        if (createdAt && !isNaN(createdAt.getTime())) item.appendChild(element("span", "inbox-time", createdAt.toLocaleString()));
        notificationsList.appendChild(item);
      });
      content.appendChild(notificationsList);
    }
    async function refreshInboxCount() {
      if (state.inboxClearInProgress) return;
      if (!state.user || !state.db || !state.accountPassword) {
        state.inboxCount = 0;
        state.inboxError = "";
        renderInboxCount();
        return;
      }
      try {
        var response = await state.db.rpc("get_player_inbox", {
          p_username: state.user.username,
          p_password: state.accountPassword,
          p_clear: false
        });
        var result = parseInboxResponse(response, "Could not check inbox");
        state.inboxNotifications = Array.isArray(result.notifications) ? result.notifications : [];
        state.inboxCount = Number(result.count) || state.inboxNotifications.length;
        state.inboxError = "";
        if (!byId("inbox-panel").classList.contains("hidden")) renderInboxPopup();
      } catch (error) {
        state.inboxCount = 0;
        state.inboxError = text(error.message || error);
      }
      renderInboxCount();
    }
    async function openInbox() {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (state.inboxClearInProgress) return;
      state.inboxError = "";
      state.inboxNotifications = [];
      try {
        var response = await state.db.rpc("get_player_inbox", {
          p_username: state.user.username,
          p_password: state.accountPassword,
          p_clear: false
        });
        var result = parseInboxResponse(response, "Could not open inbox");
        state.inboxNotifications = Array.isArray(result.notifications) ? result.notifications : [];
        state.inboxCount = Number(result.count) || state.inboxNotifications.length;
      } catch (error) {
        state.inboxError = text(error.message || error);
      }
      byId("inbox-panel").classList.remove("hidden");
      renderInboxCount();
      renderInboxPopup();
    }
    async function closeInbox() {
      var inboxPanel = byId("inbox-panel");
      var wasOpen = Boolean(inboxPanel && !inboxPanel.classList.contains("hidden"));
      if (inboxPanel) inboxPanel.classList.add("hidden");
      if (!wasOpen && state.inboxClearPromise) {
        await state.inboxClearPromise;
        return;
      }
      var username = state.user && state.user.username;
      var password = state.accountPassword;
      state.inboxNotifications = [];
      state.inboxError = "";
      if (wasOpen && username && password && state.db) {
        state.inboxCount = 0;
        state.inboxClearInProgress = true;
        renderInboxCount();
        state.inboxClearPromise = (async function () {
          try {
            var response = await state.db.rpc("get_player_inbox", {
              p_username: username,
              p_password: password,
              p_clear: true
            });
            parseInboxResponse(response, "Could not clear inbox messages");
          } catch (error) {
            state.inboxError = text(error.message || error);
          } finally {
            state.inboxClearInProgress = false;
            state.inboxClearPromise = null;
            renderInboxCount();
          }
        })();
        await state.inboxClearPromise;
      }
      renderInboxCount();
    }
    function renderAuth() {
      var authArea = byId("auth-area");
      var settingsPanel = byId("site-settings-panel");
      var siteSettingsOpen = Boolean(settingsPanel && !settingsPanel.classList.contains("hidden"));
      authArea.replaceChildren();
      if (state.user) {
        authArea.appendChild(button("My profile · " + state.user.username, "open-own-profile", { playerName: state.user.username }, "drawer-profile-link"));
        var appearanceButton = button("Settings", "toggle-site-settings", {}, "");
        appearanceButton.dataset.sitePageLink = "settings";
        appearanceButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", siteSettingsOpen ? "true" : "false");
        appearanceButton.setAttribute("aria-controls", "site-settings-panel");
        authArea.appendChild(appearanceButton);
      } else {
        setSiteSettingsOpen(false);
        var signinButton = button("Sign in / Register", "auth-action", {}, "login");
        signinButton.dataset.sitePageLink = "signin";
        authArea.appendChild(signinButton);
      }
      var clan = currentClan();
      if (state.user && clan) authArea.appendChild(button("[" + clan.tag + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "chip clan-chip"));
      if (state.user) authArea.appendChild(button("Sign out", "auth-action", {}, "logout"));
      renderInboxCount();
      updateCompletionFilterOptions();
      byId("staff-panel").classList.toggle("hidden", !(state.user && isStaff()));
      byId("staff-packs-button").classList.toggle("hidden", !isListOwner());
      byId("staff-badges-button").classList.toggle("hidden", !isListOwner());
      byId("staff-permissions-button").classList.toggle("hidden", !isListOwner());
      byId("drawer-staff-button").classList.toggle("hidden", !(state.user && isStaff()));
      syncSitePageNav(document.body.dataset.sitePage || "leaderboard");
      if (!isListOwner()) {
        byId("staff-permissions-tab").classList.add("hidden");
        byId("staff-packs-tab").classList.add("hidden");
        byId("staff-badges-tab").classList.add("hidden");
        byId("staff-mode-tab").classList.remove("hidden");
        byId("staff-queue-tab").classList.add("hidden");
      }
      byId("submit-as").textContent = state.user ? state.user.username : "Guest";
      renderClanHub();
    }
    function renderLeaderboard() {
      if (inlineDetailHost && list.contains(inlineDetailHost)) showEmptyPreview();
      var target = list;
      target.replaceChildren();
      var filterSkill = byId("skill-filter").value || "all";
      var deviceFilter = byId("device-filter").value || "all";
      var searchTerm = lower(byId("mode-search").value);
      var modeIdSearch = searchTerm.replace(/^id\s*[:#]?\s*/, "").replace(/^#/, "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
      var modeSort = byId("mode-sort").value || "placement";
      state.modeSort = modeSort;
      updateCompletionFilterOptions();
      var currentBoard = leaderboardConfig();
      state.activeLeaderboard = currentBoard.key;
      byId("list-heading").textContent = currentBoard.label;
      var boardSwitch = byId("leaderboard-list-switch");
      var nextBoard = LEADERBOARDS[(LEADERBOARDS.findIndex(function (item) { return item.key === currentBoard.key; }) + 1) % LEADERBOARDS.length];
      boardSwitch.textContent = currentBoard.label + " ↻";
      boardSwitch.title = "Switch to " + nextBoard.label;
      boardSwitch.setAttribute("aria-label", currentBoard.label + " selected. Switch to " + nextBoard.label);
      var playerCompletionFilters = [
        byId("filter-player-beaten").checked ? "beaten" : "",
        byId("filter-player-not-beaten").checked ? "not-beaten" : ""
      ].filter(Boolean);
      var clanCompletionFilters = [
        byId("filter-clan-beaten").checked ? "beaten" : "",
        byId("filter-clan-not-beaten").checked ? "not-beaten" : ""
      ].filter(Boolean);
      var mobileActiveFilters = (searchTerm ? 1 : 0) + (modeSort !== "placement" ? 1 : 0) + (filterSkill !== "all" ? 1 : 0)
        + (deviceFilter !== "all" ? 1 : 0) + playerCompletionFilters.length + clanCompletionFilters.length;
      byId("mobile-filter-status").textContent = mobileActiveFilters
        ? mobileActiveFilters + (mobileActiveFilters === 1 ? " active filter" : " active filters")
        : "All modes";
      var clan = currentClan();
      var ranked = rankedModesForLeaderboard(currentBoard.key);
      var rankByModeId = Object.create(null);
      ranked.forEach(function (mode, index) { rankByModeId[text(mode.id)] = index + 1; });
      var rankedPool = ranked;
      function selectedStatusMatches(filters, completed) {
        return !filters.length || filters.some(function (filter) {
          return filter === "beaten" ? completed : !completed;
        });
      }
      function matchesMode(mode) {
        var matchesSkill = filterSkill === "all" || highestModeSkills(mode).some(function (skill) { return skill.key === filterSkill; });
        var playableOn = lower(mode.playable_on || mode.playableOn) || "both";
        var matchesDevice = deviceFilter === "all"
          || (deviceFilter === "mobile" && (playableOn === "mobile" || playableOn === "both"))
          || (deviceFilter === "pc" && (playableOn === "pc" || playableOn === "both"))
          || (deviceFilter === "both" && playableOn === "both");
        var beatenByPlayer = state.user ? modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, state.user.username) : false;
        var beatenByClan = modeBeatenByClan(mode, clan);
        var matchesCompletion = selectedStatusMatches(playerCompletionFilters, beatenByPlayer)
          && selectedStatusMatches(clanCompletionFilters, beatenByClan);
        var matchesSearch = !searchTerm || [mode.title, mode.source, mode.verifier, mode.creator, mode.author]
          .some(function (value) { return lower(value).indexOf(searchTerm) >= 0; })
          || Boolean(modeIdSearch && text(mode.id).toLowerCase().indexOf(modeIdSearch) >= 0);
        return matchesSkill && matchesDevice && matchesCompletion && matchesSearch;
      }
      var visibleRankedModes = rankedPool.map(function (mode) {
        var boardRank = rankByModeId[text(mode.id)] || Number(mode.placement) || 1;
        return { mode: mode, rank: boardRank, displayRank: boardRank };
      })
        .filter(function (entry) { return matchesMode(entry.mode); });
      if (modeSort === "enjoyment") {
        visibleRankedModes.sort(function (a, b) {
          var enjoymentA = modeAverageEnjoyment(a.mode);
          var enjoymentB = modeAverageEnjoyment(b.mode);
          if (enjoymentA === null && enjoymentB !== null) return 1;
          if (enjoymentB === null && enjoymentA !== null) return -1;
          return (enjoymentB || 0) - (enjoymentA || 0)
            || (a.rank || 0) - (b.rank || 0)
            || text(a.mode.title).localeCompare(text(b.mode.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
        });
      }
      var hasNarrowingFilters = filterSkill !== "all" || deviceFilter !== "all"
        || playerCompletionFilters.length > 0 || clanCompletionFilters.length > 0 || Boolean(searchTerm);
      if (hasNarrowingFilters) {
        visibleRankedModes.forEach(function (entry, index) { entry.displayRank = index + 1; });
      }
      var noNarrowingFilters = filterSkill === "all" && deviceFilter === "all" && !playerCompletionFilters.length && !clanCompletionFilters.length && !searchTerm;
      var caption = noNarrowingFilters ? rankedPool.length + " ranked modes" : visibleRankedModes.length + " of " + rankedPool.length + " ranked modes";
      byId("list-caption").textContent = caption;
      function appendModeCard(mode, rank) {
        var podiumClass = rank === 1 ? " podium-gold" : rank === 2 ? " podium-silver" : rank === 3 ? " podium-bronze" : "";
        var item = element("li", "mode-card" + podiumClass);
        item.dataset.modeId = text(mode.id);
        var playerHasBeatenMode = Boolean(state.user && modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, state.user.username));
        if (state.highlightBeatenModes && playerHasBeatenMode) item.classList.add("player-completed-highlight");
        var imageUrl = safeUrl(mode.direct_image_url || mode.directImageUrl);
        if (imageUrl) {
          item.classList.add("has-background-thumb");
          item.style.setProperty("--mode-thumb", 'url("' + imageUrl + '")');
          var backgroundImage = document.createElement("img");
          backgroundImage.className = "mode-card-background";
          backgroundImage.classList.toggle("is-hidden", !state.thumbnailBackgrounds);
          backgroundImage.src = imageUrl;
          backgroundImage.alt = "";
          backgroundImage.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
          backgroundImage.loading = "lazy";
          backgroundImage.decoding = "async";
          item.appendChild(backgroundImage);
        }
        var videoUrl = safeUrl(mode.watch_url || mode.watchUrl);
        if (videoUrl) {
          var imageLink = openExternal(videoUrl, "", "thumb-link");
          if (imageUrl) {
            var img = document.createElement("img");
            img.src = imageUrl;
            img.alt = "Thumbnail for " + text(mode.title);
            img.loading = "lazy";
            imageLink.appendChild(img);
          } else imageLink.appendChild(element("span", "thumb-fallback", "Open video"));
          item.appendChild(imageLink);
        } else item.appendChild(element("div", "thumb-link thumb-fallback", "No video link"));
        item.appendChild(element("div", "rank " + (rank === 1 ? "" : rank === 2 ? "silver" : rank === 3 ? "bronze" : "normal"), "#" + rank));
        var info = element("div", "mode-info");
        info.appendChild(button(text(mode.title) || "Untitled mode", "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "mode-title"));
        var creditedPeople = [];
        var creatorName = text(mode.creator || mode.author).trim();
        var verifierName = text(mode.verifier).trim();
        if (creatorName) creditedPeople.push("Creator: " + creatorName);
        if (verifierName) creditedPeople.push("Verifier: " + verifierName);
        if (creditedPeople.length) info.appendChild(element("p", "source mode-credits", creditedPeople.join(" · ")));
        info.appendChild(element("p", "source", text(mode.source) || "Source not listed"));
        var chips = element("div", "chips");
        chips.appendChild(element("span", "chip points", modePoints(mode, currentBoard.key) + " pts"));
        var enjoymentSummary = state.modeEnjoymentAverages[text(mode.id)];
        if (enjoymentSummary && enjoymentSummary.count) {
          var enjoymentAverage = enjoymentSummary.total / enjoymentSummary.count;
          chips.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Avg enjoyment " + enjoymentAverage.toFixed(1) + "/10 · " + enjoymentSummary.count + (enjoymentSummary.count === 1 ? " rating" : " ratings")));
        }
        if (state.highlightBeatenModes && playerHasBeatenMode) chips.appendChild(element("span", "chip beaten-chip", "Beaten"));
        info.appendChild(chips);
        if (state.user && isStaff()) {
          var controls = actionRow();
          controls.appendChild(button("Edit", "edit-mode", { modeId: mode.id }));
          controls.appendChild(button("Delete", "delete-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "danger"));
          info.appendChild(controls);
        }
        item.appendChild(info);
        target.appendChild(item);
      }
      visibleRankedModes.forEach(function (entry) { appendModeCard(entry.mode, entry.displayRank); });
      if (!visibleRankedModes.length) {
        var emptyMessage;
        if (!rankedPool.length) {
          emptyMessage = "No modes are assigned to the " + currentBoard.label + " yet.";
        } else if (searchTerm) {
          emptyMessage = "No modes match “" + text(byId("mode-search").value).trim() + "”.";
        } else if (playerCompletionFilters.length || clanCompletionFilters.length) {
          emptyMessage = "No modes match the selected completion filters.";
        } else if (filterSkill !== "all") {
          emptyMessage = "No modes have " + skillLabel(filterSkill) + " as their highest percentage.";
        } else if (deviceFilter !== "all") {
          emptyMessage = "No modes match the selected device filter.";
        } else {
          emptyMessage = "No modes match the selected filters.";
        }
        target.appendChild(element("li", "empty", emptyMessage));
      }
    }
    function populateCompareSelect(select, entries, valueOf, labelOf, defaultIndex) {
      var previous = select.value;
      select.replaceChildren();
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = text(valueOf(entry));
        option.textContent = text(labelOf(entry));
        select.appendChild(option);
      });
      if (previous && Array.from(select.options).some(function (option) { return option.value === previous; })) select.value = previous;
      else if (entries.length) select.selectedIndex = Math.min(defaultIndex, entries.length - 1);
      select.disabled = entries.length < 2;
    }
    function renderComparisonColumn(host, heading, modes) {
      var column = element("section", "compare-column");
      column.appendChild(element("h4", "", heading + " · " + modes.length));
      if (!modes.length) column.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No exclusive modes."));
      else {
        var modeList = element("div", "compare-mode-list");
        modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
          return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
        }).forEach(function (mode) {
          var label = text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode";
          modeList.appendChild(button(label, "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "link-button"));
        });
        column.appendChild(modeList);
      }
      host.appendChild(column);
    }
    function renderModeComparison(resultId, nameA, nameB, beatsA, beatsB) {
      var result = byId(resultId);
      result.replaceChildren();
      if (!nameA || !nameB) {
        result.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Choose two different " + (resultId.indexOf("clan") >= 0 ? "clans" : "players") + " to compare."));
        return;
      }
      if (lower(nameA) === lower(nameB)) {
        result.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Choose two different entries to compare."));
        return;
      }
      var modesA = state.modes.filter(beatsA);
      var modesB = state.modes.filter(beatsB);
      var onlyA = modesA.filter(function (mode) { return !beatsB(mode); });
      var onlyB = modesB.filter(function (mode) { return !beatsA(mode); });
      var shared = modesA.filter(beatsB).length;
      result.appendChild(element("p", "muted", shared + " modes completed by both."));
      var columns = element("div", "compare-columns");
      renderComparisonColumn(columns, nameA + " only", onlyA);
      renderComparisonColumn(columns, nameB + " only", onlyB);
      result.appendChild(columns);
    }
    function renderPlayerComparison() {
      var scores = playerScores().slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      });
      var selectA = byId("compare-player-a");
      var selectB = byId("compare-player-b");
      populateCompareSelect(selectA, scores, function (entry) { return entry.name; }, function (entry) { return entry.name; }, 0);
      populateCompareSelect(selectB, scores, function (entry) { return entry.name; }, function (entry) { return entry.name; }, 1);
      var nameA = selectA.value;
      var nameB = selectB.value;
      renderModeComparison("player-comparison-results", nameA, nameB,
        function (mode) { return modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, nameA); },
        function (mode) { return modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, nameB); });
    }
    function renderClanComparison() {
      var clans = Object.keys(state.clans).map(function (key) { return state.clans[key]; }).sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      });
      var selectA = byId("compare-clan-a");
      var selectB = byId("compare-clan-b");
      populateCompareSelect(selectA, clans, function (clan) { return clan.tag; }, function (clan) { return clan.name + " [" + clan.tag + "]"; }, 0);
      populateCompareSelect(selectB, clans, function (clan) { return clan.tag; }, function (clan) { return clan.name + " [" + clan.tag + "]"; }, 1);
      var tagA = selectA.value;
      var tagB = selectB.value;
      var clanA = state.clans[lower(tagA)];
      var clanB = state.clans[lower(tagB)];
      renderModeComparison("clan-comparison-results", clanA ? clanA.name + " [" + clanA.tag + "]" : "", clanB ? clanB.name + " [" + clanB.tag + "]" : "",
        function (mode) { return modeBeatenByClan(mode, clanA); },
        function (mode) { return modeBeatenByClan(mode, clanB); });
    }
    function updateCountryRankingFilterOptions() {
      var select = byId("stats-country-filter");
      if (!select) return;
      var countries = availableCountries();
      if (state.countryRankingFilter !== "all" && countries.indexOf(state.countryRankingFilter) < 0) state.countryRankingFilter = "all";
      select.replaceChildren(new Option("All countries", "all"));
      countries.forEach(function (code) { select.appendChild(new Option(countryName(code), code)); });
      select.value = state.countryRankingFilter;
    }
    function countryLeaderboardScores() {
      var scores = Object.create(null);
      Object.keys(state.profileCustomizations).forEach(function (key) {
        var profile = state.profileCustomizations[key] || {};
        var code = normalizeCountryCode(profile.country_code);
        if (!code) return;
        if (!scores[code]) scores[code] = { code: code, members: [], points: 0, completions: 0, clearedModes: [] };
        scores[code].members.push(text(profile.username).trim() || key);
      });
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        if (!modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, state.activeLeaderboard)) return;
        var names = [text(mode.verifier).trim()].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
        var contributorsByCountry = Object.create(null);
        var seenPlayers = Object.create(null);
        names.forEach(function (rawName) {
          var name = text(rawName).trim();
          var playerKey = lower(name);
          if (!name || playerKey === "unknown" || seenPlayers[playerKey]) return;
          seenPlayers[playerKey] = true;
          var code = countryCodeForPlayer(name);
          if (!code || !scores[code]) return;
          if (!contributorsByCountry[code]) contributorsByCountry[code] = [];
          contributorsByCountry[code].push(name);
        });
        Object.keys(contributorsByCountry).forEach(function (code) {
          var entry = scores[code];
          entry.points += modePoints(mode);
          entry.completions += 1;
          entry.clearedModes.push({ mode: mode, rank: leaderboardRank(mode, state.activeLeaderboard), names: contributorsByCountry[code] });
        });
      });
      return Object.keys(scores).map(function (code) { return scores[code]; })
        .map(function (entry) {
          entry.members.sort(function (a, b) { return text(a).localeCompare(text(b), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }); });
          entry.clearedModes.sort(function (a, b) {
            return (a.rank || 0) - (b.rank || 0) || text(a.mode.title).localeCompare(text(b.mode.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
          });
          return entry;
        }).sort(function (a, b) {
          return b.points - a.points || countryName(a.code).localeCompare(countryName(b.code), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
        });
    }
    function renderStats() {
      if (inlineDetailHost && statsList.contains(inlineDetailHost)) showEmptyPreview();
      statsList.replaceChildren();
      var playerView = state.statsKind === "players";
      var clanView = state.statsKind === "clans";
      var countryView = state.statsKind === "countries";
      byId("player-ranking-filter").classList.toggle("hidden", !playerView);
      byId("player-comparison-wrap").classList.toggle("hidden", !playerView);
      byId("clan-comparison-wrap").classList.toggle("hidden", !clanView);
      updateCountryRankingFilterOptions();
      byId("stats-pack-points-filter").value = state.includePackPoints ? "include" : "exclude";
      renderPlayerComparison();
      renderClanComparison();
      var currentBoard = leaderboardConfig();
      var boardIndex = LEADERBOARDS.findIndex(function (item) { return item.key === currentBoard.key; });
      var nextBoard = LEADERBOARDS[(boardIndex + 1) % LEADERBOARDS.length];
      var boardSwitch = byId("leaderboard-list-switch");
      boardSwitch.textContent = currentBoard.label + " ↻";
      boardSwitch.title = "Switch to " + nextBoard.label;
      boardSwitch.setAttribute("aria-label", currentBoard.label + " selected. Switch to " + nextBoard.label);
      byId("list-heading").textContent = currentBoard.label + " · " + (clanView ? "Clan Rankings" : countryView ? "Country Rankings" : "Player Rankings");
      byId("stats-switch").querySelectorAll("[data-kind]").forEach(function (tab) {
        tab.classList.toggle("active", tab.dataset.kind === state.statsKind);
      });
      if (countryView) {
        var countryScores = countryLeaderboardScores();
        byId("list-caption").textContent = countryScores.length + (countryScores.length === 1 ? " country" : " countries");
        if (!countryScores.length) {
          statsList.appendChild(element("li", "empty", "No players have picked a country yet."));
          return;
        }
        countryScores.forEach(function (entry, index) {
          var row = element("li", "mode-card");
          row.style.gridTemplateColumns = "45px minmax(0,1fr) auto";
          row.appendChild(element("div", "rank " + (index === 0 ? "" : index === 1 ? "silver" : index === 2 ? "bronze" : "normal"), "#" + (index + 1)));
          var countryArea = element("div", "mode-info");
          countryArea.appendChild(countryNameButton(entry.code, true, "mode-title country-ranking-title"));
          countryArea.appendChild(element("p", "source", entry.members.length + (entry.members.length === 1 ? " member" : " members") + " · " + entry.completions + " ranked modes completed"));
          row.appendChild(countryArea);
          row.appendChild(element("span", "chip points", entry.points + " pts"));
          statsList.appendChild(row);
        });
        return;
      }
      if (clanView) {
        var clanScores = Object.keys(state.clans).map(function (key) {
          var clan = state.clans[key];
          var members = Object.create(null);
          (Array.isArray(clan.members) ? clan.members : []).forEach(function (member) {
            members[lower(member)] = true;
          });
          var points = 0;
          var completions = 0;
          state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
            if (!modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, state.activeLeaderboard)) return;
            var names = [mode.verifier].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
            var hasClanCompletion = names.some(function (name) {
              return Boolean(members[lower(name)]);
            });
            if (hasClanCompletion) {
              points += modePoints(mode);
              completions += 1;
            }
          });
          return { clan: clan, points: points, completions: completions };
        }).sort(function (a, b) {
          return b.points - a.points || a.clan.name.localeCompare(b.clan.name);
        });
        byId("list-caption").textContent = clanScores.length + (clanScores.length === 1 ? " clan" : " clans");
        if (!clanScores.length) {
          statsList.appendChild(element("li", "empty", "No clans have been created yet."));
          return;
        }
        clanScores.forEach(function (entry, index) {
          var row = element("li", "mode-card");
          row.style.gridTemplateColumns = "45px minmax(0,1fr) auto";
          row.appendChild(element("div", "rank " + (index === 0 ? "" : index === 1 ? "silver" : index === 2 ? "bronze" : "normal"), "#" + (index + 1)));
          var clanArea = element("div", "mode-info");
          clanArea.appendChild(button(entry.clan.name + " [" + entry.clan.tag + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: entry.clan.tag }, "mode-title"));
          clanArea.appendChild(element("p", "source", "Founder: " + entry.clan.founder + " · " + (entry.clan.members || []).length + " members · " + entry.completions + " ranked modes completed"));
          row.appendChild(clanArea);
          row.appendChild(element("span", "chip points", entry.points + " pts"));
          statsList.appendChild(row);
        });
        return;
      }
      var scores = playerScores().filter(function (stat) {
        return state.countryRankingFilter === "all" || countryCodeForPlayer(stat.name) === state.countryRankingFilter;
      });
      byId("list-caption").textContent = scores.length + (scores.length === 1 ? " player" : " players");
      if (!scores.length) {
        statsList.appendChild(element("li", "empty", state.countryRankingFilter === "all" ? "No player scores have been recorded." : "No player scores are listed for " + countryName(state.countryRankingFilter) + "."));
        return;
      }
      scores.forEach(function (stat, index) {
        var row = element("li", "mode-card");
        row.style.gridTemplateColumns = "45px minmax(0,1fr) auto";
        row.appendChild(element("div", "rank " + (index === 0 ? "" : index === 1 ? "silver" : index === 2 ? "bronze" : "normal"), "#" + (index + 1)));
        var playerArea = element("div", "mode-info");
        var playerNameButton = button(stat.name, "open-player", { playerName: stat.name }, "mode-title");
        playerArea.appendChild(playerNameButton);
        appendPlayerCountryFlag(playerArea, stat.name);
        var clan = clanForPlayer(stat.name);
        if (clan) playerArea.appendChild(button("[" + clan.tag + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "chip clan-chip"));
        row.appendChild(playerArea);
        row.appendChild(element("span", "chip points", stat.points + " pts"));
        statsList.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    function renderListMode() {
      if (byId("workspace").classList.contains("dedicated-detail-view")) showEmptyPreview();
      if (inlineDetailHost && (list.contains(inlineDetailHost) || statsList.contains(inlineDetailHost))) showEmptyPreview();
      state.showingStats = false;
      list.classList.remove("hidden");
      statsList.classList.add("hidden");
      byId("player-comparison-wrap").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("clan-comparison-wrap").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("player-ranking-filter").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("leaderboard-filter-wrap").classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("mobile-filter-toggle").classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("stats-switch").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("stats-button").textContent = "Player stats";
      byId("list-heading").textContent = "Leaderboard";
      renderLeaderboard();
    }
    function renderStatsMode() {
      if (byId("workspace").classList.contains("dedicated-detail-view")) showEmptyPreview();
      if (inlineDetailHost && (list.contains(inlineDetailHost) || statsList.contains(inlineDetailHost))) showEmptyPreview();
      state.showingStats = true;
      list.classList.add("hidden");
      statsList.classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("leaderboard-filter-wrap").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("mobile-filter-toggle").classList.add("hidden");
      byId("stats-switch").classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("stats-button").textContent = "Player stats";
      renderStats();
    }
    function renderModeDetails(id, anchor) {
      var mode = modeById(id);
      if (!mode) { showEmptyPreview("That mode could not be found."); return; }
      var currentBoard = leaderboardConfig();
      var homeBoard = primaryLeaderboardForMode(mode);
      var activeRank = leaderboardRank(mode, currentBoard.key);
      var homeRank = leaderboardRank(mode, homeBoard.key);
      var positionLabel;
      if (activeRank) {
        positionLabel = currentBoard.label + " position #" + activeRank;
      } else {
        positionLabel = homeBoard.label + " position #" + (homeRank || "—") + " · not on " + currentBoard.label;
      }
      var box = showPreview(mode.title || "Untitled mode", positionLabel, anchor, true);
      box.classList.add("mode-detail-page");
      addDetailShareLink(box, "mode", mode.id);
      var imgUrl = safeUrl(mode.direct_image_url || mode.directImageUrl);
      if (imgUrl) {
        var video = modeVideoEmbed(mode.watch_url || mode.watchUrl);
        if (video) {
          var launch = button("", "play-mode-video", { modeId: mode.id }, "mode-video-launch");
          launch.setAttribute("aria-label", "Play verification video for " + (mode.title || "mode"));
          var image = document.createElement("img");
          image.src = imgUrl;
          image.alt = "Play verification video for " + (mode.title || "mode");
          image.loading = "lazy";
          launch.appendChild(image);
          launch.appendChild(element("span", "video-play-indicator", "Play video"));
          box.appendChild(launch);
        } else {
          var image = document.createElement("img");
          image.className = "detail-image";
          image.src = imgUrl;
          image.alt = "Mode thumbnail";
          box.appendChild(image);
        }
      }
      var row = element("div", "detail-row");
      var pointsBoard = modeBelongsToLeaderboard(mode, currentBoard.key) ? currentBoard : homeBoard;
      row.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", modePoints(mode, pointsBoard.key) + " points on " + pointsBoard.label));
      var enjoymentSummary = state.modeEnjoymentAverages[text(mode.id)];
      var averageEnjoyment = modeAverageEnjoyment(mode);
      var enjoymentLabel = averageEnjoyment === null
        ? "Average enjoyment: Not rated yet"
        : "Average enjoyment: " + averageEnjoyment.toFixed(1) + "/10 · " + enjoymentSummary.count + (enjoymentSummary.count === 1 ? " rating" : " ratings");
      row.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", enjoymentLabel));
      row.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Mode ID: " + text(mode.id)));
      row.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Challenge type: " + modeChallengeLabel(mode)));
      row.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Playable on: " + modePlayableLabel(mode)));
      box.appendChild(row);
      if (state.user && isStaff()) {
        var modeControls = actionRow();
        modeControls.appendChild(button("Edit", "edit-mode", { modeId: mode.id }));
        modeControls.appendChild(button("Delete", "delete-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "danger"));
        box.appendChild(modeControls);
      }
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Skill requirements"));
      var skillBreakdown = element("div", "skill-breakdown");
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
        var percentage = skillPercentage(mode, skill.key);
        skillBreakdown.appendChild(element("div", "detail-box", skill.label + ": " + (percentage === null ? "Not set" : percentage + "%")));
      });
      box.appendChild(skillBreakdown);
      var verifierLine = element("p", "");
      verifierLine.appendChild(element("strong", "", "Verifier: "));
      verifierLine.appendChild(element("span", mode.verifier ? "verifier-shine" : "", mode.verifier || "Not listed"));
      box.appendChild(verifierLine);
      var creatorName = text(mode.creator || mode.author).trim();
      if (creatorName) addParagraph(box, "Creator", creatorName);
      var sourceParagraph = element("p", "");
      sourceParagraph.appendChild(element("strong", "", mode.source ? "Source: " : "Source"));
      var sourceUrl = safeUrl(mode.source_url || mode.sourceUrl);
      if (sourceUrl) {
        var sourceName = element("a", "mode-source-link", mode.source || "Open source");
        sourceName.href = sourceUrl;
        sourceName.target = "_blank";
        sourceName.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
        sourceParagraph.appendChild(sourceName);
      } else {
        sourceParagraph.appendChild(document.createTextNode(mode.source || "Not listed"));
      }
      box.appendChild(sourceParagraph);
      addParagraph(box, "Description", mode.description || "No description provided.");
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Verified players"));
      var names = [text(mode.verifier).trim()].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
      names = names.filter(function (name, index, array) {
        return text(name).trim() && lower(name) !== "unknown" && array.findIndex(function (other) { return lower(other) === lower(name); }) === index;
      });
      if (!names.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No verified players listed yet."));
      else {
        var ul = element("ul", "detail-list");
        names.forEach(function (name) {
          var li = document.createElement("li");
          li.appendChild(button(name, "open-player", { playerName: name }, "link-button"));
          appendPlayerCountryFlag(li, name);
          var clan = clanForPlayer(name);
          if (clan) li.appendChild(button(" [" + clan.tag + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "link-button"));
          var completionVideoLink = openExternal(completionVideoFor(mode, name), "Video", "completion-video-link");
          if (completionVideoLink) li.appendChild(completionVideoLink);
          var enjoymentRating = modeEnjoymentRatingFor(mode, name);
          if (enjoymentRating) li.appendChild(element("span", "chip", enjoymentRating.enjoyment + "/10 · " + enjoymentRatingDescription(enjoymentRating.enjoyment)));
          ul.appendChild(li);
        });
        box.appendChild(ul);
      }
    }
    function renderProfileCustomization(parent, username, completions, profile) {
      var section = element("section", "profile-customization");
      section.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Profile customization"));
      if (!state.profileCustomizationSetupAvailable) {
        section.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Run supabase-full-repair.sql in Supabase to enable profile settings, then reload the site."));
        parent.appendChild(section);
        return;
      }

      var bioLabel = element("label", "field", "Short bio (up to 160 characters)");
      bioLabel.htmlFor = "profile-bio";
      var bio = document.createElement("textarea");
      bio.id = "profile-bio";
      bio.maxLength = 160;
      bio.rows = 3;
      bio.value = profile.bio;
      bio.placeholder = "A short message about you…";
      bioLabel.appendChild(bio);
      section.appendChild(bioLabel);
      section.appendChild(element("p", "profile-bio-count", text(profile.bio.length) + "/160"));

      var countryLabel = element("label", "field", "Country displayed on your profile");
      countryLabel.htmlFor = "profile-country";
      var countrySelect = document.createElement("select");
      countrySelect.id = "profile-country";
      countrySelect.appendChild(new Option("Choose a country", ""));
      COUNTRY_CODES.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return countryName(a).localeCompare(countryName(b), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (code) {
        countrySelect.appendChild(new Option(countryName(code), code));
      });
      countrySelect.value = profile.country_code || "";
      countryLabel.appendChild(countrySelect);
      section.appendChild(countryLabel);
      var flagOption = element("label", "profile-section-toggle", "Show my flag next to my name in profiles and lists");
      var flagCheckbox = document.createElement("input");
      flagCheckbox.type = "checkbox";
      flagCheckbox.id = "profile-show-country-flag";
      flagCheckbox.checked = profile.show_country_flag;
      flagOption.insertBefore(flagCheckbox, flagOption.firstChild);
      section.appendChild(flagOption);
      section.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Your country is public and is used for country rankings; this setting hides only the flag."));

      section.appendChild(createCustomColorField("Profile accent color", "profile-accent-theme", "profile-accent-picker", profile.accent_theme));
      section.appendChild(createCustomColorField("Profile background color", "profile-background-theme", "profile-background-picker", profile.background_theme));

      var featuredLabel = element("label", "field", "Featured completion");
      featuredLabel.htmlFor = "profile-featured-completion";
      var featuredSelect = document.createElement("select");
      featuredSelect.id = "profile-featured-completion";
      var noFeatured = document.createElement("option");
      noFeatured.value = "";
      noFeatured.textContent = "No featured completion";
      featuredSelect.appendChild(noFeatured);
      completions.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return (a.rank || Number(a.mode.placement) || 0) - (b.rank || Number(b.mode.placement) || 0)
          || text(a.mode.title).localeCompare(text(b.mode.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (item) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = text(item.mode.id);
        option.textContent = item.mode.title + " — #" + item.rank;
        featuredSelect.appendChild(option);
      });
      if (profile.featured_mode_id != null && Array.from(featuredSelect.options).some(function (option) { return modeIdEquals(option.value, profile.featured_mode_id); })) {
        featuredSelect.value = text(profile.featured_mode_id);
      }
      featuredLabel.appendChild(featuredSelect);
      section.appendChild(featuredLabel);

      section.appendChild(button("Save profile", "save-profile-customization", { playerName: username }, "success"));
      parent.appendChild(section);
    }
    async function renderPlayerPointsGraph(parent, username, requestNumber) {
      var board = leaderboardConfig();
      var ranked = rankedModesForLeaderboard(board.key);
      var completed = ranked.filter(function (mode) { return modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, username); });
      var timelineByMode = Object.create(null);
      var timelineAvailable = false;
      if (state.db) {
        try {
          var timelineResponse = await state.db.rpc("get_public_player_completion_timeline", { p_runner_name: username });
          if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
          if (!timelineResponse.error) {
            var timelineData = timelineResponse.data;
            if (typeof timelineData === "string") timelineData = JSON.parse(timelineData);
            if (timelineData && timelineData.ok && Array.isArray(timelineData.completions)) {
              timelineAvailable = true;
              timelineData.completions.forEach(function (completion) {
                var acceptedAt = completion && completion.accepted_at ? Date.parse(completion.accepted_at) : NaN;
                var completionDate = completion && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text(completion.completion_date))
                  ? Date.parse(text(completion.completion_date) + "T00:00:00") : NaN;
                if (completion && completion.mode_id != null) {
                  timelineByMode[text(completion.mode_id)] = { completionDate: completionDate, acceptedAt: acceptedAt };
                }
              });
            }
          }
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      completed.sort(function (modeA, modeB) {
        var timelineA = timelineByMode[text(modeA.id)] || {};
        var timelineB = timelineByMode[text(modeB.id)] || {};
        var orderA = Number.isFinite(timelineA.completionDate) ? timelineA.completionDate : timelineA.acceptedAt;
        var orderB = Number.isFinite(timelineB.completionDate) ? timelineB.completionDate : timelineB.acceptedAt;
        var knownA = Number.isFinite(orderA);
        var knownB = Number.isFinite(orderB);
        if (knownA && knownB && orderA !== orderB) return orderA - orderB;
        if (knownA !== knownB) return knownA ? -1 : 1;
        return leaderboardRank(modeA, board.key) - leaderboardRank(modeB, board.key);
      });
      var packRewards = (state.includePackPoints ? state.packCompletions : []).filter(function (reward) {
        return lower(reward.player_username) === lower(username) && Number(reward[board.key + "_points"]) > 0;
      }).map(function (reward) {
        return { isPack: true, title: "Pack completion · " + reward.pack_name, added: Number(reward[board.key + "_points"]), earnedAt: Date.parse(reward.completed_at || "") };
      });
      var pointEvents = completed.map(function (mode) {
        var timeline = timelineByMode[text(mode.id)] || {};
        return { mode: mode, title: mode.title, added: modePoints(mode, board.key), timeline: timeline,
          orderTime: Number.isFinite(timeline.completionDate) ? timeline.completionDate : timeline.acceptedAt,
          fallbackRank: leaderboardRank(mode, board.key) || 0 };
      }).concat(packRewards.map(function (reward) {
        return { isPack: true, title: reward.title, added: reward.added, earnedAt: reward.earnedAt, orderTime: reward.earnedAt, fallbackRank: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER };
      }));
      pointEvents.sort(function (a, b) {
        var knownA = Number.isFinite(a.orderTime);
        var knownB = Number.isFinite(b.orderTime);
        if (knownA && knownB && a.orderTime !== b.orderTime) return a.orderTime - b.orderTime;
        if (knownA !== knownB) return knownA ? -1 : 1;
        return a.fallbackRank - b.fallbackRank;
      });
      var totalPoints = pointEvents.reduce(function (total, item) { return total + item.added; }, 0);
      var section = element("section", "player-points-graph");
      var header = element("div", "player-points-graph-header");
      header.appendChild(element("div", "", "Points progression"));
      header.appendChild(element("span", "chip points", totalPoints.toLocaleString() + " pts"));
      section.appendChild(header);
      var datedCount = completed.filter(function (mode) {
        var entry = timelineByMode[text(mode.id)] || {};
        return Number.isFinite(entry.completionDate) || Number.isFinite(entry.acceptedAt);
      }).length;
      var caption = timelineAvailable
        ? "Cumulative " + board.label + " points, including pack bonuses and ordered by completion date." + (datedCount < completed.length ? " Older entries without a completion or approval date follow, ordered by placement." : "")
        : "Completion dates could not be loaded; showing modes by placement and pack bonuses when earned.";
      section.appendChild(element("p", "muted player-points-graph-caption", caption));
      if (!pointEvents.length) {
        section.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "No ranked completions to graph yet."));
        parent.appendChild(section);
        return;
      }

      var svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
      svg.setAttribute("class", "player-points-chart");
      svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 720 260");
      svg.setAttribute("role", "group");
      svg.setAttribute("aria-label", "Cumulative points from " + completed.length + " completed modes and " + packRewards.length + " pack bonuses on the " + board.label + ".");
      var svgNode = function (tag, attributes, content) {
        var node = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", tag);
        Object.keys(attributes || {}).forEach(function (key) { node.setAttribute(key, attributes[key]); });
        if (content != null) node.textContent = text(content);
        svg.appendChild(node);
        return node;
      };
      var title = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "title");
      title.textContent = username + " cumulative " + board.label + " points";
      svg.appendChild(title);

      var left = 58;
      var right = 18;
      var top = 16;
      var plotHeight = 184;
      var plotWidth = 720 - left - right;
      var baseY = top + plotHeight;
      var eventCount = pointEvents.length;
      var maxOrder = Math.max(1, eventCount);
      var maxPoints = Math.max(1, totalPoints);
      [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(function (fraction) {
        var y = baseY - plotHeight * fraction;
        svgNode("line", { x1: left, y1: y, x2: left + plotWidth, y2: y, class: "player-points-gridline" });
        svgNode("text", { x: left - 9, y: y + 4, "text-anchor": "end", class: "player-points-axis-label" }, Math.round(maxPoints * fraction).toLocaleString());
      });
      var samples = [{ order: 0, points: 0, added: 0 }];
      var runningTotal = 0;
      pointEvents.forEach(function (item, index) {
        runningTotal += item.added;
        samples.push({
          order: index + 1,
          points: runningTotal,
          added: item.added,
          title: item.title,
          isPack: Boolean(item.isPack),
          earnedAt: item.earnedAt,
          timeline: item.timeline || {}
        });
      });
      var coordinates = samples.map(function (sample) {
        return {
          x: left + (Number(sample.order) / maxOrder) * plotWidth,
          y: baseY - (Number(sample.points) / maxPoints) * plotHeight
        };
      });
      var path = coordinates.map(function (point, index) { return (index ? "L" : "M") + point.x.toFixed(1) + " " + point.y.toFixed(1); }).join(" ");
      var areaPath = path + " L " + coordinates[coordinates.length - 1].x.toFixed(1) + " " + baseY + " L " + coordinates[0].x.toFixed(1) + " " + baseY + " Z";
      svgNode("path", { d: areaPath, class: "player-points-area" });
      svgNode("path", { d: path, class: "player-points-line" });
      var tooltip = element("div", "player-points-tooltip");
      tooltip.hidden = true;
      tooltip.setAttribute("role", "status");
      var positionTooltip = function (circle) {
        var pointRect = circle.getBoundingClientRect();
        var graphRect = section.getBoundingClientRect();
        var tooltipWidth = tooltip.offsetWidth || 180;
        tooltip.style.left = Math.max(0, Math.min(graphRect.width - tooltipWidth, pointRect.left + pointRect.width / 2 - graphRect.left - tooltipWidth / 2)) + "px";
        tooltip.style.top = Math.max(0, pointRect.top - graphRect.top - (tooltip.offsetHeight || 44) - 8) + "px";
      };
      samples.slice(1).forEach(function (sample, index) {
        var point = coordinates[index + 1];
        var completionEntry = sample.timeline || {};
        var shownCompletionDate = sample.isPack
          ? (Number.isFinite(sample.earnedAt) ? new Date(sample.earnedAt).toLocaleDateString() : "unavailable")
          : (Number.isFinite(completionEntry.completionDate)
            ? new Date(completionEntry.completionDate).toLocaleDateString()
            : (Number.isFinite(completionEntry.acceptedAt) ? "unavailable · approved " + new Date(completionEntry.acceptedAt).toLocaleDateString() : "unavailable"));
        var dateLabel = sample.isPack ? "pack award date " : "completion date ";
        var detail = "+" + sample.added.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + " pts · " + sample.points.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + " total · " + dateLabel + shownCompletionDate;
        var circle = svgNode("circle", {
          cx: point.x,
          cy: point.y,
          r: 4,
          class: "player-points-dot",
          tabindex: "0",
          role: "img",
          "aria-label": sample.title + ": +" + sample.added.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + " points. " + detail
        });
        var pointTitle = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "title");
        pointTitle.textContent = sample.title + " · +" + sample.added.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + " pts · " + dateLabel + shownCompletionDate;
        circle.appendChild(pointTitle);
        var showTooltip = function () {
          tooltip.replaceChildren(element("strong", "", sample.title), element("span", "", detail));
          tooltip.hidden = false;
          positionTooltip(circle);
        };
        circle.addEventListener("pointerenter", showTooltip);
        circle.addEventListener("pointermove", function () { positionTooltip(circle); });
        circle.addEventListener("pointerleave", function () { tooltip.hidden = true; });
        circle.addEventListener("focus", showTooltip);
        circle.addEventListener("blur", function () { tooltip.hidden = true; });
      });
      [0, Math.round(eventCount / 2), eventCount].filter(function (order, index, labels) { return labels.indexOf(order) === index; }).forEach(function (order) {
        var x = left + (order / maxOrder) * plotWidth;
        svgNode("text", { x: x, y: baseY + 18, "text-anchor": "middle", class: "player-points-axis-label" }, String(order));
      });
      svgNode("text", { x: 360, y: 247, "text-anchor": "middle", class: "player-points-axis-title" }, "Completions and pack bonuses · chronological");
      var yTitle = svgNode("text", { x: 14, y: 108, "text-anchor": "middle", class: "player-points-axis-title", transform: "rotate(-90 14 108)" }, "Cumulative points");
      yTitle.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
      section.appendChild(svg);
      section.appendChild(tooltip);
      parent.appendChild(section);
    }
    async function renderPlayerProfile(username, anchor) {
      var requestNumber = ++state.profileRequest;
      var clickedNameText = anchor && typeof anchor.querySelector === "function" ? anchor.querySelector(".player-name-text") : null;
      var clickedBadgeKey = badgeKeyFromPlayerButton(anchor);
      var clickedGradientSnapshot = snapshotPlayerNameGradient(clickedNameText);
      var scores = playerScores();
      var score = scores.find(function (entry) { return lower(entry.name) === lower(username); });
      var position = score ? scores.indexOf(score) + 1 : null;
      var account = null;
      if (state.db) {
        var result = await state.db.from("accounts").select("username,is_banned,role").eq("username", lower(username)).maybeSingle();
        if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
        if (!result.error) account = result.data;
      }
      if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
      var completions = [];
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        if (modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, username)) completions.push({ mode: mode, rank: modeRank(mode) });
      });
      var enjoymentByMode = Object.create(null);
      if (state.db) {
        try {
          var enjoymentResult = await state.db.from("player_mode_enjoyments").select("mode_id,enjoyment").eq("player_username", lower(username));
          if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
          if (!enjoymentResult.error) (enjoymentResult.data || []).forEach(function (rating) {
            if (rating.enjoyment == null) return;
            var ratingValue = Number(rating.enjoyment);
            if (Number.isInteger(ratingValue) && ratingValue >= 0 && ratingValue <= 10) enjoymentByMode[text(rating.mode_id)] = ratingValue;
          });
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
      var playerEnjoyment = beatenModeEnjoymentSummary(completions);
      var isOwnProfile = Boolean(state.user && lower(state.user.username) === lower(username));
      var profile = playerProfileCustomization(username);
      state.staffSubmissionHistory = [];
      state.staffSubmissionHistoryError = "";
      if (state.user && isStaff() && state.db && state.accountPassword) {
        try {
          var staffHistory = parseSubmissionResponse(await state.db.rpc("get_staff_player_submission_history", {
            p_staff_username: state.user.username,
            p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
            p_runner_name: username
          }), "Could not load staff submission history");
          if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
          state.staffSubmissionHistory = Array.isArray(staffHistory.submissions) ? staffHistory.submissions : [];
        } catch (historyError) {
          if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
          state.staffSubmissionHistory = [];
