          state.staffSubmissionHistoryError = text(historyError.message || historyError);
        }
      }
      var badgeInfo = playerBadge(username);
      if (clickedBadgeKey && (!badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge || badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge === "none")) {
        badgeInfo = Object.assign({}, badgeInfo, { equipped_name_badge: clickedBadgeKey });
      }
      if (isOwnProfile && state.db && state.accountPassword) {
        try {
          var badgeResponse = parseNameBadgeResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_name_badge", {
            p_username: state.user.username,
            p_password: state.accountPassword,
            p_badge: null
          }), "Could not load completion badges");
          state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable = true;
          badgeInfo = {
            equipped_name_badge: badgeResponse.equipped_name_badge || "none",
            platinum_unlocked: Boolean(badgeResponse.platinum_unlocked)
          };
          state.accountBadges[lower(username)] = badgeInfo;
          refreshVisiblePlayerBadge(username);
        } catch (_) {
          state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable = false;
        }
      }
      if (requestNumber !== state.profileRequest) return;
      var clan = clanForPlayer(username);
      var box = showPreview(username, "Player profile", anchor, true);
      addDetailShareLink(box, "player", username);
      box.classList.add("player-profile");
      box.style.setProperty("--profile-accent", profile.accent_theme);
      box.style.setProperty("--profile-background", profile.background_theme);
      box.style.setProperty("--profile-text-color", readableTextColor(profile.background_theme));
      var playerTitle = box.querySelector(".preview-title");
      if (playerTitle) {
        playerTitle.replaceChildren();
        var playerTitleName = clickedNameText ? clickedNameText.cloneNode(true) : element("span", "player-name-text", username);
        playerTitleName.textContent = username;
        playerTitleName.classList.add("player-name-text");
        var titleBadgeKey = isOwnProfile
          ? badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge
          : (clickedBadgeKey || badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge);
        var clonedGradient = NAME_BADGES.some(function (badge) { return playerTitleName.classList.contains(badge.className); });
        if (titleBadgeKey) decoratePlayerName(playerTitleName, username, titleBadgeKey);
        else if (!clonedGradient) decoratePlayerName(playerTitleName, username, badgeInfo.equipped_name_badge);
        if (!isOwnProfile) applyPlayerNameGradientSnapshot(playerTitleName, clickedGradientSnapshot);
        playerTitle.appendChild(playerTitleName);
      }
      if (clan) {
        if (playerTitle) {
          playerTitle.appendChild(document.createTextNode(" "));
          playerTitle.appendChild(button("[" + clan.tag + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "chip clan-chip"));
        }
      }
      if (profile.country_code) {
        var countryLine = element("p", "country-profile-line");
        countryLine.appendChild(element("strong", "", "Country: "));
        if (profile.show_country_flag) {
          var profileFlag = countryFlagButton(profile.country_code);
          if (profileFlag) countryLine.appendChild(profileFlag);
          countryLine.appendChild(document.createTextNode(" "));
        }
        var profileCountryName = countryNameButton(profile.country_code, false, "country-name-button");
        if (profileCountryName) countryLine.appendChild(profileCountryName);
        box.appendChild(countryLine);
      }
      if (profile.bio.trim()) box.appendChild(element("p", "profile-bio", profile.bio.trim()));
      {
        var header = element("div", "detail-row");
        var activeBoard = leaderboardConfig();
        header.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", activeBoard.label + " rank: " + (position ? "#" + position : "Unranked")));
        header.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", activeBoard.label + " score: " + (score ? score.points : 0) + " pts"));
        header.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Beaten modes’ average enjoyment: " + (playerEnjoyment.average === null ? "No rated modes yet" : playerEnjoyment.average.toFixed(1) + "/10 · " + playerEnjoyment.count + (playerEnjoyment.count === 1 ? " rated mode" : " rated modes"))));
        box.appendChild(header);
      }
      await renderPlayerPointsGraph(box, username, requestNumber);
      if (profile.featured_mode_id != null) {
        var featured = completions.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.mode.id, profile.featured_mode_id); });
        if (featured) {
          box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Featured completion"));
          var featuredCard = element("div", "queue-card profile-featured");
          featuredCard.appendChild(button(featured.mode.title, "open-mode", { modeId: featured.mode.id }, "mode-title"));
          featuredCard.appendChild(element("p", "source", "Leaderboard position #" + featured.rank));
          var featuredVideo = openExternal(completionVideoFor(featured.mode, username), "Watch completion", "completion-video-link");
          if (featuredVideo) featuredCard.appendChild(featuredVideo);
          box.appendChild(featuredCard);
        }
      }
      if (account && account.is_banned) box.appendChild(element("p", "banned", "This account is banned."));
      if (isOwnProfile) renderCompletionBadges(box, username, completions, badgeInfo, isOwnProfile);
      renderPlayerRecommendations(box, username, completions);
      {
        var verifiedModes = state.modes.filter(function (mode) {
          return lower(mode.verifier) === lower(username);
        }).slice().sort(function (a, b) {
          var rankA = modeRank(a);
          var rankB = modeRank(b);
          if (rankA !== null && rankB === null) return -1;
          if (rankB !== null && rankA === null) return 1;
          if (rankA !== null && rankB !== null && rankA !== rankB) return rankA - rankB;
          return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
        });
        box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Modes verified"));
        if (!verifiedModes.length) {
          box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No modes are currently assigned to this player as verifier."));
        } else {
          var verifiedList = element("ul", "detail-list");
          verifiedModes.forEach(function (mode) {
            var verifiedItem = document.createElement("li");
            var verifiedLabel = text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode";
            verifiedLabel += " — " + modePositionLabel(mode);
            var verifiedRow = element("div", "verified-mode-row");
            verifiedRow.appendChild(button(verifiedLabel, "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "link-button"));
            if (state.user && isStaff()) verifiedRow.appendChild(button("Edit mode", "edit-mode", { modeId: mode.id }));
            verifiedItem.appendChild(verifiedRow);
            verifiedList.appendChild(verifiedItem);
          });
          box.appendChild(verifiedList);
        }
      }
      {
        box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Verified completions"));
        if (state.user && isStaff() && state.staffSubmissionHistoryError) {
          box.appendChild(element("p", "inline-message visible", "Staff edit records could not be loaded: " + state.staffSubmissionHistoryError + ". Run the latest combined Supabase setup, then reload this profile."));
        }
        if (!completions.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No verified completions recorded."));
        else {
          var ul = element("ul", "detail-list");
          completions.forEach(function (item) {
            var li = document.createElement("li");
            li.appendChild(button(item.mode.title + " — " + modePositionLabel(item.mode), "open-mode", { modeId: item.mode.id }, "link-button"));
            var completionEnjoyment = enjoymentByMode[text(item.mode.id)];
            var completionEnjoymentDescription = enjoymentRatingDescription(completionEnjoyment);
            if (completionEnjoymentDescription) {
              li.appendChild(element("span", "chip", "Enjoyment: " + completionEnjoyment + "/10 · " + completionEnjoymentDescription));
            }
            var videoLink = openExternal(completionVideoFor(item.mode, username), "Video", "completion-video-link");
            if (videoLink) li.appendChild(videoLink);
            if (state.user && isStaff()) {
              var acceptedSubmission = state.staffSubmissionHistory.find(function (submission) {
                return lower(submission.status) === "approved" &&
                  lower(submission.runner_name) === lower(username) &&
                  modeIdEquals(submission.target_mode_id, item.mode.id);
              });
              if (acceptedSubmission) {
                li.appendChild(button("Edit submission", "edit-submission", {
                  submissionId: acceptedSubmission.id,
                  modeId: item.mode.id,
                  playerName: username
                }));
              } else if (!state.staffSubmissionHistoryError) {
                li.appendChild(element("span", "muted", "Editable record missing. Run the updated combined Supabase setup once; future verifier completions will be linked automatically."));
              }
            }
            ul.appendChild(li);
          });
          box.appendChild(ul);
        }
      }
      if (isOwnProfile) renderProfileCustomization(box, username, completions, profile);
      if (state.user && isStaff()) {
        box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Staff moderation"));
        var accountRole = lower(account && account.role);
        var targetIsStaff = accountRole === "staff" || accountRole === "admin";
        var targetIsOwner = lower(username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME;
        if (account && !targetIsOwner && (!targetIsStaff || isListOwner())) {
          box.appendChild(button(account.is_banned ? "Unban account" : "Ban account", "toggle-ban", { playerName: username, banned: account.is_banned ? "true" : "false" }, account.is_banned ? "success" : "danger"));
        } else if (account && targetIsStaff && !isListOwner()) {
          box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Only Mintex can change staff account access."));
        }
        var select = document.createElement("select");
        select.id = "staff-add-mode";
        state.modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }); }).forEach(function (mode) {
          var option = document.createElement("option");
          option.value = text(mode.id);
          option.textContent = text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode";
          select.appendChild(option);
        });
        if (!state.modes.length) select.disabled = true;
        box.appendChild(select);
        box.appendChild(button("Add completion", "staff-add-completion", { playerName: username }, "success"));
        box.appendChild(button("Remove all completions", "purge-completions", { playerName: username }, "danger"));
        completions.forEach(function (item) {
          box.appendChild(button("Remove from " + item.mode.title, "staff-remove-completion", { playerName: username, modeId: item.mode.id }, "danger"));
        });
      }
    }
    function renderClanDirectory(results, query) {
      results.replaceChildren();
      var needle = lower(query);
      var clans = Object.keys(state.clans).map(function (key) { return state.clans[key]; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }) || text(a.tag).localeCompare(text(b.tag)); })
        .filter(function (clan) {
          return !needle || [clan.name, clan.tag, clan.founder, clan.description]
            .some(function (value) { return lower(value).indexOf(needle) >= 0; });
        });
      byId("clan-browser-count").textContent = clans.length + (clans.length === 1 ? " clan" : " clans");
      if (!clans.length) {
        results.appendChild(element("p", "empty", needle ? "No clans match your search." : "No clans have been created yet."));
        return;
      }
      clans.forEach(function (clan) {
        var card = element("article", "clan-directory-card");
        var top = element("div", "clan-directory-top");
        top.appendChild(button(text(clan.name) + " [" + text(clan.tag) + "]", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "link-button clan-directory-name"));
        top.appendChild(element("span", "chip", clan.is_open === false ? "Closed · not joinable" : "Open · joinable"));
        card.appendChild(top);
        card.appendChild(element("p", "clan-directory-description", text(clan.description).trim() || "No description added yet."));
        card.appendChild(element("p", "clan-directory-meta", "Founder: " + text(clan.founder) + " · " + (Array.isArray(clan.members) ? clan.members.length : 0) + " members"));
        var actions = element("div", "clan-directory-actions");
        actions.appendChild(button("View clan", "open-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }));
        if (clan.is_open !== false && !currentClan()) {
          actions.appendChild(state.user
            ? button("Join", "join-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "gold-button")
            : button("Sign in to join", "auth-action", {}, "gold-button"));
        }
        card.appendChild(actions);
        results.appendChild(card);
      });
    }
    function renderClanHub() {
      var content = byId("clan-hub-content");
      if (inlineDetailHost && content.contains(inlineDetailHost)) showEmptyPreview();
      content.replaceChildren();
      var clan = currentClan();
      var ownSection = element("section", "");
      ownSection.appendChild(element("h3", "", "Your clan"));
      if (!state.user) {
        ownSection.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Sign in to create or join a clan. You can browse every clan below."));
      } else if (!clan) {
        ownSection.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "You are not in a clan yet. Create one here or browse existing clans below."));
        var form = document.createElement("form");
        form.id = "create-clan-form";
        var grid = element("div", "form-grid");
        var nameWrap = element("div", "field");
        nameWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Clan name"));
        var name = document.createElement("input");
        name.id = "clan-name";
        name.name = "name";
        name.required = true;
        name.maxLength = 60;
        name.placeholder = "Legends Elite";
        nameWrap.appendChild(name);
        var tagWrap = element("div", "field");
        tagWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Clan tag (up to 5 characters)"));
        var tag = document.createElement("input");
        tag.id = "clan-tag";
        tag.name = "tag";
        tag.required = true;
        tag.maxLength = 5;
        tag.pattern = "[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,5}";
        tag.placeholder = "LGD";
        tagWrap.appendChild(tag);
        var accessWrap = element("div", "field");
        accessWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Who can join?"));
        var access = document.createElement("select");
        access.id = "clan-open-status";
        access.name = "isOpen";
        access.appendChild(new Option("Open — anyone can join", "true"));
        access.appendChild(new Option("Closed — not joinable", "false"));
        accessWrap.appendChild(access);
        grid.appendChild(nameWrap);
        grid.appendChild(tagWrap);
        grid.appendChild(accessWrap);
        form.appendChild(grid);
        var descriptionWrap = element("div", "field");
        descriptionWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Clan description (optional)"));
        var description = document.createElement("textarea");
        description.name = "description";
        description.rows = 3;
        description.maxLength = 500;
        description.placeholder = "Tell people what your clan is about.";
        descriptionWrap.appendChild(description);
        form.appendChild(descriptionWrap);
        form.appendChild(button("Create clan", "create-clan-submit", {}, "gold-button"));
        ownSection.appendChild(form);
      } else {
        var isFounder = lower(clan.founder) === lower(state.user.username);
        ownSection.appendChild(element("h4", "subheading", clan.name + " [" + clan.tag + "]"));
        addParagraph(ownSection, "Founder", clan.founder);
        ownSection.appendChild(element("p", "muted", clan.is_open === false ? "Status: Closed · not joinable" : "Status: Open · anyone can join"));
        ownSection.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "Members: " + (clan.members || []).length));
        if (isFounder) {
          var descriptionWrap = element("div", "field");
          descriptionWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Clan description"));
          var description = document.createElement("textarea");
          description.id = "clan-description-edit";
          description.rows = 3;
          description.maxLength = 500;
          description.value = text(clan.description);
          description.placeholder = "Tell people what your clan is about.";
          descriptionWrap.appendChild(description);
          ownSection.appendChild(descriptionWrap);
          ownSection.appendChild(button("Save description", "save-clan-description", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "success"));
        } else {
          ownSection.appendChild(element("p", "clan-directory-description", text(clan.description).trim() || "No description added yet."));
        }
        renderClanGoals(ownSection, clan);
        ownSection.appendChild(element("h4", "subheading", "Roster"));
        var roster = element("div", "");
        (clan.members || []).forEach(function (member) {
          var row = element("div", "roster-row");
          row.appendChild(button(member, "open-player", { playerName: member }, "link-button"));
          appendPlayerCountryFlag(row, member);
          if (isFounder && lower(member) !== lower(state.user.username)) {
            row.appendChild(button("Remove", "kick-member", { clanTag: clan.tag, memberName: member }, "danger"));
          }
          roster.appendChild(row);
        });
        ownSection.appendChild(roster);
        if (isFounder) {
          ownSection.appendChild(button(clan.is_open === false ? "Open clan to new members" : "Close clan to new members", "toggle-clan-open", { clanTag: clan.tag, renderTarget: "hub" }, clan.is_open === false ? "gold-button" : ""));
        }
        ownSection.appendChild(button(isFounder ? "Disband clan" : "Leave clan", "leave-clan", {}, "danger"));
      }
      content.appendChild(ownSection);

      var directory = element("section", "clan-section");
      var directoryHeading = element("div", "panel-heading");
      directoryHeading.appendChild(element("h3", "", "Browse clans"));
      directoryHeading.appendChild(element("span", "heading-note", "All clans"));
      directory.appendChild(directoryHeading);
      var searchWrap = element("div", "field clan-directory-search");
      searchWrap.appendChild(element("label", "", "Search by name, tag, founder, or description"));
      var search = document.createElement("input");
      search.id = "clan-browser-search";
      search.type = "search";
      search.placeholder = "Search clans…";
      search.autocomplete = "off";
      searchWrap.appendChild(search);
      directory.appendChild(searchWrap);
      var count = element("p", "muted", "0 clans");
      count.id = "clan-browser-count";
      directory.appendChild(count);
      var results = element("div", "clan-directory-list");
      results.id = "clan-browser-results";
      directory.appendChild(results);
      content.appendChild(directory);
      renderClanDirectory(results, "");
    }
    function renderClanProfile(tag, anchor) {
      var clan = state.clans[lower(tag)];
      if (!clan) { setNotice("That clan could not be found.", "error"); return; }
      var box = showPreview(clan.name + " [" + clan.tag + "]", "Clan profile", anchor, true);
      box.classList.add("clan-profile-page");
      addDetailShareLink(box, "clan", clan.tag);
      var members = Array.isArray(clan.members) ? clan.members : [];
      var memberKeys = Object.create(null);
      members.forEach(function (member) { memberKeys[lower(member)] = true; });
      var score = 0;
      var completions = [];
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        var names = [mode.verifier].concat(Array.isArray(mode.victors) ? mode.victors : []);
        var contributors = names.filter(function (name, pos, all) {
          return memberKeys[lower(name)] && all.findIndex(function (other) { return lower(other) === lower(name); }) === pos;
        });
        if (contributors.length) {
          score += modePoints(mode);
          completions.push({ mode: mode, rank: modeRank(mode), names: contributors });
        }
      });
      var summary = element("div", "detail-row");
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Founder: " + clan.founder));
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Members: " + members.length));
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", leaderboardConfig().label + " team score: " + score + " pts"));
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", clan.is_open === false ? "Closed · not joinable" : "Open · joinable"));
      box.appendChild(summary);
      addParagraph(box, "Description", text(clan.description).trim() || "No description added yet.");
      renderClanGoals(box, clan);
      if (state.user && lower(clan.founder) === lower(state.user.username)) {
        box.appendChild(button(clan.is_open === false ? "Open clan to new members" : "Close clan to new members", "toggle-clan-open", { clanTag: clan.tag, renderTarget: "profile" }, clan.is_open === false ? "gold-button" : ""));
      } else if (state.user && !currentClan() && clan.is_open !== false) {
        box.appendChild(button("Join this clan", "join-clan", { clanTag: clan.tag }, "gold-button"));
      }
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Roster"));
      var roster = element("ul", "detail-list");
      members.forEach(function (member) {
        var li = document.createElement("li");
        li.appendChild(button(member + (lower(member) === lower(clan.founder) ? " (Founder)" : ""), "open-player", { playerName: member }, "link-button"));
        appendPlayerCountryFlag(li, member);
        roster.appendChild(li);
      });
      box.appendChild(roster);
      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Clan completions"));
      if (!completions.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No verified team completions yet."));
      else {
        var ul = element("ul", "detail-list");
        completions.forEach(function (item) {
          var li = document.createElement("li");
          li.appendChild(button(item.mode.title + " — cleared by " + item.names.join(", "), "open-mode", { modeId: item.mode.id }, "link-button"));
          ul.appendChild(li);
        });
        box.appendChild(ul);
      }
    }
    function renderCountryProfile(rawCode, anchor) {
      var code = normalizeCountryCode(rawCode);
      if (!code) { setNotice("That country could not be found.", "error"); return; }
      var entry = countryLeaderboardScores().find(function (item) { return item.code === code; });
      if (!entry) { setNotice("No players have selected " + countryName(code) + " yet.", "error"); return; }
      var box = showPreview(countryName(code), "Country profile", anchor, true);
      box.classList.add("country-profile-page");
      addDetailShareLink(box, "country", code);
      var title = box.querySelector(".preview-title");
      if (title) {
        title.replaceChildren();
        title.appendChild(countryFlagButton(code));
        title.appendChild(document.createTextNode(" " + countryName(code)));
      }
      var summary = element("div", "detail-row");
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Members: " + entry.members.length));
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", leaderboardConfig().label + " score: " + entry.points + " pts"));
      summary.appendChild(element("span", "detail-box", "Ranked modes completed: " + entry.completions));
      box.appendChild(summary);

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Country members"));
      if (!entry.members.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No members have selected this country yet."));
      else {
        var scoreByPlayer = Object.create(null);
        playerScores().forEach(function (player) { scoreByPlayer[lower(player.name)] = player; });
        var members = entry.members.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
          var scoreA = scoreByPlayer[lower(a)] ? scoreByPlayer[lower(a)].points : 0;
          var scoreB = scoreByPlayer[lower(b)] ? scoreByPlayer[lower(b)].points : 0;
          return scoreB - scoreA || text(a).localeCompare(text(b), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
        });
        var roster = element("ul", "detail-list");
        members.forEach(function (member) {
          var li = document.createElement("li");
          li.appendChild(button(member, "open-player", { playerName: member }, "link-button"));
          appendPlayerCountryFlag(li, member);
          var player = scoreByPlayer[lower(member)];
          li.appendChild(element("span", "muted", " · " + (player ? player.points : 0) + " pts"));
          roster.appendChild(li);
        });
        box.appendChild(roster);
      }

      box.appendChild(element("h3", "subheading", "Country completions"));
      if (!entry.clearedModes.length) box.appendChild(element("p", "muted", "No verified completions are recorded for this country yet."));
      else {
        var completions = element("ul", "detail-list");
        entry.clearedModes.forEach(function (item) {
          var li = document.createElement("li");
          li.appendChild(button(item.mode.title + " — " + modePositionLabel(item.mode), "open-mode", { modeId: item.mode.id }, "link-button"));
          var contributors = element("div", "country-contributors");
          item.names.forEach(function (name, index) {
            if (index) contributors.appendChild(document.createTextNode(", "));
            contributors.appendChild(button(name, "open-player", { playerName: name }, "link-button"));
            appendPlayerCountryFlag(contributors, name);
          });
          li.appendChild(contributors);
          completions.appendChild(li);
        });
        box.appendChild(completions);
      }
    }
    function renderQueue() {
      var root = byId("queue-list");
      root.replaceChildren();
      byId("queue-count").textContent = text(state.queue.length);
      if (state.queueError) { root.appendChild(element("p", "empty", state.queueError)); return; }
      if (!state.queue.length) { root.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "The verification queue is empty.")); return; }
      state.queue.forEach(function (run) {
        var card = element("article", "queue-card");
        var mode = modeById(run.target_mode_id || run.targetModeId);
        addParagraph(card, "Runner", run.runner_name || run.runnerName);
        addParagraph(card, "Mode", (mode ? mode.title : "Unknown mode") + " (ID " + text(run.target_mode_id || run.targetModeId) + ")");
        addParagraph(card, "Completion date", run.completion_date || "Not entered");
        var link = openExternal(run.video_url || run.videoUrl, "Open submitted completion video");
        if (link) card.appendChild(link);
        var rawFootageLink = openExternal(run.raw_footage_url || run.rawFootageUrl, "Open raw footage (staff only)");
        if (rawFootageLink) card.appendChild(rawFootageLink);
        addParagraph(card, "Review material", rawFootageLink ? "Raw footage is available above." : "No raw footage was supplied; review the submitted completion video.");
        var actions = actionRow();
        actions.appendChild(button("Edit submission", "edit-submission", { submissionId: run.id }));
        actions.appendChild(button("Approve", "approve-run", { queueId: run.id }, "success"));
        actions.appendChild(button("Reject", "reject-run", { queueId: run.id }, "danger"));
        card.appendChild(actions);
        root.appendChild(card);
      });
    }
    function populateSubmissionModeSelect(selectedModeId) {
      var select = byId("edit-submission-mode");
      select.replaceChildren();
      state.modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (mode) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = text(mode.id);
        option.textContent = (text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode") + " — " + (text(mode.source).trim() || "Source not listed");
        select.appendChild(option);
      });
      if (selectedModeId != null) select.value = text(selectedModeId);
      if (!select.value && select.options.length) select.selectedIndex = 0;
      select.disabled = !select.options.length;
    }
    function openSubmissionEditor(id, playerName, modeId) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var run = id ? (state.queue.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, id); }) ||
        state.staffSubmissionHistory.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, id); })) : null;
      if (!run && playerName && modeId != null) {
        run = state.staffSubmissionHistory.find(function (item) {
          return lower(item.status) === "approved" && lower(item.runner_name) === lower(playerName) &&
            modeIdEquals(item.target_mode_id, modeId);
        });
      }
      if (!run || (playerName && lower(run.runner_name) !== lower(playerName))) {
        throw new Error("This completion needs its editable record in Supabase. Run the updated supabase-full-repair.sql once, then reload the profile.");
      }
      var form = byId("edit-submission-form");
      form.elements.namedItem("submissionId").value = text(run.id);
      populateSubmissionModeSelect(run.target_mode_id || run.targetModeId);
      form.elements.namedItem("videoUrl").value = text(run.video_url || run.videoUrl);
      form.elements.namedItem("completionDate").value = text(run.completion_date || run.completionDate);
      form.elements.namedItem("rawFootageUrl").value = text(run.raw_footage_url || run.rawFootageUrl);
      form.elements.namedItem("enjoyment").value = run.enjoyment == null ? "" : text(run.enjoyment);
      state.editSubmissionProfile = playerName || "";
      byId("edit-submission-title").textContent = "Edit " + lower(run.status || "pending") + " submission";
      setSubmissionEditMessage("", false);
      showModal(editSubmissionModal);
    }
    function renderAll() {
      renderAuth();
      if (state.showingStats) renderStatsMode();
      else renderListMode();
      renderQueue();
      populateModeSelect();
      if (isListOwner()) {
        renderPackManagement();
        renderBadgeManagement();
      }
      if (document.body.dataset.sitePage === "packs") renderPacksPage();
    }
    function mountOwnerPackTools() {
      var packContent = byId("pack-management-content");
      var badgeContent = byId("badge-management-content");
      var packTarget = byId("staff-packs-content");
      var badgeTarget = byId("staff-badges-content");
      if (packContent && packTarget) packTarget.appendChild(packContent);
      if (badgeContent && badgeTarget) badgeTarget.appendChild(badgeContent);
    }
    function populateModeSelect() {
      var select = byId("submit-mode");
      select.replaceChildren();
      if (!state.modes.length) {
        var emptyOption = document.createElement("option");
        emptyOption.value = "";
        emptyOption.textContent = "No modes available";
        select.appendChild(emptyOption);
        select.disabled = true;
        return;
      }
      select.disabled = false;
      state.modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }); }).forEach(function (mode) {
        var option = document.createElement("option");
        option.value = text(mode.id);
        var title = text(mode.title).trim() || "Untitled mode";
        var source = text(mode.source).trim() || "Source not listed";
        option.textContent = title + " — " + source;
        select.appendChild(option);
      });
    }
    async function renderStaffTab(tab) {
      var ownerTabs = ["permissions", "packs", "badges"];
      var selected = tab === "queue" ? "queue" : (ownerTabs.indexOf(tab) >= 0 && isListOwner() ? tab : "modes");
      byId("staff-mode-tab").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "modes");
      byId("staff-queue-tab").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "queue");
      byId("staff-packs-tab").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "packs");
      byId("staff-badges-tab").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "badges");
      byId("staff-permissions-tab").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "permissions");
      document.querySelectorAll("#staff-panel .tab").forEach(function (node) {
        node.classList.toggle("active", node.dataset.tab === selected);
      });
      if (selected === "permissions") await loadStaffAccounts();
      else if (selected === "packs") renderPackManagement();
      else if (selected === "badges") renderBadgeManagement();
    }
    async function loadStaffAccounts() {
      if (!isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can manage list staff.");
      var root = byId("staff-account-list");
      root.replaceChildren(element("p", "muted", "Loading accounts…"));
      var response = await state.db.from("accounts").select("username,role,is_banned").order("username", { ascending: true });
      if (response.error) throw new Error("Could not load accounts: " + friendlyDatabaseError(response.error));
      state.staffAccounts = response.data || [];
      renderStaffAccounts();
    }
    function renderStaffAccounts() {
      var root = byId("staff-account-list");
      root.replaceChildren();
      var searchField = byId("staff-account-search");
      var needle = lower(searchField && searchField.value);
      var accounts = state.staffAccounts.filter(function (account) {
        return !needle || lower(account.username).indexOf(needle) >= 0;
      });
      if (!accounts.length) {
        root.appendChild(element("p", "empty", needle ? "No accounts match your search." : "No accounts were found."));
        return;
      }
      accounts.forEach(function (account) {
        var username = text(account.username).trim();
        var isOwner = lower(username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME;
        var role = lower(account.role);
        var hasStaffAccess = role === "staff" || role === "admin";
        var isBanned = Boolean(account.is_banned);
        var row = element("div", "queue-card staff-account-row");
        var details = element("div", "staff-account-details");
        details.appendChild(element("strong", "", username));
        details.appendChild(element("span", "chip staff-account-role", isOwner ? "Owner" : (hasStaffAccess ? "Staff" : "Player")));
        if (!isOwner) details.appendChild(element("span", "chip", isBanned ? "Banned" : "Active"));
        row.appendChild(details);
        if (!isOwner) {
          var actions = actionRow();
          actions.appendChild(button(
              hasStaffAccess ? "Remove staff" : "Grant staff",
              "set-staff-role",
              { playerName: username, makeStaff: hasStaffAccess ? "false" : "true" },
              hasStaffAccess ? "danger" : "success"
            ));
          actions.appendChild(button(isBanned ? "Unban account" : "Ban account", "owner-toggle-ban", {
            playerName: username,
            banned: isBanned ? "true" : "false"
          }, isBanned ? "success" : ""));
          actions.appendChild(button("Delete account", "delete-player-account", { playerName: username }, "danger"));
          row.appendChild(actions);
        }
        root.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    async function loadLibrary() {
      if (window.supabase && typeof window.supabase.createClient === "function") return;
      var urls = [
        "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2",
        "https://unpkg.com/@supabase/supabase-js@2"
      ];
      var lastError = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < urls.length; i++) {
        try {
          await new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
            var script = document.createElement("script");
            var timer = window.setTimeout(function () { reject(new Error("Timed out loading Supabase client")); }, 12000);
            script.src = urls[i];
            script.async = true;
            script.onload = function () { window.clearTimeout(timer); resolve(); };
            script.onerror = function () { window.clearTimeout(timer); reject(new Error("Could not load Supabase client")); };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
          });
          if (window.supabase && typeof window.supabase.createClient === "function") return;
        } catch (error) { lastError = error; }
      }
      throw lastError || new Error("Supabase client did not load.");
    }
    function packModesFor(packId) {
      return state.packModesById[text(packId)] || [];
    }
    function estimatedPackPoints(pack, listKey) {
      var items = packModesFor(pack.pack_id);
      if (!items.length) return 0;
      return Math.ceil(items.reduce(function (sum, item) {
        var mode = modeById(item.mode_id);
        return sum + (mode ? modePoints(mode, listKey) : 0);
      }, 0) / items.length);
    }
    function renderPacksPage() {
      var root = byId("packs-list");
      if (!root) return;
      root.replaceChildren();
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable && !state.packs.length) {
        root.appendChild(element("p", "empty", state.packSetupError || "Packs are not set up yet. The owner needs to run the updated combined Supabase setup."));
        return;
      }
      if (state.packSetupError) root.appendChild(element("p", "inline-message visible", "Pack reward update: " + state.packSetupError));
      if (state.packProgressError) root.appendChild(element("p", "inline-message visible", "Pack progress could not be refreshed: " + state.packProgressError + ". Saved packs and gradients are still shown."));
      if (!state.packs.length) root.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "No packs have been created yet."));
      state.packs.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (pack) {
        var items = packModesFor(pack.pack_id);
        var card = element("article", "pack-card");
        var heading = element("div", "pack-card-heading");
        heading.appendChild(element("h3", "", pack.name));
        var rewardBadge = pack.reward_badge_id && state.customBadgesById[text(pack.reward_badge_id)];
        if (rewardBadge) heading.appendChild(customGradientText(rewardBadge));
        else heading.appendChild(element("span", "muted", "No name gradient reward"));
        card.appendChild(heading);
        if (text(pack.description).trim()) card.appendChild(element("p", "muted", pack.description));
        var completedCount = state.user ? items.filter(function (item) {
          var mode = modeById(item.mode_id);
          return mode && modeBeatenByPlayer(mode, state.user.username);
        }).length : 0;
        card.appendChild(element("p", "pack-progress", state.user
          ? completedCount + " of " + items.length + " modes completed"
          : items.length + " modes · Sign in to see your progress"));
        var award = state.user && state.packCompletions.find(function (item) {
          return item.pack_id && text(item.pack_id) === text(pack.pack_id) && lower(item.player_username) === lower(state.user.username);
        });
        var points = award ? Number(award[state.activeLeaderboard + "_points"]) : estimatedPackPoints(pack, state.activeLeaderboard);
        var pointMessage = (award ? "Earned pack bonus: " : "Pack bonus when complete: ")
          + (Number.isFinite(points) ? points.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) : "0") + " pts · " + leaderboardConfig().label;
        if (award && award.badge_id && state.customBadgesById[text(award.badge_id)]) pointMessage += " · Name gradient unlocked";
        card.appendChild(element("p", "muted pack-progress", pointMessage));
        var modeList = element("div", "pack-mode-list");
        items.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return Number(a.position) - Number(b.position); }).forEach(function (item) {
          var mode = modeById(item.mode_id);
          if (mode) modeList.appendChild(button(mode.title, "open-mode", { modeId: mode.id }, "mode-title"));
        });
        card.appendChild(modeList);
        root.appendChild(card);
      });
    }
    function renderPackManagement() {
      var note = byId("staff-packs-setup-note");
      var packForm = byId("pack-form");
      var packList = byId("owner-pack-list");
      if (!note || !packForm || !packList) return;
      note.textContent = !state.packSetupAvailable
        ? (state.packSetupError || "The saved pack list could not be refreshed; existing data is being kept on screen.")
        : (state.packSetupError || state.packProgressError || "");
      packForm.classList.toggle("hidden", !state.packSetupAvailable);
      packList.classList.remove("hidden");
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable) {
        if (!packList.children.length) packList.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "The saved pack list could not be loaded. Retry when Supabase is available."));
        return;
      }
      var badgeSelect = byId("pack-reward-badge");
      var selectedBadge = badgeSelect.value;
      badgeSelect.replaceChildren(new Option("No name gradient reward", ""));
      state.customBadges.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (badge) { badgeSelect.appendChild(new Option(badge.name, text(badge.badge_id))); });
      if (Array.from(badgeSelect.options).some(function (option) { return option.value === selectedBadge; })) badgeSelect.value = selectedBadge;

      var modeSelect = byId("pack-mode-select");
      var selectedModes = Array.from(modeSelect.selectedOptions).map(function (option) { return option.value; });
      modeSelect.replaceChildren();
      state.modes.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.title).localeCompare(text(b.title), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (mode) {
        var option = new Option(text(mode.title) + " · " + modePositionLabel(mode), text(mode.id));
        option.selected = selectedModes.indexOf(option.value) >= 0;
        modeSelect.appendChild(option);
      });
      packList.replaceChildren();
      if (!state.packs.length) packList.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "No packs have been created yet."));
      state.packs.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (pack) {
        var row = element("div", "pack-card-heading");
        row.appendChild(element("strong", "", pack.name));
        row.appendChild(element("span", "muted", packModesFor(pack.pack_id).length + " modes"));
        row.appendChild(button("Edit", "edit-pack", { packId: pack.pack_id }));
        row.appendChild(button("Delete", "delete-pack", { packId: pack.pack_id }, "danger"));
        packList.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    function renderBadgeManagement() {
      var note = byId("staff-badges-setup-note");
      var badgeForm = byId("custom-badge-form");
      var badgeList = byId("custom-badge-list");
      if (!note || !badgeForm || !badgeList) return;
      note.textContent = !state.packSetupAvailable
        ? (state.packSetupError || "The saved gradient list could not be refreshed; existing data is being kept on screen.")
        : (state.packSetupError || state.packProgressError || "");
      badgeForm.classList.toggle("hidden", !state.packSetupAvailable);
      badgeList.classList.remove("hidden");
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable) {
        if (!badgeList.children.length) badgeList.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "The saved gradient list could not be loaded. Retry when Supabase is available."));
        return;
      }
      badgeList.replaceChildren();
      if (!state.customBadges.length) badgeList.appendChild(element("p", "empty", "No custom badges yet."));
      state.customBadges.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        return text(a.name).localeCompare(text(b.name), undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });
      }).forEach(function (badge) {
        var row = element("div", "pack-card-heading");
        row.appendChild(customGradientText(badge));
        if (text(badge.description).trim()) row.appendChild(element("span", "muted", badge.description));
        row.appendChild(button("Edit", "edit-custom-badge", { badgeId: badge.badge_id }));
        row.appendChild(button("Delete", "delete-custom-badge", { badgeId: badge.badge_id }, "danger"));
        badgeList.appendChild(row);
      });
      updateCustomBadgePreview();
    }
    function updateCustomBadgePreview() {
      var previewNode = byId("custom-badge-preview");
      var form = byId("custom-badge-form");
      if (!previewNode || !form) return;
      var value = function (name, fallback) { return form.elements.namedItem(name).value || fallback; };
      var badge = {
        name: value("name", "Name gradient preview"),
        gradient_start: value("gradientStart", "#4f46e5"),
        gradient_mid: value("gradientMid", "#a855f7"),
        gradient_end: value("gradientEnd", "#ec4899"),
        text_color: value("textColor", "#ffffff"),
        border_color: value("borderColor", "#ffffff"),
        glow_color: value("glowColor", "#a855f7"),
        gradient_angle: value("gradientAngle", "110")
      };
      previewNode.replaceChildren(customGradientText(badge));
    }
    async function loadPackData() {
      state.packSetupError = "";
      state.packProgressError = "";
      var results = await Promise.all([
        state.db.from("packs").select("pack_id,name,description,reward_badge_id,created_at").order("name", { ascending: true }),
        state.db.from("pack_modes").select("pack_id,mode_id,position").order("position", { ascending: true }),
        state.db.from("custom_badges").select("badge_id,name,description,gradient_start,gradient_mid,gradient_end,gradient_angle,text_color,border_color,glow_color").order("name", { ascending: true })
      ]);
      var failed = results.find(function (result) { return result.error; });
      state.packSetupAvailable = !failed;
      state.packSetupError = failed ? "Packs need the latest combined Supabase setup: " + friendlyDatabaseError(failed.error) : "";
      if (!results[0].error) state.packs = results[0].data || [];
      if (!results[2].error) {
        state.customBadges = results[2].data || [];
        state.customBadgesById = Object.create(null);
        state.customBadges.forEach(function (badge) {
          state.customBadgesById[text(badge.badge_id)] = badge;
          state.customBadgesById[text(badge.badge_id).toLowerCase()] = badge;
        });
      }
      if (!results[1].error) {
        state.packModesById = Object.create(null);
        (results[1].data || []).forEach(function (item) {
        var key = text(item.pack_id);
        if (!state.packModesById[key]) state.packModesById[key] = [];
        state.packModesById[key].push(item);
        });
      }
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable) return;
      if (state.user && state.accountPassword) {
        try {
          parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("claim_completed_pack_badges", {
            p_username: state.user.username,
            p_password: state.accountPassword
          }), "Could not apply pack rewards");
        } catch (error) {
          state.packSetupError = text(error.message || error);
        }
      }
      var completionResult = await state.db.from("player_pack_completions").select("completion_id,player_username,pack_id,pack_name,badge_id,main_points,extended_points,unlimited_points,completed_at");
      if (completionResult.error) {
        state.packProgressAvailable = false;
        state.packProgressError = friendlyDatabaseError(completionResult.error);
      } else {
        state.packProgressAvailable = true;
        state.packProgressError = "";
        state.packCompletions = completionResult.data || [];
      }
    }
    async function saveCustomBadge(form) {
      if (!isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can manage pack badges.");
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable) throw new Error(state.packSetupError || "Run the updated combined Supabase setup first.");
      var field = function (name) { return form.elements.namedItem(name).value; };
      var existingId = field("badgeId");
      var draft = {
        badge_id: existingId,
        name: field("name").trim(),
        description: field("description").trim(),
        gradient_start: normalizeHexColor(field("gradientStart")),
        gradient_mid: normalizeHexColor(field("gradientMid")),
        gradient_end: normalizeHexColor(field("gradientEnd")),
        gradient_angle: Number(field("gradientAngle")),
        text_color: normalizeHexColor(field("textColor")),
        border_color: normalizeHexColor(field("borderColor")),
        glow_color: normalizeHexColor(field("glowColor"))
      };
      var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_custom_badge", {
        p_owner_username: state.user.username,
        p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: existingId ? "update" : "create",
        p_badge_id: existingId || null,
        p_name: draft.name,
        p_description: draft.description,
        p_gradient_start: draft.gradient_start,
        p_gradient_mid: draft.gradient_mid,
        p_gradient_end: draft.gradient_end,
        p_gradient_angle: draft.gradient_angle,
        p_text_color: draft.text_color,
        p_border_color: draft.border_color,
        p_glow_color: draft.glow_color
      });
      var saved = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not save name gradient");
      draft.badge_id = text(saved.badge_id || existingId);
      var badgeIndex = state.customBadges.findIndex(function (badge) { return text(badge.badge_id) === draft.badge_id; });
      if (badgeIndex >= 0) state.customBadges[badgeIndex] = draft;
      else state.customBadges.push(draft);
      state.customBadgesById[draft.badge_id] = draft;
      state.customBadgesById[draft.badge_id.toLowerCase()] = draft;
      form.reset();
      renderBadgeManagement();
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Name gradient saved.", "success");
    }
    async function savePack(form) {
      if (!isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can manage packs.");
      if (!state.packSetupAvailable) throw new Error(state.packSetupError || "Run the updated combined Supabase setup first.");
      var field = function (name) { return form.elements.namedItem(name).value; };
      var existingId = field("packId");
      var selected = Array.from(byId("pack-mode-select").selectedOptions).map(function (option) { return option.value; });
      if (!selected.length) throw new Error("Choose at least one mode for this pack.");
      var response = await state.db.rpc("manage_pack", {
        p_owner_username: state.user.username,
        p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: existingId ? "update" : "create",
        p_pack_id: existingId || null,
        p_name: field("name").trim(),
        p_description: field("description").trim(),
        p_reward_badge_id: field("rewardBadgeId") || null,
        p_mode_ids: selected
      });
      var saved = parseManagementResponse(response, "Could not save pack");
      var packId = text(saved.pack_id || existingId);
      var priorPack = state.packs.find(function (pack) { return text(pack.pack_id) === packId; });
      var savedPack = {
        pack_id: packId,
        name: field("name").trim(),
        description: field("description").trim(),
        reward_badge_id: field("rewardBadgeId") || null,
        created_at: priorPack ? priorPack.created_at : new Date().toISOString()
      };
      var packIndex = state.packs.findIndex(function (pack) { return text(pack.pack_id) === packId; });
      if (packIndex >= 0) state.packs[packIndex] = savedPack;
      else state.packs.push(savedPack);
      state.packModesById[packId] = selected.map(function (modeId, index) {
        return { pack_id: packId, mode_id: modeId, position: index + 1 };
      });
      form.reset();
      renderPackManagement();
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Pack saved.", "success");
    }
    async function loadData() {
      var results = await Promise.all([
        state.db.from("modes").select("*").order("placement", { ascending: true }).order("id", { ascending: true }),
        state.db.from("clans").select("*").order("tag", { ascending: true }),
        state.db.from("player_mode_enjoyments").select("player_username,mode_id,enjoyment")
      ]);
      state.modes = must(results[0], "Could not load modes") || [];
      var clanRows = must(results[1], "Could not load clans") || [];
      state.clans = Object.create(null);
      clanRows.forEach(function (clan) { state.clans[lower(clan.tag)] = clan; });
      state.modeEnjoymentAverages = Object.create(null);
      state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode = Object.create(null);
      (results[2].error ? [] : (results[2].data || [])).forEach(function (row) {
        var rating = Number(row.enjoyment);
        var username = text(row.player_username).trim();
        if (!username || !Number.isInteger(rating) || rating < 0 || rating > 10) return;
        var key = text(row.mode_id);
        if (!state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[key]) state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[key] = [];
        state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[key].push({ player_username: username, enjoyment: rating, is_verifier: false });
      });
      state.modes.forEach(function (mode) {
        var key = text(mode.id);
        var ratings = state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[key] || (state.modeEnjoymentRatingsByMode[key] = []);
        var verifier = text(mode.verifier).trim();
        var verifierRating = mode.verifier_enjoyment == null ? NaN : Number(mode.verifier_enjoyment);
        if (verifier && Number.isInteger(verifierRating) && verifierRating >= 0 && verifierRating <= 10) {
          var existingVerifierRating = ratings.find(function (item) { return lower(item.player_username) === lower(verifier); });
          if (existingVerifierRating) {
            existingVerifierRating.enjoyment = verifierRating;
            existingVerifierRating.is_verifier = true;
          } else ratings.push({ player_username: verifier, enjoyment: verifierRating, is_verifier: true });
        }
        ratings.forEach(function (item) {
          if (!state.modeEnjoymentAverages[key]) state.modeEnjoymentAverages[key] = { total: 0, count: 0 };
          state.modeEnjoymentAverages[key].total += item.enjoyment;
          state.modeEnjoymentAverages[key].count += 1;
        });
      });
      state.queue = [];
      state.queueError = "";
      if (state.user && isStaff()) {
        var queueResponse = await state.db.rpc("get_staff_submission_queue", {
          p_staff_username: state.user.username,
          p_staff_password: state.accountPassword
        });
        try {
          var queueData = parseManagementResponse(queueResponse, "Could not load the verification queue");
          state.queue = Array.isArray(queueData.queue) ? queueData.queue : [];
        } catch (queueError) {
          state.queueError = text(queueError.message || queueError);
        }
      }
      state.accountBadges = Object.create(null);
      var badgeResult = await state.db.from("accounts").select("username,equipped_name_badge,platinum_unlocked");
      state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable = !badgeResult.error;
      if (!badgeResult.error) (badgeResult.data || []).forEach(function (account) {
        state.accountBadges[lower(account.username)] = {
          equipped_name_badge: account.equipped_name_badge || "none",
          platinum_unlocked: Boolean(account.platinum_unlocked)
        };
      });
      var profileResult = await state.db.from("player_profiles").select("username,bio,accent_theme,background_theme,visible_sections,featured_mode_id,country_code,show_country_flag");
      state.profileCustomizationSetupAvailable = !profileResult.error;
      state.profileCustomizations = Object.create(null);
      if (!profileResult.error) (profileResult.data || []).forEach(function (profile) {
        state.profileCustomizations[lower(profile.username)] = profile;
      });
      try { await loadPackData(); }
      catch (packError) {
        state.packSetupAvailable = false;
        state.packSetupError = "Could not load packs: " + text(packError.message || packError);
      }
      await refreshInboxCount();
      renderAll();
    }
    async function start() {
      setNotice("Connecting to Supabase…", "loading");
      try {
        await loadLibrary();
        state.db = window.supabase.createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
        var restored = false;
        var restoreError = "";
        try { restored = await restoreRememberedLogin(); }
        catch (error) { restoreError = text(error.message || error); }
        await loadData();
        var appearanceError = "";
        if (restored) {
          try { await loadAccountSiteTheme(); }
          catch (error) { appearanceError = text(error.message || error); }
        }
        try { await restoreDetailFromUrl(); }
        catch (detailError) {
          console.error("Could not open the linked page:", detailError);
          setNotice("The site loaded, but this link could not be opened: " + text(detailError.message || detailError), "error");
        }
        if (restoreError) setNotice("Could not restore the saved sign-in: " + restoreError, "error");
        else if (appearanceError) setNotice("Signed in, but account appearance could not load: " + appearanceError, "error");
        else if (state.rememberedLoginIssue) setNotice(state.rememberedLoginIssue, "info");
        else setNotice(restored ? "Signed in as " + state.user.username + "." : "", restored ? "success" : "");
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Leaderboard startup failed:", error);
        setNotice("The leaderboard could not load: " + text(error.message || error), "error");
        list.replaceChildren(element("li", "empty", "Database connection failed. Check the Supabase URL, key, network, and table permissions."));
      }
    }
    async function restoreRememberedLogin() {
      state.rememberedLoginIssue = "";
      var tokens = await getRememberedTokens();
      if (!tokens.length) return false;
      var sawBannedAccount = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
        var token = tokens[i];
        var response = await state.db.rpc("restore_account_session", { p_session_token: token });
        if (response.error) {
          var detail = response.error.code === "PGRST202"
            ? "Run the current supabase-full-repair.sql setup in Supabase, then reload the page."
            : friendlyDatabaseError(response.error);
          throw new Error(detail);
        }
        var result = unwrapRpcData(response);
        if (result && result.ok) {
          state.user = { username: result.username, role: result.role || "player" };
          state.rememberToken = token;
          state.accountPassword = "session:" + token;
          state.rememberedLoginIssue = "";
          try { await saveRememberedToken(token); } catch (_) {}
          return true;
        }
        if (result && result.code === "banned") sawBannedAccount = true;
      }
      await clearRememberedToken();
      state.rememberedLoginIssue = sawBannedAccount
        ? "This account is blocked from signing in. Contact the list owner."
        : "The saved sign-in expired or was revoked. Please sign in again and select Keep me signed in.";
      return false;
    }
    function requireLogin() {
      if (state.user) return true;
      setNotice("Sign in first to use that feature.", "info");
      setAuthMessage("", false);
      authPanel.classList.remove("hidden");
      return false;
    }
    function requireStaff() {
      if (state.user && isStaff()) return true;
      setNotice("This action requires a staff account.", "error");
      return false;
    }
    function loginError(code, message) {
      var messages = {
        reserved_username: "That staff username is reserved. Ask the site owner to set up its account.",
        wrong_password: "That password does not match this username.",
        banned: "This account is blocked from signing in.",
        invalid_username: "Use 3–20 characters: letters, numbers, spaces, underscores, or hyphens.",
        invalid_password: "The password is empty or too long.",
        duplicate_username: "This username has duplicate account rows. Ask the site owner to resolve them."
      };
      if (code === "PGRST202") return "The Supabase setup is missing or out of date. Run supabase-full-repair.sql in Supabase.";
      return messages[code] || ("Sign-in failed: " + text(message || code || "Unknown error"));
    }
    async function submitAuth(form) {
      if (!state.db) throw new Error("The database is still connecting. Try again in a moment.");
      var username = form.elements.username.value.trim();
      var password = form.elements.password.value;
      var rememberLogin = Boolean(form.elements.remember && form.elements.remember.checked);
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_ -]{3,20}$/.test(username)) {
        setAuthMessage("Use 3–20 characters: letters, numbers, spaces, underscores, or hyphens.", false);
        form.elements.username.focus();
        return;
      }
      setAuthMessage("Checking account…", true);
      var response = await state.db.rpc("login_or_register_account", { p_username: username, p_password: password });
      if (response.error) throw new Error(loginError(response.error.code, response.error.message));
      var result = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data[0] : response.data;
      if (!result || typeof result !== "object") throw new Error("Supabase returned an invalid account response.");
      if (!result.ok) {
        setAuthMessage(loginError(result.code), false);
        return;
      }
      state.user = { username: result.username || lower(username), role: result.role || "player" };
      state.accountPassword = password;
      state.rememberToken = null;
      state.rememberedLoginIssue = "";
      var rememberWarning = "";
      var previousToken = await getRememberedToken();
      if (rememberLogin) {
        await clearRememberedToken();
        if (previousToken) {
          try { await state.db.rpc("revoke_account_session", { p_session_token: previousToken }); } catch (_) {}
        }
        try {
          var sessionResponse = await state.db.rpc("create_account_session", { p_username: username, p_password: password });
          var session = parseManagementResponse(sessionResponse, "Could not save remembered sign-in");
          if (!session.token || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(session.token)) throw new Error("Supabase returned an invalid session token.");
          try {
            await saveRememberedToken(session.token);
            state.rememberToken = session.token;
            state.accountPassword = "session:" + session.token;
          } catch (storageError) {
            await clearRememberedToken();
            try { await state.db.rpc("revoke_account_session", { p_session_token: session.token }); } catch (_) {}
            rememberWarning = "This browser blocked persistent storage, so this sign-in will last only until you close or reload the page.";
          }
        } catch (error) {
          rememberWarning = "This sign-in could not be saved on this device: " + text(error.message || error);
        }
      } else {
        await clearRememberedToken();
        if (previousToken) {
          try { await state.db.rpc("revoke_account_session", { p_session_token: previousToken }); } catch (_) {}
        }
      }
      form.reset();
      hideModal(authPanel);
      await loadData();
      var appearanceError = "";
      try { await loadAccountSiteTheme(); }
      catch (error) { appearanceError = text(error.message || error); }
      var noticeMessage = appearanceError
        ? "Signed in, but account appearance could not load: " + appearanceError
        : result.code === "registered" ? "Account created. Welcome, " + state.user.username + "." : "Signed in as " + state.user.username + ".";
      if (rememberWarning) noticeMessage += " " + rememberWarning;
      setNotice(noticeMessage, appearanceError || rememberWarning ? "error" : "success");
      await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard");
    }
    async function signOut() {
      await closeInbox();
      try { await state.displaySettingsSaveChain; } catch (_) {}
      var token = state.rememberToken || await getRememberedToken();
      await clearRememberedToken();
      if (token && state.db) {
        try { await state.db.rpc("revoke_account_session", { p_session_token: token }); }
        catch (error) { console.warn("Could not revoke remembered sign-in; local token was still cleared:", error); }
      }
      state.user = null;
      state.accountPassword = null;
      state.rememberToken = null;
      state.inboxCount = 0;
      state.inboxNotifications = [];
      state.inboxError = "";
      setSiteSettingsOpen(false);
      applySiteTheme("graphite");
      applyDisplaySettings(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, "Sign in to load and save your account settings.");
      state.queue = [];
      state.queueError = "";
      hideModal(submitModal);
      cancelRejectRun();
      hideModal(authPanel);
      closeClanHub(false);
      showEmptyPreview();
      renderAll();
      setNotice("Signed out.", "info");
    }
    async function refresh() { await loadData(); }
    function formValues(form) {
      var out = {};
      new FormData(form).forEach(function (value, key) { out[key] = text(value).trim(); });
      return out;
    }
    function requireHttpsField(value, label) {
      if (!safeUrl(value)) throw new Error(label + " must be a valid http or https URL.");
    }
    function requireCompletionDate(value) {
      var dateText = text(value).trim();
      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateText)) throw new Error("Enter the completion date shown on the video.");
      var parts = dateText.split("-").map(Number);
      var date = new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2]);
      var today = new Date();
      today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
      if (date.getFullYear() !== parts[0] || date.getMonth() !== parts[1] - 1 || date.getDate() !== parts[2]) {
        throw new Error("Enter a real calendar date.");
      }
      if (date > today) throw new Error("Completion date cannot be in the future.");
      return dateText;
    }
    function optionalCompletionDate(value) {
      return text(value).trim() === "" ? null : requireCompletionDate(value);
    }
    function nextId() { return Math.floor(Date.now() * 1000 + Math.random() * 1000); }
    async function submitRun(form) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (!state.db || !state.modes.length) throw new Error("There are no modes available for submission.");
      var values = formValues(form);
      requireHttpsField(values.videoUrl, "Video URL");
      var completionDate = requireCompletionDate(values.completionDate);
      if (values.rawFootageUrl) requireHttpsField(values.rawFootageUrl, "Raw footage URL");
      var enjoyment = values.enjoyment === "" ? null : Number(values.enjoyment);
      if (enjoyment !== null && (!Number.isInteger(enjoyment) || enjoyment < 0 || enjoyment > 10)) {
        throw new Error("Enjoyment must be a rating from 0 to 10, or left blank.");
      }
      var record = {
        id: nextId(),
        runner_name: state.user.username,
        target_mode_id: Number(values.modeId),
        video_url: values.videoUrl,
        completion_date: completionDate
      };
      parseSubmissionResponse(await state.db.rpc("submit_player_run", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_submission_id: record.id,
        p_target_mode_id: record.target_mode_id,
        p_video_url: record.video_url,
        p_completion_date: completionDate,
        p_raw_footage_url: values.rawFootageUrl || null,
        p_enjoyment: enjoyment
      }), "Could not submit the run");
      form.reset();
      hideModal(submitModal);
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Run sent to the staff verification queue.", "success");
    }
    function modeFormValues(form) {
      var values = formValues(form);
      requireHttpsField(values.sourceUrl, "Source URL");
      requireHttpsField(values.watchUrl, "Video URL");
      var imageField = form.elements.namedItem("image");
      var thumbnailUrl = resolveModeThumbnail(values.image, values.watchUrl, imageField);
      if (!thumbnailUrl) throw new Error("Add a thumbnail URL for this video host, or use a YouTube video link to fill it automatically.");
      requireHttpsField(thumbnailUrl, "Thumbnail URL");
      var placement = Number(values.placement);
      if (!Number.isInteger(placement) || placement < 1) throw new Error("Placement must be a positive whole number.");
      var skillValues = {};
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
        var raw = values[skill.key];
        if (raw === "") {
          skillValues[skill.key] = null;
          return;
        }
        var percentage = Number(raw);
        if (!Number.isInteger(percentage) || percentage < 0 || percentage > 100) {
          throw new Error(skill.label + " must be a whole number from 0 to 100, or left blank.");
        }
        skillValues[skill.key] = percentage;
      });
      var challengeType = lower(values.challengeType || "standard");
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(MODE_CHALLENGE_TYPES, challengeType)) throw new Error("Choose a valid challenge type.");
      var playableOn = lower(values.playableOn || "both");
      if (["mobile", "pc", "both"].indexOf(playableOn) < 0) throw new Error("Choose Mobile, PC, or both for the playable device.");
      var creator = text(values.creator).trim();
      if (creator.length > 160) throw new Error("The mod author or creator must be 160 characters or fewer.");
      var verifierEnjoyment = values.verifierEnjoyment === "" ? null : Number(values.verifierEnjoyment);
      if (verifierEnjoyment !== null && (!Number.isInteger(verifierEnjoyment) || verifierEnjoyment < 0 || verifierEnjoyment > 10)) {
        throw new Error("Verifier enjoyment must be a whole number from 0 to 10, or left blank.");
      }
      return Object.assign({
        title: values.title,
        placement: placement,
        challenge_type: challengeType,
        playable_on: playableOn,
        creator: creator || null,
        verifier_enjoyment: verifierEnjoyment,
        verifier: values.verifier,
        source: values.source,
        source_url: values.sourceUrl,
        watch_url: values.watchUrl,
        direct_image_url: thumbnailUrl,
        description: values.description,
        victors: []
      }, skillValues);
    }
    function orderedModes() {
      return rankedModes().sort(function (a, b) {
        return Number(a.placement) - Number(b.placement) || text(a.id).localeCompare(text(b.id));
      });
    }
    function boundedPlacement(value, max) {
      return Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(value), max));
    }
    async function manageLeaderboardMode(action, modeId, modeData) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return null;
      return parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_leaderboard_mode", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: action,
        p_mode_id: modeId == null ? null : Number(modeId),
        p_mode: modeData || {}
      }), action === "delete" ? "Could not delete mode" : action === "create" ? "Could not create mode" : "Could not save mode changes");
    }
    async function createMode(form) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var record = modeFormValues(form);
      var ordered = orderedModes();
      var position = boundedPlacement(record.placement, ordered.length + 1);
      record.placement = position;
      record.id = nextId();
      await manageLeaderboardMode("create", null, record);
      form.reset();
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Mode added at #" + position + ". Modes at that position and below moved down.", "success");
    }
    function openEditMode(id) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var mode = modeById(id);
      if (!mode) throw new Error("That mode no longer exists.");
      setEditMessage("", false);
      byId("edit-id").value = text(mode.id);
      byId("edit-title-input").value = text(mode.title);
      byId("edit-placement").value = text(mode.placement || modeRank(mode) || 1);
      byId("edit-challenge-type").value = modeChallengeType(mode);
      var playableOn = lower(mode.playable_on || mode.playableOn);
      byId("edit-playable-on").value = ["mobile", "pc", "both"].indexOf(playableOn) >= 0 ? playableOn : "both";
      byId("edit-verifier").value = text(mode.verifier);
      byId("edit-creator").value = text(mode.creator || mode.author);
      byId("edit-verifier-enjoyment").value = mode.verifier_enjoyment == null ? "" : text(mode.verifier_enjoyment);
      byId("edit-image").value = text(mode.direct_image_url || mode.directImageUrl);
      byId("edit-source").value = text(mode.source);
      byId("edit-source-url").value = text(mode.source_url || mode.sourceUrl);
      byId("edit-watch-url").value = text(mode.watch_url || mode.watchUrl);
      syncModeThumbnailFields(byId("edit-watch-url"), byId("edit-image"), true);
      byId("edit-description").value = text(mode.description);
      MODE_SKILLS.forEach(function (skill) {
        var value = skillPercentage(mode, skill.key);
        byId("edit-" + skill.key).value = value === null ? "" : String(value);
      });
      showModal(editModal);
    }
    async function saveEditMode(form) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      setEditMessage("Saving changes…", true);
      var values = modeFormValues(form);
      var id = byId("edit-id").value;
      delete values.victors;
      var mode = modeById(id);
      if (!mode) throw new Error("That mode no longer exists.");
      var position = values.placement;
      await manageLeaderboardMode("update", id, Object.assign({}, values, { id: Number(id), placement: position }));
      await refresh();
      hideModal(editModal);
      setNotice("Mode changes saved at #" + position + ". Modes were shifted to keep the ranking in order.", "success");
    }
    async function deleteMode(id) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var mode = modeById(id);
      if (!mode || !(await askConfirm("Delete “" + mode.title + "”? Its victor records for this mode will be removed.", "Delete mode", "Delete mode"))) return;
      await manageLeaderboardMode("delete", id, {});
      showEmptyPreview();
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Mode deleted.", "success");
    }
    async function managePlayerClan(action, tag, name, isOpen, memberName, description) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return null;
      return parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_clan", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: action,
        p_tag: text(tag).trim().toLowerCase() || null,
        p_name: text(name).trim() || null,
        p_description: text(description).trim() || null,
        p_is_open: typeof isOpen === "boolean" ? isOpen : null,
        p_member_name: text(memberName).trim() || null
      }), "Could not " + ({ create: "create clan", join: "join clan", leave: "update clan membership", toggle_open: "update clan access", kick: "update clan roster" }[action] || "update clan"));
    }
    async function createClan(form) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (currentClan()) throw new Error("You already belong to a clan. Leave it before founding another.");
      var values = formValues(form);
      var name = values.name.trim();
      var tag = values.tag.trim().toLowerCase();
      var description = text(values.description).trim();
      if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 60) throw new Error("Clan name must be between 2 and 60 characters.");
      if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,5}$/.test(values.tag.trim())) throw new Error("Clan tags can contain 1–5 letters, numbers, underscores, or hyphens.");
      if (description.length > 500) throw new Error("Clan descriptions can contain up to 500 characters.");
      await managePlayerClan("create", tag, name, values.isOpen !== "false", "", description);
      await refresh();
      byId("clan-panel").classList.remove("hidden");
      setNotice("Clan [" + tag + "] created.", "success");
    }
    async function saveClanDescription(tag) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      var clan = currentClan();
      if (!clan || lower(clan.tag) !== lower(tag) || lower(clan.founder) !== lower(state.user.username)) {
        throw new Error("Only the clan founder can change this description.");
      }
      var description = text(byId("clan-description-edit").value).trim();
      if (description.length > 500) throw new Error("Clan descriptions can contain up to 500 characters.");
      await managePlayerClan("update_description", clan.tag, "", null, "", description);
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Clan description saved.", "success");
    }
    async function leaveClan() {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      var clan = currentClan();
      if (!clan) { setNotice("You are not in a clan.", "info"); return; }
      if (lower(clan.founder) === lower(state.user.username)) {
        if (!(await askConfirm("You founded this clan. Disband it and remove its roster?", "Disband clan", "Disband clan"))) return;
        await managePlayerClan("leave", clan.tag);
      } else {
        if (!(await askConfirm("Leave clan [" + clan.tag + "]?", "Leave clan", "Leave clan"))) return;
        await managePlayerClan("leave", clan.tag);
      }
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Clan membership updated.", "success");
    }
    async function joinClan(tag) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (currentClan()) throw new Error("Leave your current clan before joining another.");
      var clan = state.clans[lower(tag)];
      if (!clan) throw new Error("That clan could not be found.");
      if (clan.is_open === false) throw new Error("This clan is closed and is not accepting new members.");
      await managePlayerClan("join", clan.tag);
      await refresh();
      renderClanProfile(tag);
      setNotice("You joined [" + clan.tag + "] " + clan.name + ".", "success");
    }
    async function toggleClanOpen(tag, renderTarget) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      var clan = state.clans[lower(tag)];
      if (!clan || lower(clan.founder) !== lower(state.user.username)) throw new Error("Only the clan founder can change join access.");
      var isOpen = clan.is_open === false;
      var result = await managePlayerClan("toggle_open", clan.tag, "", isOpen);
      clan.is_open = Boolean(result.is_open);
      renderClanHub();
      if (renderTarget === "profile") renderClanProfile(clan.tag);
