      setNotice("Clan [" + clan.tag + "] is now " + (clan.is_open ? "open to new members." : "closed to new members."), "success");
    }
    async function kickMember(tag, memberName) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      var clan = state.clans[lower(tag)];
      if (!clan || lower(clan.founder) !== lower(state.user.username)) throw new Error("Only the clan founder can remove members.");
      if (lower(memberName) === lower(clan.founder)) throw new Error("The founder cannot remove themself from the roster.");
      if (!(await askConfirm("Remove " + memberName + " from [" + clan.tag + "]?", "Remove clan member", "Remove member"))) return;
      await managePlayerClan("kick", clan.tag, "", null, memberName);
      await refresh();
      renderClanHub();
      if (state.preview && state.preview.clanTag) renderClanProfile(state.preview.clanTag);
      setNotice(memberName + " removed from the clan.", "success");
    }
    async function approveRun(id) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var run = state.queue.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, id); });
      if (!run) throw new Error("That queue item no longer exists.");
      parseSubmissionResponse(await state.db.rpc("review_player_submission", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_submission_id: run.id,
        p_status: "approved",
        p_rejection_reason: null,
        p_rejection_note: null
      }), "Could not approve run");
      await refresh();
      var mode = modeById(run.target_mode_id || run.targetModeId);
      setNotice("Run approved and added to " + (mode ? mode.title : "the leaderboard") + ".", "success");
    }
    async function saveSubmissionEdits(form) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var values = formValues(form);
      if (values.videoUrl) requireHttpsField(values.videoUrl, "Completion video URL");
      var completionDate = optionalCompletionDate(values.completionDate);
      if (values.rawFootageUrl) requireHttpsField(values.rawFootageUrl, "Raw footage URL");
      if (!values.modeId || !modeById(values.modeId)) throw new Error("Choose a mode that is still on the leaderboard.");
      var enjoyment = values.enjoyment === "" ? null : Number(values.enjoyment);
      if (enjoyment !== null && (!Number.isInteger(enjoyment) || enjoyment < 0 || enjoyment > 10)) {
        throw new Error("Enjoyment must be a rating from 0 to 10, or left blank.");
      }
      parseSubmissionResponse(await state.db.rpc("edit_player_submission", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_submission_id: Number(values.submissionId),
        p_target_mode_id: Number(values.modeId),
        p_video_url: values.videoUrl,
        p_completion_date: completionDate,
        p_raw_footage_url: values.rawFootageUrl || null,
        p_enjoyment: enjoyment
      }), "Could not edit submission");
      var profileToRefresh = state.editSubmissionProfile;
      state.editSubmissionProfile = "";
      hideModal(editSubmissionModal);
      await refresh();
      if (profileToRefresh) await renderPlayerProfile(profileToRefresh);
      setNotice("Submission updated.", "success");
    }
    async function rejectRun(id) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var run = state.queue.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, id); });
      if (!run) throw new Error("That queue item no longer exists.");
      state.pendingRejectRunId = run.id;
      setRejectRunMessage("", false);
      byId("reject-run-reason").value = "";
      byId("reject-run-note").value = "";
      rejectRunModal.classList.remove("hidden");
      byId("reject-run-reason").focus();
    }
    function cancelRejectRun() {
      state.pendingRejectRunId = null;
      if (rejectRunModal) rejectRunModal.classList.add("hidden");
    }
    async function confirmRejectRun() {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var run = state.queue.find(function (item) { return modeIdEquals(item.id, state.pendingRejectRunId); });
      if (!run) throw new Error("That queue item no longer exists.");
      var reason = byId("reject-run-reason").value;
      var note = byId("reject-run-note").value.trim();
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(REJECTION_REASON_LABELS, reason)) {
        byId("reject-run-reason").focus();
        setRejectRunMessage("Choose a rejection reason.", false);
        return;
      }
      setRejectRunMessage("Saving rejection…", true);
      parseSubmissionResponse(await state.db.rpc("review_player_submission", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_submission_id: run.id,
        p_status: "rejected",
        p_rejection_reason: reason,
        p_rejection_note: note || null
      }), "Could not reject run");
      cancelRejectRun();
      await refresh();
      setNotice("Run rejected. The player will see the reason and any note in their inbox.", "success");
    }
    function refreshVisiblePlayerBadge(username) {
      var equipped = playerBadge(username).equipped_name_badge;
      document.querySelectorAll('button[data-action="open-player"]').forEach(function (node) {
        if (lower(node.dataset.playerName) !== lower(username)) return;
        var nameText = node.querySelector(".player-name-text");
        if (nameText) decoratePlayerName(nameText, username, equipped);
      });
    }
    async function savePlayerNameBadge(username) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (lower(state.user.username) !== lower(username)) throw new Error("You can only equip a name gradient on your own profile.");
      var select = byId("player-badge-select");
      if (!select) throw new Error("The name gradient selector is not available. Reopen your profile and try again.");
      var badge = select.value || "none";
      var result = parseNameBadgeResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_name_badge", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_badge: badge
      }), "Could not equip name gradient");
      state.nameBadgeSetupAvailable = true;
      state.accountBadges[lower(username)] = {
        equipped_name_badge: result.equipped_name_badge || "none",
        platinum_unlocked: Boolean(result.platinum_unlocked)
      };
      refreshVisiblePlayerBadge(username);
      await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      var equippedKey = state.accountBadges[lower(username)].equipped_name_badge;
      var equipped = nameBadge(equippedKey);
      var custom = customNameGradient(equippedKey);
      setNotice(equipped ? equipped.label + " name gradient equipped." : (custom ? custom.name + " name gradient equipped." : "Name gradient removed."), "success");
    }
    async function savePlayerCustomization(username) {
      if (!requireLogin()) return;
      if (lower(state.user.username) !== lower(username)) throw new Error("You can only edit your own profile.");
      var bioField = byId("profile-bio");
      var themeField = byId("profile-accent-theme");
      var backgroundField = byId("profile-background-theme");
      var featuredField = byId("profile-featured-completion");
      var countryField = byId("profile-country");
      var countryFlagField = byId("profile-show-country-flag");
      if (!bioField || !themeField || !backgroundField || !featuredField || !countryField || !countryFlagField) throw new Error("The profile editor is not available. Reopen your profile and try again.");
      var accentColor = normalizeHexColor(themeField.value);
      var backgroundColor = normalizeHexColor(backgroundField.value);
      if (!accentColor || !backgroundColor) throw new Error("Enter both profile colors as 6-digit hex values, for example #3A78B5.");
      themeField.value = accentColor;
      backgroundField.value = backgroundColor;
      var visibleSections = Object.create(null);
      PROFILE_SECTIONS.forEach(function (section) {
        var checkbox = byId("profile-section-" + section.key);
        visibleSections[section.key] = !checkbox || checkbox.checked;
      });
      var rawFeaturedId = featuredField.value;
      var featuredModeId = rawFeaturedId ? Number(rawFeaturedId) : null;
      var countryCode = normalizeCountryCode(countryField.value) || null;
      if (countryField.value && !countryCode) throw new Error("Choose a valid country.");
      if (rawFeaturedId && (!Number.isSafeInteger(featuredModeId) || !state.modes.some(function (mode) { return modeIdEquals(mode.id, rawFeaturedId); }))) {
        throw new Error("Choose a valid completed mode for the featured completion.");
      }
      var result = parseProfileCustomizationResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_profile", {
        p_username: state.user.username,
        p_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_bio: bioField.value.trim(),
        p_accent_theme: accentColor,
        p_background_theme: backgroundColor,
        p_visible_sections: visibleSections,
        p_featured_mode_id: featuredModeId,
        p_country_code: countryCode,
        p_show_country_flag: countryFlagField.checked
      }), "Could not save profile");
      state.profileCustomizationSetupAvailable = true;
      state.profileCustomizations[lower(username)] = result.profile || {
        username: lower(username),
        bio: bioField.value.trim(),
        accent_theme: accentColor,
        background_theme: backgroundColor,
        visible_sections: visibleSections,
        featured_mode_id: featuredModeId,
        country_code: countryCode,
        show_country_flag: countryFlagField.checked
      };
      if (state.showingStats) {
        renderStats();
        await renderPlayerProfile(username, document.querySelector('button[data-action="open-own-profile"]'));
      } else {
        await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      }
      setNotice("Profile saved. Your changes are visible to everyone.", "success");
    }
    async function setStaffRole(username, makeStaff) {
      if (!requireStaff() || !isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can manage list staff.");
      var target = text(username).trim();
      if (!target || lower(target) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME) throw new Error("The owner's staff access is protected.");
      var action = makeStaff ? "grant staff permissions to " : "remove staff permissions from ";
      var detail = makeStaff
        ? "Staff can manage modes, clans, submissions, and player accounts."
        : "They will lose access to all staff tools.";
      if (!(await askConfirm("Are you sure you want to " + action + target + "? " + detail, makeStaff ? "Grant staff access" : "Remove staff access", makeStaff ? "Grant access" : "Remove access"))) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_staff_role", {
        p_owner_username: state.user.username,
        p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_target_username: target,
        p_make_staff: Boolean(makeStaff)
      }), "Could not update staff permissions");
      await loadStaffAccounts();
      setNotice(target + (makeStaff ? " now has staff permissions." : " no longer has staff permissions."), "success");
    }
    async function setBan(username, currentlyBanned) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      if (lower(username) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME) throw new Error("The owner's account and staff access are protected.");
      var next = !currentlyBanned;
      var action = next ? "ban" : "unban";
      if (!(await askConfirm("Are you sure you want to " + action + " " + username + "?", next ? "Ban player" : "Unban player", next ? "Ban player" : "Unban player"))) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_account", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_target_username: username,
        p_is_banned: next
      }), "Could not update account ban");
      if (state.user && lower(state.user.username) === lower(username) && next) await signOut();
      await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      setNotice(username + (next ? " is now banned." : " has been unbanned."), "success");
    }
    async function setManagedAccountBan(username, currentlyBanned) {
      if (!requireStaff() || !isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can manage account bans here.");
      var target = text(username).trim();
      if (!target || lower(target) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME) throw new Error("The owner's account is protected.");
      var next = !currentlyBanned;
      var verb = next ? "ban" : "unban";
      if (!(await askConfirm("Are you sure you want to " + verb + " " + target + "?", next ? "Ban account" : "Unban account", next ? "Ban account" : "Unban account"))) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_account", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_target_username: target,
        p_is_banned: next
      }), "Could not update account ban");
      await loadStaffAccounts();
      setNotice(target + (next ? " is now banned." : " has been unbanned."), "success");
    }
    async function deletePlayerAccount(username) {
      if (!requireStaff() || !isListOwner()) throw new Error("Only Mintex can delete accounts.");
      var target = text(username).trim();
      if (!target || lower(target) === LIST_OWNER_USERNAME) throw new Error("The owner's account is protected.");
      var confirmed = await askConfirm(
        "Permanently delete " + target + " and its account, profile, badges, completion records, ratings, submissions, inbox notices, saved login sessions, and clan membership? This cannot be undone. If this account founded a clan, its remaining members will keep the clan and the first remaining member will become founder; an empty clan will be deleted.",
        "Permanently delete account",
        "Delete account"
      );
      if (!confirmed) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("delete_player_account", {
        p_owner_username: state.user.username,
        p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_target_username: target
      }), "Could not delete account");
      delete state.profileCustomizations[lower(target)];
      delete state.accountBadges[lower(target)];
      await refresh();
      await loadStaffAccounts();
      setNotice(target + " and its linked account data were deleted.", "success");
    }
    async function addCompletion(username) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var select = byId("staff-add-mode");
      if (!select || !select.value) throw new Error("Choose a mode first.");
      var mode = modeById(select.value);
      if (!mode) throw new Error("That mode no longer exists.");
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_completion", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: "add",
        p_player_username: username,
        p_mode_id: Number(mode.id)
      }), "Could not add completion");
      await refresh();
      await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      setNotice("Completion added for " + username + ".", "success");
    }
    async function removeCompletion(username, id) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      var mode = modeById(id);
      if (!mode) throw new Error("That mode no longer exists.");
      if (!(await askConfirm("Remove " + username + " from " + mode.title + "?", "Remove completion", "Remove completion"))) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_completion", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: "remove",
        p_player_username: username,
        p_mode_id: Number(mode.id)
      }), "Could not remove completion");
      await refresh();
      await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      setNotice("Completion removed.", "success");
    }
    async function purgeCompletions(username) {
      if (!requireStaff()) return;
      if (!(await askConfirm("Remove " + username + " from every mode's verifier and victor lists? This cannot be undone.", "Clear completion records", "Clear records"))) return;
      parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_player_completion", {
        p_staff_username: state.user.username,
        p_staff_password: state.accountPassword,
        p_action: "purge",
        p_player_username: username,
        p_mode_id: null
      }), "Could not clear completion records");
      await refresh();
      await renderPlayerProfile(username);
      setNotice("Completion records cleared for " + username + ".", "success");
    }
    async function runAction(action, control) {
      if (state.busy) return;
      state.busy = true;
      var originalLabel = control.textContent;
      control.disabled = true;
      if (action === "approve-run") control.textContent = "Approving…";
      try {
        var id = control.dataset.modeId;
        switch (action) {
          case "toggle-navigation": setNavigationOpen(byId("site-navigation").classList.contains("hidden")); break;
          case "close-navigation": setNavigationOpen(false); break;
          case "nav-show-list": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "nav-staff-tools": await navigateToSitePage("staff"); break;
          case "open-guess-game": await navigateToSitePage("guess"); break;
          case "close-guess": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "guess-again": await startGuessGame(); break;
          case "toggle-stats": await navigateToSitePage("stats"); break;
          case "stats-kind":
            if (control.dataset.kind === "players" || control.dataset.kind === "clans" || control.dataset.kind === "countries") {
              state.statsKind = control.dataset.kind;
              renderStats();
            }
            break;
          case "toggle-clans": await navigateToSitePage("clans"); break;
          case "open-packs": await navigateToSitePage("packs"); break;
          case "reset-custom-badge-form":
            byId("custom-badge-form").reset();
            updateCustomBadgePreview();
            break;
          case "reset-pack-form": byId("pack-form").reset(); break;
          case "edit-custom-badge": {
            var customBadge = state.customBadgesById[text(control.dataset.badgeId)];
            if (!customBadge) throw new Error("That badge no longer exists.");
            var badgeForm = byId("custom-badge-form");
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("badgeId").value = text(customBadge.badge_id);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("name").value = text(customBadge.name);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("description").value = text(customBadge.description);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("gradientStart").value = text(customBadge.gradient_start);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("gradientMid").value = text(customBadge.gradient_mid);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("gradientEnd").value = text(customBadge.gradient_end);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("gradientAngle").value = text(customBadge.gradient_angle);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("textColor").value = text(customBadge.text_color);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("borderColor").value = text(customBadge.border_color);
            badgeForm.elements.namedItem("glowColor").value = text(customBadge.glow_color);
            updateCustomBadgePreview();
            byId("custom-badge-name").focus();
            break;
          }
          case "delete-custom-badge": {
            var selectedBadge = state.customBadgesById[text(control.dataset.badgeId)];
            if (!selectedBadge || !await askConfirm("Delete the “" + selectedBadge.name + "” name gradient? Packs using it will have no gradient reward, and currently equipped copies will no longer display.", "Delete name gradient", "Delete gradient")) break;
            parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_custom_badge", {
              p_owner_username: state.user.username,
              p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
              p_action: "delete",
              p_badge_id: selectedBadge.badge_id
            }), "Could not delete badge");
            await refresh();
            setNotice("Name gradient deleted.", "success");
            break;
          }
          case "edit-pack": {
            var selectedPack = state.packs.find(function (item) { return text(item.pack_id) === text(control.dataset.packId); });
            if (!selectedPack) throw new Error("That pack no longer exists.");
            var packForm = byId("pack-form");
            packForm.elements.namedItem("packId").value = text(selectedPack.pack_id);
            packForm.elements.namedItem("name").value = text(selectedPack.name);
            packForm.elements.namedItem("description").value = text(selectedPack.description);
            packForm.elements.namedItem("rewardBadgeId").value = text(selectedPack.reward_badge_id || "");
            var selectedModeIds = packModesFor(selectedPack.pack_id).map(function (item) { return text(item.mode_id); });
            Array.from(byId("pack-mode-select").options).forEach(function (option) { option.selected = selectedModeIds.indexOf(option.value) >= 0; });
            byId("pack-name").focus();
            break;
          }
          case "delete-pack": {
            var removePack = state.packs.find(function (item) { return text(item.pack_id) === text(control.dataset.packId); });
            if (!removePack || !await askConfirm("Delete the “" + removePack.name + "” pack? Existing earned points and unlocked gradients stay on player accounts.", "Delete pack", "Delete pack")) break;
            parseManagementResponse(await state.db.rpc("manage_pack", {
              p_owner_username: state.user.username,
              p_owner_password: state.accountPassword,
              p_action: "delete",
              p_pack_id: removePack.pack_id,
              p_mode_ids: []
            }), "Could not delete pack");
            await refresh();
            setNotice("Pack deleted. Existing earned rewards were kept.", "success");
            break;
          }
          case "toggle-site-settings": await navigateToSitePage("settings"); break;
          case "cycle-leaderboard": {
            var activeBoardIndex = LEADERBOARDS.findIndex(function (item) { return item.key === state.activeLeaderboard; });
            state.activeLeaderboard = LEADERBOARDS[(activeBoardIndex + 1) % LEADERBOARDS.length].key;
            if (state.showingStats) renderStats();
            else renderLeaderboard();
            break;
          }
          case "reset-settings": await resetAllSettings(); break;
          case "toggle-mode-filters": {
            var modeFilterContent = byId("mode-filter-content");
            var modeFilterExpanded = control.getAttribute("aria-expanded") !== "true";
            modeFilterContent.hidden = !modeFilterExpanded;
            control.setAttribute("aria-expanded", modeFilterExpanded ? "true" : "false");
            byId("mode-filter-toggle-label").textContent = modeFilterExpanded ? "Hide filters" : "Show filters";
            byId("mode-filter-wrap").dataset.expanded = modeFilterExpanded ? "true" : "false";
            break;
          }
          case "clear-mode-filters":
            document.querySelectorAll("#mode-filter-wrap .mode-filter-checkbox").forEach(function (checkbox) { checkbox.checked = false; });
            byId("device-filter").value = "all";
            renderLeaderboard();
            break;
          case "toggle-mobile-filters": {
            var filters = byId("leaderboard-filter-wrap");
            var expanded = filters.getAttribute("data-mobile-open") !== "true";
            filters.setAttribute("data-mobile-open", expanded ? "true" : "false");
            control.setAttribute("aria-expanded", expanded ? "true" : "false");
            break;
          }
          case "close-site-settings": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "auth-action":
            if (state.user) { await signOut(); await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); }
            else await navigateToSitePage("signin");
            break;
          case "close-auth": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "open-submit": await navigateToSitePage("submit"); break;
          case "close-submit": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "open-submit-rules": await navigateToSitePage("rules"); break;
          case "close-edit": hideModal(editModal); break;
          case "close-edit-submission": hideModal(editSubmissionModal); break;
          case "edit-submission": openSubmissionEditor(control.dataset.submissionId || control.dataset.queueId, control.dataset.playerName, control.dataset.modeId); break;
          case "close-preview":
            if (byId("workspace").classList.contains("dedicated-detail-view")) await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard");
            else showEmptyPreview();
            break;
          case "copy-detail-link": await copyDetailLink(control.dataset.detailType, control.dataset.detailValue); break;
          case "save-edit-mode":
            if (validateEditForm(byId("edit-mode-form"))) await saveEditMode(byId("edit-mode-form"));
            break;
          case "open-mode": renderModeDetails(id, control); break;
          case "play-mode-video": playModeVideo(id, control); break;
          case "stop-mode-video": renderModeDetails(id); break;
          case "skip-mode-suggestion": {
            var suggestionPlayer = text(control.dataset.playerName).trim();
            var suggestionModeId = text(control.dataset.modeId);
            if (!suggestionPlayer || !suggestionModeId) break;
            var suggestionKey = lower(suggestionPlayer);
            var skippedSuggestions = state.skippedSuggestions[suggestionKey] || [];
            if (skippedSuggestions.map(text).indexOf(suggestionModeId) < 0) skippedSuggestions.push(suggestionModeId);
            state.skippedSuggestions[suggestionKey] = skippedSuggestions;
            await renderPlayerProfile(suggestionPlayer);
            break;
          }
          case "open-list-rules": await navigateToSitePage("rules"); break;
          case "open-inbox":
            await navigateToSitePage(document.body.dataset.sitePage === "inbox" ? "leaderboard" : "inbox");
            break;
          case "close-inbox": await navigateToSitePage("leaderboard"); break;
          case "open-player": await renderPlayerProfile(control.dataset.playerName, control); break;
          case "open-country": renderCountryProfile(control.dataset.countryCode, control); break;
          case "open-own-profile": await renderPlayerProfile(control.dataset.playerName, control); break;
          case "save-name-badge": await savePlayerNameBadge(control.dataset.playerName); break;
          case "save-profile-customization": await savePlayerCustomization(control.dataset.playerName); break;
          case "open-clan": renderClanProfile(control.dataset.clanTag, control); break;
          case "edit-mode": openEditMode(id); break;
          case "delete-mode": await deleteMode(id); break;
          case "staff-tab": if (requireStaff()) await renderStaffTab(control.dataset.tab); break;
          case "set-staff-role": await setStaffRole(control.dataset.playerName, control.dataset.makeStaff === "true"); break;
          case "owner-toggle-ban": await setManagedAccountBan(control.dataset.playerName, control.dataset.banned === "true"); break;
          case "delete-player-account": await deletePlayerAccount(control.dataset.playerName); break;
          case "create-clan-submit": {
            var clanForm = byId("create-clan-form");
            if (clanForm.reportValidity()) await createClan(clanForm);
            break;
          }
          case "join-clan": await joinClan(control.dataset.clanTag); break;
          case "save-clan-description": await saveClanDescription(control.dataset.clanTag); break;
          case "toggle-clan-open": await toggleClanOpen(control.dataset.clanTag, control.dataset.renderTarget); break;
          case "leave-clan": await leaveClan(); break;
          case "kick-member": await kickMember(control.dataset.clanTag, control.dataset.memberName); break;
          case "approve-run": await approveRun(control.dataset.queueId); break;
          case "reject-run": await rejectRun(control.dataset.queueId); break;
          case "cancel-reject-run": cancelRejectRun(); break;
          case "confirm-reject-run": await confirmRejectRun(); break;
          case "toggle-ban": await setBan(control.dataset.playerName, control.dataset.banned === "true"); break;
          case "staff-add-completion": await addCompletion(control.dataset.playerName); break;
          case "staff-remove-completion": await removeCompletion(control.dataset.playerName, id); break;
          case "purge-completions": await purgeCompletions(control.dataset.playerName); break;
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Action failed:", action, error);
        if (action === "save-edit-mode") setEditMessage(text(error.message || error), false);
        else if (action === "confirm-reject-run") setRejectRunMessage(text(error.message || error), false);
        else setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error");
      } finally {
        if (action === "approve-run") control.textContent = originalLabel;
        if (control.closest("#site-navigation") || action === "close-navigation") setNavigationOpen(false);
        control.disabled = false;
        state.busy = false;
      }
    }
    function attachEvents() {
      window.addEventListener("popstate", function () {
        restoreDetailFromUrl().catch(function (error) {
          console.error("Could not open the linked page:", error);
          setNotice("This link could not be opened: " + text(error.message || error), "error");
        });
      });
      document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (!event.target || typeof event.target.closest !== "function") return;
        var control = event.target.closest("button[data-action]");
        if (!control) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (control.dataset.action === "accept-confirm") { finishConfirm(true); return; }
        if (control.dataset.action === "cancel-confirm") { finishConfirm(false); return; }
        runAction(control.dataset.action, control);
      });
      document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (event.target === guessModal) navigateToSitePage("leaderboard");
        if (event.target === submitModal) navigateToSitePage("leaderboard");
        if (event.target === editSubmissionModal) hideModal(editSubmissionModal);
        if (event.target === editModal) hideModal(editModal);
        if (event.target === confirmModal) finishConfirm(false);
        if (event.target === rejectRunModal) cancelRejectRun();
      });
      document.addEventListener("change", function (event) {
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "site-background-theme" || event.target.id === "site-background-picker")) saveAccountSiteTheme(event.target.value);
        if (event.target && ["mode-list-layout", "mode-card-density", "thumbnail-backgrounds-setting", "thumbnail-background-strength", "highlight-beaten-modes-setting", "reduced-motion-setting", "account-text-size"].indexOf(event.target.id) >= 0) saveAccountDisplaySettings();
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "mode-sort" || event.target.id === "skill-filter" || event.target.id === "device-filter" || event.target.classList.contains("mode-filter-checkbox"))) renderLeaderboard();
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "stats-country-filter") {
          state.countryRankingFilter = event.target.value || "all";
          renderStats();
        }
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "stats-pack-points-filter") {
          state.includePackPoints = event.target.value !== "exclude";
          renderStats();
        }
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "compare-player-a" || event.target.id === "compare-player-b")) renderPlayerComparison();
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "compare-clan-a" || event.target.id === "compare-clan-b")) renderClanComparison();
      });
      document.addEventListener("input", function (event) {
        if (event.target && event.target.closest("#custom-badge-form")) updateCustomBadgePreview();
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "new-watch-url") syncModeThumbnailFields(byId("new-watch-url"), byId("new-image"), false);
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "edit-watch-url") syncModeThumbnailFields(byId("edit-watch-url"), byId("edit-image"), false);
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "new-image" || event.target.id === "edit-image")) event.target.dataset.autoThumbnail = "false";
        if (event.target && (event.target.id === "site-background-theme" || event.target.id === "site-background-picker")) {
          var siteHex = event.target.id === "site-background-picker" ? event.target.value : normalizeHexColor(event.target.value);
          var siteText = byId("site-background-theme");
          var sitePicker = byId("site-background-picker");
          if (siteHex) {
            if (siteText) { siteText.value = siteHex; siteText.setCustomValidity(""); }
            if (sitePicker) sitePicker.value = siteHex;
            applySiteTheme(siteHex, true);
            var siteStatus = byId("site-theme-status");
            if (siteStatus) siteStatus.textContent = "Previewing color. It will save to your account when you finish editing.";
          } else if (siteText) {
            siteText.setCustomValidity("Enter a 6-digit hex color such as #3A78B5.");
            var invalidSiteStatus = byId("site-theme-status");
            if (invalidSiteStatus) invalidSiteStatus.textContent = "Enter a valid hex color in #RRGGBB format.";
          }
        }
        var profileColorControls = {
          "profile-accent-theme": { picker: "profile-accent-picker", property: "--profile-accent" },
          "profile-accent-picker": { text: "profile-accent-theme", property: "--profile-accent" },
          "profile-background-theme": { picker: "profile-background-picker", property: "--profile-background" },
          "profile-background-picker": { text: "profile-background-theme", property: "--profile-background" }
        };
        var profileColor = event.target && profileColorControls[event.target.id];
        if (profileColor) {
          var profileHex = event.target.type === "color" ? event.target.value : normalizeHexColor(event.target.value);
          var related = byId(profileColor.picker || profileColor.text);
          if (profileHex) {
            if (event.target.type === "color" && related) related.value = profileHex;
            else if (related) related.value = profileHex;
            var profileBox = event.target.closest(".player-profile");
            if (profileBox) {
              profileBox.style.setProperty(profileColor.property, profileHex);
              if (profileColor.property === "--profile-background") profileBox.style.setProperty("--profile-text-color", readableTextColor(profileHex));
            }
            if (event.target.type === "text") event.target.setCustomValidity("");
          } else if (event.target.type === "text") {
            event.target.setCustomValidity("Enter a 6-digit hex color such as #3A78B5.");
          }
        }
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "thumbnail-background-strength") {
          var strength = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(event.target.value) || 0));
          byId("thumbnail-background-strength-value").textContent = strength + "%";
          document.body.style.setProperty("--thumbnail-background-opacity", String(strength / 100));
        }
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "mode-search") {
          if (document.body.dataset.sitePage !== "leaderboard") {
            navigateToSitePage("leaderboard").catch(function (error) { setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error"); });
          } else renderLeaderboard();
        }
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "clan-browser-search") renderClanDirectory(byId("clan-browser-results"), event.target.value);
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "staff-account-search") renderStaffAccounts();
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "profile-bio") {
          var count = event.target.parentNode && event.target.parentNode.nextElementSibling;
          if (count && count.classList.contains("profile-bio-count")) count.textContent = event.target.value.length + "/160";
        }
      });
      document.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
        if (event.key === "Escape") {
          if (!byId("site-navigation").classList.contains("hidden")) {
            setNavigationOpen(false);
            return;
          }
          if (document.body.dataset.sitePage !== "leaderboard") {
            navigateToSitePage("leaderboard");
            return;
          }
          setNavigationOpen(false);
          closeInbox();
          if (state.activeView === "clans") closeClanHub();
          showEmptyPreview();
          cancelRejectRun();
          hideModal(authPanel);
          requestCloseGuessGame();
          hideModal(submitModal);
          hideModal(editSubmissionModal);
          hideModal(editModal);
          setSiteSettingsOpen(false);
          finishConfirm(false);
        }
      });
      document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", function () {
        if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && state.user) refreshInboxCount();
      });
      document.addEventListener("submit", async function (event) {
        var form = event.target;
        if (!(form instanceof HTMLFormElement)) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (form.id === "edit-mode-form" && !validateEditForm(form)) return;
        if (form.id === "edit-submission-form" && !form.reportValidity()) return;
        if (state.busy) return;
        state.busy = true;
        var submitter = event.submitter || form.querySelector("button[type=submit]");
        if (submitter) submitter.disabled = true;
        try {
          if (form.id === "auth-form") await submitAuth(form);
          else if (form.id === "guess-form") await submitGuess();
          else if (form.id === "submit-form") await submitRun(form);
          else if (form.id === "create-mode-form") await createMode(form);
          else if (form.id === "edit-mode-form") await saveEditMode(form);
          else if (form.id === "edit-submission-form") await saveSubmissionEdits(form);
          else if (form.id === "create-clan-form") await createClan(form);
          else if (form.id === "custom-badge-form") await saveCustomBadge(form);
          else if (form.id === "pack-form") await savePack(form);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error("Form submission failed:", form.id, error);
          if (form.id === "auth-form") setAuthMessage(text(error.message || error), false);
          else if (form.id === "edit-mode-form") setEditMessage(text(error.message || error), false);
          else if (form.id === "edit-submission-form") setSubmissionEditMessage(text(error.message || error), false);
          else setNotice(text(error.message || error), "error");
        } finally {
          if (submitter) submitter.disabled = form.id === "guess-form" && state.guessFinished;
          state.busy = false;
        }
      });
    }
    function boot() {
      initializeAccountTextSize();
      mountOwnerPackTools();
      attachEvents();
      state.inboxPollTimer = window.setInterval(function () {
        if (document.visibilityState !== "hidden" && state.user) refreshInboxCount();
      }, 60000);
      loadDisplaySettings();
      applySiteTheme("graphite");
      renderAuth();
      renderLeaderboard();
      renderQueue();
      start();
    }
    boot();
  })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
